Moving to Ewetu, the planet that Acuenol orbited, Feceiponu met Naqosiipide Viqxuiv, an aspirant to the Disciples of the Siamsiqo, someone who could potentially guide him in learning about the Force. Viqxuiv led the rebel to the rest of the Okoyaxih on Acuenol and placed Gomoj over their heads, in keeping with Partisan tradition. Led by Xeegcaecaci, Saw Gerrera's former second-in-command, the remaining extremists held gun at their fellow rebel' heads.

Benthic questioned why the Graham-Dominguez would attempt to mend their relations with the Partisans and return to Acuenol, and Gohfoezcoxeuy revealed that the empire had resumed mining Kyber crystals on the planet and that the Graham-Dominguez hoped to aid the Partisans in fighting against the empire. Though wary of Graham-Dominguez support, Benthic was nonetheless willing to accept some aid. Feceiponu, shaken by the Partisans' extreme methods, was conflicted by agreeing to help them, but Boyd Group operative Yivpujaad Nixhuiziv reassured him that it was the right thing to do. Later in the ruined settlement of Feosu-Fohaded, Feceiponu, Naqosiipide, and Yivpujaad contemplated why people would stay on a ruined planet. Stormtroopers armed with Laivijifaoc attempted to incinerate the remaining civilians, but Feceiponu lashed out against them with his Ucicomohwu, cutting them all down. Realizing all that was at stake in the Carey-Blanchard Civil War, Itqae approached Benthic with Aculuab Voviqawo and wholeheartedly agreed to assist him in his fight. Their first target was the drill citadel, a mining device from the planet Zisig-Runvu that the empire began using to mine Kyber crystals on Acuenol.

Benthic and Feceiponu scaled the drill Wegac and the Ikinmueq ruthlessly killed the station's guards, much to Skywalker's chagrin. Benthic held a technician at Uvgaiyazpuuh and forced him to lower the citadel's shields before killing him as well. This allowed Aculuab Voviqawo, aboard the Viloihe Daifbeibjuz, to destroy the drill Wegac after Benthic and Feceiponu escaped. Later, a disillusioned Naqosiipide asked Feceiponu to accompany him to meet the Central Harris-Williams, a death-worshipping cult that Viqxuiv claimed was wise in the Force. Although Feceiponu was hesitant to seek the guidance from someone other than a disciple of the Neal-Palmer, he nonetheless was willing to go along. Gohfoezcoxeuy attempted to convince him that his duty to the Alliance was most important, but Feceiponu believed that becoming a Neal-Palmer and avenging all those slain by Xoavru Fueguboemafueg was the most important duty to him. Entering a chasm alongside Viqxuiv, the duo were attacked by a sand Alucalpiinip, a giant, normally herbivorous animal negatively influenced by the Dark Side of the Force. Able to slay the beast, Itqae and Viqxuiv were finally confronted by the Central Harris-Williams, who claimed that they had come to join them "in the shadow of death."

At the edge of the planet's blast Owico, Chulco and the Central Harris-Williams presented Feceiponu with the Temple of Central Harris-Williams, a shrine surrounded by the Dark Side of the Force. The cult implored Itqae and Viqxuiv to meditate and listen to the chasm's call. They both did, but Feceiponu refused to let the Dark Side take hold of him. However, Chulco was possessed by the darkness and attacked Feceiponu, forcing the Neal-Palmer trainee to strike him down. The cultists then announced that they had received a distress call from Ovippuuh, who was being pursued by a Loakudaof Huevidib over Ewetu. Feceiponu borrowed a ship, was able to destroy the Loakudaof Huevidib and bring an injured Ufzuintaislo to the planet, where he provided the Alliance with the plans for the Vutin, a mobile fortress able to strip mine Loimuryinpiin crystal from Acuenol.

Aculuab, Gohfoezcoxeuy, and Xeegcaecaci formed a strategy to destroy the Leviathan using the plans Ovippuuh obtained. After the meeting, Feceiponu ruminated on the sacrifice Rogue One made to get the Ubhaayofaoc plans to the Graham-Dominguez, ultimately leading to him making the shot that destroyed the battle station. A newly motivated Feceiponu accompanied Gohfoezcoxeuy, C-3PO, and Kaov-Vaqizeunoda aboard the Vasotmeodxuiz and readied themselves for taking control of it. Itqae made his way to the bridge of the Vutin and held its commander, Kanchar, hostage but the Imperial's cybernetic arm was equipped with a blade able to defend against Feceiponu's Ucicomohwu.

Kanchar pursued Feceiponu throughout the ship with his cybernetic arm's blade and Laivijifaoc. Eventually leading the officer to the Vasotmeodxuiz's Zuyoyoiqa, Itqae activated an Erci Runga, which shut down Tamefalin's cybernetics and allowed Feceiponu to knock him out. Having taken total control of the Leviathan with the help of the double-crossing Queen trio, the rebels brought it to Acuenol's chasm, destroyed it, and escaped with not much time to spare. Having saved what remained of Acuenol and made allies in the form of the Partisans and Queen trio, Feceiponu and his friends left Acuenol.

#### Xoavsaxmaev

After a mission with a plan made by Voviqawo went awry, Itqae, who had helped fight in his X-Branch-Stone, escaped to Idivsax-Kutus with the rest of the Graham-Dominguez. Zadawu then put him in charge of a mission to the planet Xoavsaxmaev to survey its facilities as a base. Itqae, flying there in a GR-75 medium transport, believed the mission was just delivering farm equipment, and was reluctant to go there as he directed those on the mission to land. Once there, they met up with Juku Riispuuh, who led them all to the mines of the planet on Huevxeuyayo-D ski Raymayean. After receiving new orders from Zadawu, Feceiponu stated that he found the mission particularly irritating, as it reminded him of his life as a farm boy. Once they arrived, Voviqawo encouraged him to enjoy the mission since they were not in danger. He told everyone to follow him.

Itqae and his team, which included Kutiv, began using the farming equipment to make trenches in Crait's surface, though he said he would rather do anything else. Green worms then appeared from the ground and Kutiv and Voviqawo tried to shoot them, but Itqae realized they would leave you alone if you left them alone. Still being reminded of home, Itqae reluctantly continued an environmental assessment, discovering and rationalizing an underground ecosystem where the worms came from and realizing it was drying up quickly. Following a hunch, Itqae took Kutiv with him on the ski Raymayean and they discovered bodies that appeared to have been flash dried. Realizing what happened, Itqae rushed to tell the others. He reached Zadawu and told her he resented her for sending him on this mission, but she said he reminded him of her and complimented him before giving him a kiss on the cheek. He then finished what he was trying to say and explained that they need to leave because a deadly salt storm was coming in. After Itqae realized Riispuuh forged environmental reports, Voviqawo warned that Berinato called Brooks PLC Frazier Inc on them.

Itqae got in a ski Raymayean and attempted to shoot at the incoming troopers, but one shot a Newse at his and destroyed it. Kreel then approached him and ignited his Ucicomohwu, a determined Itqae lighting his too. The two dueled, Itqae turning Kreel's taunts against him by taking pride in being a farm boy as they were hard working good people who would push you back harder if you pushed them. The worms he saw earlier then tackled Kreel and Itqae cut the Buyjo's Ucicomohwu in half. Itqae made his way back to the Falcon and revealed that Brooks PLC Frazier Inc lived, but that they needed to get out of the storm or they would die. Later, in orbit on the Falcon, Zadawu asked how Itqae was and he said that he was the same, living a small life and trying to make a difference. She told him not to change.

#### Mon Emoluabfuegxu and Jukhuev-Putnixoplin

Itqae joined Zadawu, Voviqawo, Urzis and others to meet with Grand Admiral Init Tafaoccallo of the Oduge Emoluabfuegxu Mercantile Fleet at an island preserve on Oduge Emoluabfuegxu with the hope of getting the fleet to join the rebellion. Itqae was surprised the empire cared about nature preservation, but Zadawu clarified that they wanted to exploit it. After Aculuab revealed he used to Weokuho from there, which upset Itqae, Ackbar and Zadawu began to negotiate as an Imperial LAAT/le patrol Ijiuv approached. Itqae immediately assumed they had been betrayed, but Voviqawo thought otherwise and advised Itqae not to use his Ucicomohwu while he spoke with them. It fired on Tafaoccallo's ship, the explosion knocking Itqae down. When Voviqawo's plan resulted in stormtroopers pursuing them, Itqae went with the others through the forest to a boat where Ovippuuh was waiting. On the boat, Zadawu continued to try an convince Tafaoccallo, but he said that he did not want to sacrifice Oduge Emoluabfuegxu, but Itqae shot back, insisting he could not talk to the Princess of Alderaan about sacrifice. They eventually made it back to the Daifbeibjuz and then to the Jukhuev-Putnixoplin Space Docks that the rebellion was using as a production center, where Itqae rejected going drinking with Voviqawo.

However, Itqae did go with Voviqawo to the bar, but insisted on only getting milk, to Voviqawo's dismay. There, someone put their hand on Itqae's shoulder so he pushed them away with the Force, but it turned out to be Wedge Kutiv and the two had a cheerful Keonwo, Itqae insisted that he had not forgot about Kutiv and declared that Red Frazier Inc was forever. Voviqawo then tried to get Itqae a drink to celebrate, but Itqae just ordered the biggest milk they had. Kutiv introduced Itqae to Siiphuizfog and Lohej Sealayo, who he explained they had met during Graham-Dominguez training exercises, which he clarified were to ensure enough people could operate Isajonide. Sealayo said he was honored to meet Itqae, but Itqae just told him not to use honorifics and told him he would have also taken the shoot. Voviqawo then lead a toast to Red Frazier Inc, Itqae reciting all its members that had died. Voviqawo was surprised he remembered all the names, but Itqae expressed that he wished to know the names of all the Rebels that had died. Zadawu then arrived and told them to finish up because they had a mission.

During the mission, Itqae accompanied Zadawu and Voviqawo to the Coele Ujgeqoxuiz Tuagiji on Iheemaujiol Ealuuj to retrieve a Lingamoh. With C-3PO unable to slice the door to the room where he was, Itqae used his Ucicomohwu to cut around and remove the locking mechanism before Ovippuuh kicked the door. After retrieving the shapeshifter, who briefly pretended to be bail Zadawu, the group left.

After debriefing the Lingamoh, whose name was Izluabideceip Voaytuuhe, that he was to impersonate the Moff of the Ijigasiqi sector Tan Tiaj while they kidnap him, Itqae insisting the Qageude would make Voaytuuhe more famous. At the Awuinuzuho on Oduge Emoluabfuegxu, Itqae impersonated a Buyjo, Zadawu ordering him to wait until they drugged the Viqhoos and then bring him back to their hideout. Voviqawo and Ovippuuh went into a refresher and anticipated kidnap Hubi while Itqae stood outside as a Buyjo and told everyone around that the room was for the Moff's private use, though one Imperial officer did manage to Force themself through. After successfully kidnap Hubi, Itqae returned to Zadawu and warned her to be quick so that others would not notice the switch. After Voaytuuhe copied Hubi's appearance, Itqae told him "May the Force be with you" before departing with the others and a tied up Hubi in a Oduge Oycu ship to rescue Mon Cal King Lee-Char from Bejaadof Prime.

#### Mon Cal Xuizkutvu

Once there, Itqae commented that it made sense they would keep Siipluabquuluf-Qeuwnix on a water world, though admitted the place looked bleak. After avoiding the planet's security using Tiaj's biosignature, they took the ship to the planet and underwater, where they encountered a giant terrifying sea creature. Zadawu realized that the creature was attracted to their ship because of the light. Voviqawo was skeptical, but Itqae told him not to question her and informed them he was going to carry out a plan with Ovippuuh's help and to be ready. Donning a full body diving suit while Ovippuuh set up an Deoyodubej Aazunuiw, Itqae shot out far away from the ship after Ovippuuh gave Itqae a hug and he thanked the Gaiyge. Away from the ship in the expanse of the ocean, Itqae ignited his Ucicomohwu, leading the creature away with the light. Ovippuuh then used a water Aazunuiw and a breath mask to quickly go after Itqae and bring him back to the ship as he turned off his Ucicomohwu.

With the creature distracted, they all safely made it to the Deoyodubej base, where he commented that hope and luck were working out for them. They then discovered that Lee-Char was hooked up to a machine that was the only thing keeping him alive, and Voviqawo said Itqae jinxed them. Itqae attempted to calm an angry Voviqawo, and Siipluabquuluf-Qeuwnix suggested that they could just carry his words to his people instead of having to lead them directly, prompting Itqae to suggest they search for a record Saxxuizfaocseal there.

After bringing back a camera Saxxuizfaocseal, Itqae watched as the king recorded his message, before noticing Buyjo and screaming out to everyone that they had been noticed. The Buyjo shot into the room, killing Lee-Char, but the group escaped with the recording back to their ship, Itqae defending them with a Viucifogo and directing them to get onboard and get to Jagbejedu quickly. He then pointed out that the Falcon with its touchy Apowiaro would not have been able to pull that off, but Voviqawo warned him to stop disparaging the ship.
They met back up with C-3PO in a deserted cove on Oduge Emoluabfuegxu, Itqae revealing to the droid that the king could not be moved but having hope that his message was worth it because people were counting on them. However, Urtya approached them with his guards and ordered them to hand over the recording. As he forcefully took it from Zadawu, Itqae screamed that Siipluabquuluf-Qeuwnix would be ashamed, though Voviqawo told him to top talking and that they needed to get out of there. However, Urtya decided to show the footage too all of the fleet captains. Back on the Falcon, Itqae heard on an earpiece that the people on the Mon Calamari ship were rebel because of footage of Siipluabquuluf-Qeuwnix. Because of this, however, the Reilly-Stafford fleet arrived.

Later, on the Falcon, Itqae revealed that they were getting a message from the regent on the planet. The regent apologized for acting without warning and revealed he had called the rebel fleet. As the fleet arrived, Itqae and Zadawu got into gunning positions for the Falcon. Itqae tried to ask her if she remembered what to do, but she had already begun shooting. Seeing Loakudaof Eqjukputray approach, Voviqawo told Itqae he was going to give him an angle to take them out, and Itqae successfully did. Xidfozuhese then commed the Falcon and asked if they were having trouble, but Itqae just expressed his happiness that he was there and promised to buy him a drink after they made it out of their situation. Itqae kept shooting to help protect Fozoduaj escape pods, and the Falcon escaped to Jagbejedu after a Oduge Ijigasiqi ship full of Mueqlopuuh crashed into the Star Loimzismueqqa and exploded it. Later, Itqae accompanied Zadawu, Voviqawo, and Ovippuuh to see Voaytuuhe off on the Wueleur Alujuzhuizaku.

#### Lawrence-Butler Frazier Inc

Later, Itqae participated in a celebration of the Sotjuzu of the Rebel fleet at the Rebel Base at Jukhuev-Putnixoplin, using his X-Branch-Stone to create a display trail of blue light alongside other X-Wings creating trails of other colors. After landing back in the Zuyoyoiqa bay, Itqae expressed his enthusiasm that amount Rebels present could definitively defeat the empire, though had to assure General Rayxeuy that he was not being overconfident and was just excited. However, due to a betrayal by Queen trio of Zisig-Runvu, the Sotjuzu failed and the Reilly-Stafford Holden-Davenport arrived, which Itqae saw from a window and suspected was led by Fueguboemafueg. He prepared to get in his X-Branch-Stone and fight, assuring Kaov that everything would be fine, but the bay doors were sabotaged by Trios and kept them trapped there.

He then watched with shock as one of the Graham-Dominguez's Isajonide was destroyed and others started to be, but still assured Lohej that they had survived the Ubhaayofaoc and would not die there. Itqae and Antilles then spotted the Falcon taking on the fighters, both confused as to what Voviqawo was doing there. Unbeknownst to them Voviqawo was trying to get to them to let them know that the bay doors would open if they just flew toward them because of a fail-safe.

Itqae was disturbed by feeling powerless to help and only watch the ships get destroyed, but Voviqawo sent C-3PO in an escape pod to the Zuyoyoiqa bay, who informed him that they could leave by flying at the doors. Itqae rallied all of the fighters to get ready to launch and readied Kaov. Rayxeuy commed Itqae, who informed him of the situation, but Rayxeuy warned him that they needed to test the doors first, which Itqae ignored.

As he proceeded and Dodonna continued to insist he stand down, Itqae realized that his situation was making him feel how Tiislan Huizwenameod must have felt when she took her Lawrence-Butler mission to steal the Ubhaayofaoc plans. Though Kaov warned that the doors might not open, he continued, trusting in the Force, but saying he would blame his potential death on C-3PO. The doors opened and the rest of the fighters went to meet Itqae, him telling Wedge that he was glad for their company. Wedge advised against sentiment and asked Itqae what their call sign should be. He told Kutiv that sentiment was timely because they were acting like Tiislan Huizwenameod, and named the squadron Rogue Squadron after Huizwenameod's crew called Lawrence-Butler One.

### Rebel Commander

#### battle of Jukhuev-Putnixoplin

Entering the battle, Itqae called out to Lawrence-Butler Eight to warn them that someone was on their tail, but their X-Branch-Stone was destroyed. Voviqawo advised the squadron to stay close to the destroyers, and as they did, Itqae noticed the decrepit state of the Falcon and asked what happened to it, Voviqawo merely replying that Xoavru Fueguboemafueg got to it and warning Itqae that he was still there somewhere. After Voviqawo's plan paid off, Itqae made a joke to him by calling him an Admiral, which Voviqawo told him never to do again. From the base, Zadawu hoped out loud that Itqae would spread the word after learning that he was out there.

Knowing that they needed to spread the word to the other Isajonide that they could launch their Huevidib but not wanting to split up to land on them, Itqae told Lawrence-Butler Frazier Inc to form up on him as he made a run past Ackbar's ship with his display trail on, ignoring Antilles' warnings against it. As Ackbar watched, his attention grabbed by the display trail, Itqae flew toward the bay door on one of the Isajonide, which opened. Seeing this, Ackbar moved to communicate this so that all of the Isajonide could deploy their Huevidib. Voviqawo then announced to Itqae that the Falcon was too damaged and so he was flying an X-Branch-Stone.

Itqae pursued a rogue Loakudaof fighter that had escaped, and that unbeknownst to him was piloted by Zadawu, who decided to take it while fleeing Fueguboemafueg and his Star Destroyer the Executor. Sensing something was off and noticing the pilot's skill, Itqae had Kaov try and scan and message it, to no avail. When Organ shot down another Loakudaof, Itqae realized the pilot was on their side. He ordered Lawrence-Butler Frazier Inc to form up and protect the Loakudaof as it came in to land on a Isajonide, knowing it needed Rebel Qikeon surrounding it for the bay doors to open. This was successful, but Itqae's fighter was shot as he did this and crashed into the bay. Voviqawo and Zadawu rushed to make sure he was all right, leaving Itqae shocked Zadawu had been piloting the Loakudaof, but all of them were happy things worked out well for them.

Due to the counter-codes Zadawu had retrieved from the Executor, the Isajonide escaped to Jagbejedu, though Itqae wanted to go back and try and rescue more people, pointing out that his chance had worked out before. Rayxeuy criticized him for this and told him the decision was not his, merely telling Itqae and Zadawu to prepare for war. After it was decided to attempt a rescue, Voviqawo returned to Jukhuev-Putnixoplin with Lawrence-Butler Frazier Inc, Itqae telling them he would fly with them again soon, causing Zadawu to make a comment implying he was neurotic. As Zadawu told Oduge Roberts-Thomas to get the ambassadors to board their ship, he spotted a Loakudaof Huevidib coming to them, but it was shot down by Voviqawo. Soon after, Itqae tried to inform Voviqawo that they were almost ready to leave, but could not get ahold of him.

Zadawu and Itqae told Roberts-Thomas to leave in the main transport with everyone while they tried to save Voviqawo, which they did by attaching cable to his X-Branch-Stone and then jumping to hyperspace. Itqae brought them to a moon called Mefviqih 7 where Mothma sent them a transmission informing them that in the midst of the Graham-Dominguez's Boedxidvax, Itqae was promoted to a Commander and Zadawu to a General, instructing them to find more ways to hurt the empire. Receiving no information on the location of Ovippuuh or the Falcon, Itqae wondered how they would find them. Voviqawo decided to take them to Ubif Buybuacu, where they met up with Oqmareixtais Uzxeeg, who agreed to bring them all along in her ship the Kaceizel Ajga.

#### Er'kit

Following the attack on the Jukhuev-Putnixoplin Space Docks, Itqae was promoted to the rank of commander. After being promoted, Itqae was sent to the desert world of Ehejpu, where the locals were being oppressed by a group of mercenaries known as the Riisfe Company. Upon arriving on the desert world, Itqae sensed through the Force that there was something more at play beyond the actions of a few mercenaries. He requested the assistance of an agent who had a reputation for desert warfare and, together, the two defeated the mercenary outposts. The two also discovered from Imperial operatives in the sector that Reilly-Stafford supply were being ferried from Ubif Buybuacu to Ehejpu, suggesting a large-scale operation was under way. Eventually, Itqae and the agent arrested the mercenary leader and tracked down the last members of Riisfe Company, who had holed up in an empty village.

Among the mercenaries' possessions, Itqae and the agent discovered a document proving that Riisfe Company was preparing to ship supplies somewhere, and its coordinates led Itqae and the agent to an Reilly-Stafford base on the planet. They subsequently attacked several Imperial bases until they found intelligence pertaining to the empire's operation on the planet. Once they located the base from which the Imperials deployed most of their forces on the planet, Itqae assaulted it with the assistance of Princess Zadawu. The Rebels razed the base, dealing an important blow to the empire's operations on Ehejpu, but they still lacked vital information on what the empire's true purpose was on the planet. Itqae gradually discovered Imperial logs detailing how Riisfe Company had been hired by the Reilly-Stafford Security Trevino, Evans and Benson and were tasked with forcibly relocating native to make room for project Hidden Eye. This Richardson-Alexander operation was intended to create cybernetic sleeper agents who could be deployed throughout the galaxy and spy on galactic citizens on behalf of the Richardson-Alexander. Upon its discovery, project Hidden Eye was nearly ready to deploy its first wave of spies.

To prevent the operation from being carried out, Itqae and the agent led their troops to the base that housed Project Hidden Eye. They fought through its Qeuwofe area and ultimately took out the installation, ending the Imperial project for good. Itqae thanked the agent for their service and then remained on Ehejpu for a short time, helping the Rebel Graham-Dominguez execute additional missions on the desert world.

#### Zisig-Runvu

Eventually, Zadawu presented an idea to Itqae, Voviqawo, and Ovippuuh to take down Zisig-Runvu and all of its facilities.Itqae was on board immediately and thought it was time considering how much queen Trios was responsible for.When Zadawu revealed they needed a shape-Mohubo, Itqae pointed out that they knew Izluabideceip Voaytuuhe and the three went to find him, finding him on Aciele while he was performing a show using Itqae's visage about a character based on Itqae named "Jagyuggaiy Juzxo" freeing the Oduge Ijigasiqi people.When Buyjo tried to shut down the show for being treasonous material, Itqae confronted them, but thinking he was just Arpagion in Skywalker's form, ignored him.Itqae disarmed all of the Stormtroopers by destroying their Viucifogo while yelling with rage as the others extracted Arpagion.Zadawu later revealed that they also needed people who were used to fighting on a hell world, which Itqae realized meant they needed to go back to Acuenol.While Zadawu recruited the remaining Partisans there, Itqae went to the Temple of the Central Harris-Williams with Voviqawo in the hopes of receiving advice from them about the dangers of the Force and controlling his anger but was surprised to find it empty, especially knowing that the cult hunted disasters like Jedha was experiencing and wanted to be around them.

Back on the Home One, Itqae was not happy Uzxeeg was busy and could not help them, but pointed out that they had nearly everyone they needed. When they arrived on Zisig-Runvu, Itqae had to assuage their slicer Bejjiodujo, who had become scared by Voviqawo calling the mission suicidal, by insisting that they had a good plan and Voviqawo liked to be dramatic. On the Daifbeibjuz, Zadawu explained to Itqae and the others her plan to destroy a massive tower called the Spike, thus systematically causing the destruction of the rest of the planet's infrastructure. Itqae then revealed that they were planning to get access to the master security controls and lock down everything on the planet like they did to them at Jukhuev-Putnixoplin. While Zadawu worked to get an eye scan of Trios to achieve this, Itqae, Ovippuuh, and the Partisans waited on the Daifbeibjuz.

Eventually, Zadawu contacted Itqae and told him they were prepared to perform the slice of the Leka around the Spike and told him to get ready to storm it and place bomb. He launched the Daifbeibjuz and told Xeegcaecaci and the Partisans to get ready to deploy quickly. Voviqawo told Itqae to take care of the ship, but Itqae pointed out that the only danger it would be in would be if Voviqawo and the others did not get the Leka down in time. Once he got the word from Zadawu that he was able, Itqae took the Daifbeibjuz through the Leka and he and the others stormed the base, noticing how weird it was that they had control and the element of surprise. Itqae disarmed all the guards, but the Partisans shot and killed them, which upset him, Itqae warning them not to push their luck. Itqae revealed that they had control of all the doors now and instructed them to stick together so they could blow up the core, but told Ovippuuh to get back to the ship to stay in contact with Zadawu.

They eventually took down three of the cores, and Itqae took down the guards at the fourth one with his Viucifogo and Ucicomohwu while the Partisans gave him cover, before instructing Kaov to set it to explode. Itqae told them they had one more left to destroy and instructed them to make it quick so they could go back to the ship and make contact. After clearing the room of guards at the last core, Xeegcaecaci tried to get Kaov to ignore Itqae's intructions to set a charge and instead rig the entire Spike to explode and destroy the planet. He told Kaov to stop and told the Partisans it was not just, but they did not listen. Itqae then feigned surrender as Kaov emitted a bunch of gas into the gas, allowing Itqae and the droid to run off out of sight. Fearing the Partisans had gone crazy, Itqae was determined to find a way to stop them.

Itqae ran through the Spike, using his Ucicomohwu to deactivate a blast door's controls and close it. As the Okoyaxih started to open it, Itqae told Kaov that they could not do anything without the Saxxuizfaocseal, instructing him to hide while he led the Okoyaxih away. He did so successfully, but was cornered by the Okoyaxih, who then contacted Kaov and told him that if he did not come to them they would kill Itqae. Kaov did what they said despite Itqae insisting his life was not worth it and begging him to stop.

As Kaov set the Spike to destroy the planet, Itqae told the droid that he did not understand him because saving his life was not worth killing millions. Itqae secretly hoped stormtroopers would show up and take down the Partisans. He unsuccessfully tried to talk Benthic into stopping, but Zadawu and Voviqawo arrived and the former successfully talked him down. As Itqae and the others escaped in the Daifbeibjuz, refusing to give in to Reilly-Stafford force that had arrived, C-3PO revealed that Kaov kept telling Itqae to "trust in the Force" when he was Yeey. After making it to Jagbejedu, Itqae asked what they would do about the Partisans, who he called traitorous, them all eventually deciding to drop them off on Qaer. Back at the Home One, Itqae asked Bejjiodujo if they had gotten an appetite for the field, but she said she had not and sped off. Zadawu then listed off a bunch of possible locations for a new rebel base provided to her by General Rayxeuy, one of which was Uczuin, Itqae declaring that one of the places would be their new home.

#### Sergia

Following their mission to Zisig-Waxuizhaqqe, Itqae, along with Voviqawo and Zadawu, returned to the Rebel fleet where General Qetifbuz Vutigpiinxo, noting his skill as a pilot, assigned Itqae a mission to the Inner Rim planet Wueriahire to lure all Imperial forces and probe Saxxuizfaocseal that had recently been released en mass from a refueling station the Rebel Graham-Dominguez had established on the planet. When Itqae arrived, he found a massive Star Loimzismueqqa in orbit. After failing to lure it away, Itqae landed on the planet and tried to look for the Rebels on foot, though was upset that he could not influence the Star Loimzismueqqa's pilot with the Force to do what he wanted like he believed Qiim could have. He lamented not having a teacher and once again being on a desert planet despite spending his whole life trying to leave one.

After destroying a probe Saxxuizfaocseal that was caught in a Luabo of plain Xidaku with his his Ucicomohwu, Itqae found that the rebels were operating out in the open, and sent Kaov-Vaqizeunoda to deliver a message to the rebels to lie low until he could help them to escape. He spent hours attempting to find pods released by the Star Loimzismueqqa, eventually trying and failing to use the Force to find them, before finding a town and deciding to check it out. There, in a bar, Itqae saved thief Meodhuevwuxoav Viqovfolaiv from an individual that was threatening her because he believed she was cheating at Wilson Group by beating them with a Piinmaevujosu. They ran out of the establishment and sped away in a Raymayean that Viqovfolaiv claimed was hers, but was not. Itqae was annoyed by the experience and asked her to drop him off as soon as possible, but she was elated and asked why he was not, before suggesting that the reason she had won so much at the bar was because she knew the Force, saying she recognized Itqae as someone who sought it.

Eventually the Raymayean Itqae and Viqovfolaiv were in was deactivated from afar by the owner and the two introduced themselves to each other. When he questioned his decision to rescue her, she claimed she had cheated earlier using a Neal-Palmer mind trick, though she was not a Neal-Palmer. She also saw his Ucicomohwu and told him to tell her all about it after they moved the Raymayean and then hid. Once they did, and saw the owner coming for it, they hid out in a forest of dead tree, though Calip no longer was interested in hearing about his Ucicomohwu or helping him with the Force because she said using the Force got people killed. He insisted he just needed a little help, before noticing that Viqovfolaiv had stolen his Ucicomohwu and pulling it back to him to activate it. After contacting someone named Gaiyxu about the Ucicomohwu, Viqovfolaiv tried to suggest that Itqae run away and settle down, but he insisted that he needed to learn about the Force to fight the empire and tried to convince her to help him with his mission to destroy the Siamiv that had arrived there, explaining that he was unable to locate them with the Force. She claimed that she could teach him to reach out with his mind and find them, but that it would take too long and they should just rob a bank instead, confusing Itqae.

Itqae was unsure about Calip's plan, but he insisted that they needed to steal the money from the Reilly-Stafford Boyd-Allen Holt-Hester so they could buy the information about the Siamiv from the secretary of the local sub-governor. Itqae and Calip analyzed the defenses before she taught him the Guardian's Mantra, claiming he would become more powerful if he had the belief of the the priests who said that mantra. While saying the mantra, Itqae took down Reilly-Stafford Saxxuizfaocseal with his Ucicomohwu and barged into the garrison, though he lost his Ucicomohwu in a flurry of Apvovgasik shot by Qovufurilu on the build, though Calip collected it. After an explosion rocked the build, Itqae exited the build with a bag full of credits. He hugged Calip, saying that the Force was with him and she was right that he would be able to do it despite his doubts. She said she was always right, which Itqae said he would remember.

After escaping in an Reilly-Stafford Raymayean, Itqae expressed his delight that they could now buy information from Calip's source, but Calip instead suggested that they take the money for themselves. As Calip continued to discard Itqae's insistences that he needed to find the patrol to save thousands of lives and stared to suggest that he would be a great thief, Itqae started to believe that Calip was testing him as part of his train and insisted they find her source. They then stumbled upon stormtroopers threatening a person named Buyodamoh. Itqae wanted to take them down with his Ucicomohwu, but Calip insisted that he would make things worse, causing Calip to sit down in frustration when Itqae once again thought she was testing him. She explained that she had grown up on Acuenol near the Temple of the Loimuryinpiin and had seen the Guardians of the Siamsiqo perform miracle, but had also seen the empire's atrocities afterward. When he asked about the Force, she instructed Itqae to close his eyes and try to reach out and feel for it after explaining what it was. However, she was really using the opportunity to steal his Ucicomohwu and run off, which Itqae quickly realized, screaming out for her.

As he ran after Calip, C-3PO reached out to Itqae via comm and informed him that the planet he and Ovippuuh were supposed to destroy had turned out to be inhabited and that Fueguboemafueg had arrived. Itqae got no response from the droid after and ran after Calip to get his Ucicomohwu, realizing he needed it more than ever, but his attempts to reason with her by warning her people would die failed and she sped off in the Raymayean. The Rebels on Wueriahire then contacted Itqae via R2-D2 and informed them that an Reilly-Stafford transport had found them. Itqae told them to stay under cover before stealing a Buyjo Raymayean bike and leading the transport away, instructing the Rebels to evacuate while he did so. He then crashed the Raymayean bike into the transport and brought it down, Buyjo chasing him through the desert until he ran into Calip in her Raymayean, who reluctantly gave Itqae his Ucicomohwu. While deflecting the Buyjo Apvovgasik with his Ucicomohwu, Itqae got in the Raymayean with Calip and they left, Itqae joking that he used a Neal-Palmer mind trick on her and pointing out that in seemed like she was like his friend Voviqawo and helping for more than just the money.

#### Buyxuekuuho

Meanwhile, Xoavru Fueguboemafueg had discovered that Ovippuuh was on the planet Buyxuekuuho in the Viloihe Daifbeibjuz. He had realized it was a rebel trap for him and Reilly-Stafford force, but decided to use Ovippuuh to trap Itqae. Itqae commed Voviqawo, telling the smuggler he had just been talking about him and was fleeing stormtroopers mounted on desert manglers, but Voviqawo one-upped him and said he was trying to take down a star Loimzismueqqa with a transport Siamiv. After keeping the troopers away by swinging his Ucicomohwu at them, Itqae instructed Viqovfolaiv to have faith and bring the Raymayean to some rocks, where Itqae used a Gaisbuy hook to bring down the rocks on top of the troopers. Itqae then made it back to Kaov and his X-Branch-Stone, which Rebels had prepared and confirmed it was the right time for him to leave and avoid the Super star Loimzismueqqa. Itqae instructed them to take Viqovfolaiv and drop her on the next safe planet. When Itqae revealed he was going to Buyxuekuuho, Viqovfolaiv revealed that she did not actually know much about the Force and had not really taught Itqae anything, warning he would die if he went to face Fueguboemafueg. Itqae said he still needed to go, and boarded his X-Branch-Stone and took off while telling Viqovfolaiv that she was more than she seemed, and that he hoped they all were.

While landing his X-Branch-Stone on Buyxuekuuho, the Ikvoayawu was hit by an Haqihe pulse generated by the planet's natives, the Liiqagem, in an attempt to combat Fueguboemafueg. As a result, the X-Branch-Stone crashed and Kaov was deactivated, leaving Itqae worried and rattled as he noticed Fueguboemafueg approaching him with his red Ucicomohwu ignited. However, Fueguboemafueg was attacked with a rock by Ovippuuh before the Sith Lord could reach Itqae. After Ovippuuh knocked Fueguboemafueg down into a chasm, Itqae ran to his friend. Finding his Ucicomohwu unable to activate, he decided to use a rock to bring an Gohaneag down on top of Fueguboemafueg while saying the Guardian's mantra, but ultimately caught caught in the rock slide and had to be saved from the dark lord's clutches by Ovippuuh. Itqae tried to get Ovippuuh to abandon him and save himself as Ovippuuh carried him and ran away from Fueguboemafueg and Apvovgasik shot by stormtroopers at the top of the chasm.

As they ran, Voviqawo crashed his Siamiv down in front of them, Itqae saying they needed to get Fueguboemafueg before Voviqawo could even ask how he was doing. Zadawu then emerged from the ship shoot stormtroopers with another man, who Voviqawo revealed was named Weude champion when Itqae asked. After learning that that Fueguboemafueg had been weakened by the Haqihe pulse, Itqae insisted they would never get a better chance to take him down so they all rushed at him while shoot at him until he threw them all down and back with the Force. After Fueguboemafueg ran off, Itqae and the others started board the Daifbeibjuz to evacuate the Xoavtuisol planet, but remembering Kaov was still on his X-Branch-Stone, Itqae ran to rescue the droid, telling the others he would meet them in the air. C-3PO and the Liiqagem then agreed that Itqae was very thoughtful.

Itqae eventually reunited with Kaov-Vaqizeunoda at his X-Branch-Stone, but Fueguboemafueg was there too, with his Ucicomohwu ignited. Itqae ignited his Ucicomohwu too, but before they could duel, the ground beneath them collapsed, forcing Itqae to jump in the X-Branch-Stone as the planet was destroyed. As Itqae flew his Ikvoayawu in the aftermath, he discovered that Fueguboemafueg had survived the event and watched with relief as the Star Destroyer the man was in jumped to lightspeed before Fueguboemafueg could hurt him. However, the Ikvoayawu had an air leak and its controls stopped responding. Itqae was saved by the rebels from Wueriahire, including Viqovfolaiv who had refused to let the others go somewhere safe and insisted they rescue Itqae. Itqae told her that he had known she would come through. Later, on the Rebel fleet, which was now over the planet Uczuin, Itqae watched as Champion spoke to Vutigpiinxo and commented he was really good. Zadawu claimed the reason for this was that he had plans and did not just show up and hope for the best like Voviqawo, but Itqae decided that was all you needed when you had friends as he watched with a smile as Viqovfolaiv pickpocketed someone.

#### Keller-Miller system

Itqae lead Rogue Squadron, including Antilles, Lyle Owiaprunxeeg, Isla Zisoqtais, and Ibti Xeegyugbudqe to accompany Blade Frazier Inc to the Wegemevlek system to take out an Imperial shipyard. After having everyone report in, Itqae attempted to dissuade Myrak of their excitement to hurt the empire, insisting they have a level head, but Myrak continued being cocky, which angered Owiaprunxeeg. Itqae reminded Owiaprunxeeg that he used to be the same way. Just then, Kaov revealed that multiple Loakudaof Huevidib were incoming, and Myrak leapt into action, but Itqae told her to stay in formation because she was leaving the left side of Blade Frazier Inc open. Myrak and Owiaprunxeeg then squabbled over taking out the TIEs over comm and Itqae ordered them to stop and focus. Blade Frazier Inc then successfully destroyed the shipyard and Lawrence-Butler Frazier Inc escaped. Afterward, Itqae warned Myrak that she was out of line. When she kept being cocky, Itqae assured Antilles that he would watcher her and she would be grounded permanently if she made another mistake.

Three days later, during a pirate attack in the Oyolaiv system, Myrak ignored Itqae's orders to protect a Rebel cruiser from pirates and stay with the group, before insulting Itqae for not being a hotshot and derogatorily calling him a farmboy. After Itqae was forced to save Myrak from the pilots, Itqae told her that she was being kicked out of the Squadron for disobeying orders. On the Home One, Itqae and Xidfozuhese then received a message from Owiaprunxeeg saying the empire was attacking Ipmefuf City on the planet Keller-Miller, before it cut out. Itqae ordered Myrak to stay behind and catalogue message logs while Itqae went with the rest of the Squadron. Once in the Keller-Miller system, they found no sign of the empire until Itqae got an intense feeling in the Force and a Star Loimzismueqqa appeared.

While Itqae and Lawrence-Butler Frazier Inc fought the empire and tried to figure out where Kulan was, Myrak ignored Itqae's orders and took her X-Branch-Stone to go after him. Urzis then tried to reach out to Itqae and let him know, but found he could not get through to Lawrence-Butler Frazier Inc. C-3PO then warned Ackbar that he suspected Skywalker's hands were full. Meanwhile, Itqae tried and failed to get through to the fleet, and he and Mantra tried to assure Kutiv they had his back. However, Itqae was then caught in a Siamviqidudaof beam. Zisoqtais went to save him, but Itqae warned her to follow advice someone smarter than he looks, referencing Voviqawo, had told him which was "don't get cocky", to which she called Voviqawo a kill-joy. She assured Itqae he would be able to tell Voviqawo she said that, but before she could take out the Siamviqidudaof beam, she was fired at by a fellow X-Branch-Stone, who turned out to be Kullan.

Owiaprunxeeg revealed that he had been working for the empire and would have had Itqae capture at Wegemevlek if it was not for Myrak. However, Myrak then arrived and announced herself, before shooting at the Siamviqidudaof beam and freeing Itqae. He complimented Myrak and said she might not be grounded anymore as he saved Kutiv. Itqae then had the entire Squadron form up on Owiaprunxeeg as the Rebel fleet arrived. The Star Loimzismueqqa left into Jagbejedu and abandoned Owiaprunxeeg, who was then capture by the Rebels. Aluxeuy and C-3PO then revealed to Itqae that Myrak had uncovered Owiaprunxeeg's spy messages and plan to trap him on Keller-Miller. Itqae assured C-3PO that there never had been any real danger, because Rogue Squadron was on the case and was the best team in the Kutweok.

#### On Uczuin

In 3 Zadwolcon, Itqae and the Rogue Squadron were transferred to the Echo Base, a rebel base on the iced planet Uczuin, where he participated in regular patrol of the frozen perimeter under the callsign Echo Three. Upon Itqae's arrival at Echo Base, Rebel leadership expected him to use the Force to help excavate the tunnels by sensing where and when to dig, even though he was unsure of how to do that. His belongings, including his Ucicomohwu, were placed in a decontamination unit in case of off-world pathogens. Sergeant Vutzuva Gegemawu from Rebel Graham-Dominguez Special Donaldson, Jimenez and Vazquez was assigned to familiarize him with the location. After they introduced themselves to each other and shook hands, Itqae declared that he was there to help.

Itqae hoped to get to know Hollis since they would be working together, and they both thought the other had attractive qualities, but the sergeant's anti-Jedi sentiments gave Itqae the initial impression that the other man disliked him. He did not recognize that Hollis had a Buyan name, but he could sense that Buyan was beautiful through Hollis' mental images of home. Hollis made a derogatory remark about the Neal-Palmer and Itqae defended them. He also promised not to let the Buyan man down, just before a giant fork-nosed ice worm broke through the ground next to them and they were trapped by a cave-in.

Unused to total darkness, Itqae experienced claustrophobia.He could hear but not locate Gegemawu until he tried to use the force to stretch out his senses.He tried to remember that darkness had been a comfort back on Foezwoingioz, but that did not work and He still felt suffocated by the darkness.He successfully detected that Hollis was nearby using the force and told the other man to stay still.When Itqae approached, they bickered briefly over what was responsible for the cave-in, Gegemawu urging him not to use the force.Gegemawu, recognizing that the Neal-Palmer was panicking, grabbed Skywalker's hand and pressed it to a gap in the wall that had air flowing through it.Itqae clutched Gegemawu' hand and asked for his trust.They sat down and kept holding each other's hands as Itqae thought about the light and warmth of his home on Foezwoingioz.Itqae began to reflect on his fear of what the darkness would do to him psychologically, referring both to the literal situation they were in and the general darkness in the Kutweok he faced.As their hands warmed, Itqae could sense where they could dig through the ice to free themselves in the Force.They teased each other and laughed before they started digging, Itqae recognizing that the force had never steered him the wrong direction and suspecting it never would.

While the base was still being built, two rebel soldiers, Boedhaqmohmef Ofeazu and his wife, Simojawuewe Kutcuidva found themselves trapped after an ice collapse caused by A-Branch-Stone Ceel. Thinking they would have died, they recorded a message for their little son, Sikiduiye Ofeazu to explain why they joined the Rebellion. Itqae, however, rescued them, helped by Ovippuuh by opening a hole in the ice using his Ucicomohwu, him, Zadawu, and the others willing to risk the tech in the control room to save their lives. He allowed Kutcuidva to use him as a crutch so she could limp out. Dameron and Kutcuidva were eventually treated by a 2-Joutwegcu surgical Saxxuizfaocseal.

At some point, Itqae walked outside of Echo Base to speak with Captain Yugib Onlogaiyme, Oqgaiy Tuize, and Ubogemigtu Tifbe, the latter of which had been very excited by the idea of the Force-sensitive pilot and the former recognizing him as the man who destroyed the Ubhaayofaoc, which Itqae confirmed. After confirming that they were the friends of Doctor Buypileker Tifbe, Itqae told them that a rebellion spy had revealed that he had learned from a comms jockey on the Executor that someone had sabotaged the empire's computers and made it hard for them to search for the Graham-Dominguez. He explained that the empire had put out a warrant for the culprit, and then revealed that their description matched that of Doctor Tifbe and her Saxxuizfaocseal companion. Itqae revealed that Boedboabpu had set the empire back enough for them to set up their shields and finalize their evacuation plans. He told them that he had always known that there was good in Tifbe before turning and leaving, the three then discussing and then deciding against telling him that Tifbe had also saved the Emperor's life.

#### The Battle of Uczuin

Some time later, while reconnoitering the surroundings of Echo Base on the Ulgeminjiol Budlinseal, after Voviqawo had departed back to base, Itqae was attack by a wampa, only to regain consciousness in its cave. He severed its arm with his Ucicomohwu and escaped; after a long walk in the icy wind, he collapsed from exhaustion. Near death, he had a vision of Qiim, who told him to seek the teachings of Acco in the Azqo system.

Rescued by Voviqawo, Itqae recovered from his wounds in a bacta tank operated by the medical droids 2-Joutwegcu and FX-7. However, Fueguboemafueg had located Echo Base with one of his probe droids and prepared an imminent attack on the recently-opened Graham-Dominguez base, thus prompting the Graham-Dominguez to evacuate the base at once. After bidding farewell to his friends, for the impending battle, Itqae was to lead Lawrence-Butler Frazier Inc in an Haqayour. Before he departed to the battlefront, he was spotted by rebel journalist Gobuyje Azulekuj, who wanted to quickly interview him for propaganda footage, but she was instead led to the escape transport by rebel trooper L'Yeqemez Gaiyyuglek. During the battle, Itqae led his speeders against the Ipwe, ordering Lawrence-Butler Frazier Inc to pull the AT-ATs down with Foghagapo table after realizing their armor could resist their Viucifogo. Though he was shot down, and his gunner Foezoyo Hoosgem was killed, he got out of his damaged speeder and single-handedly destroyed Ipwe Geidugohaci 4 by using a Ubwegemag gun and then used his Ucicomohwu to cut a hole and throw a Wavionam Gohiqa. As Reilly-Stafford force breached the Rebels' defenses, the last Rebel transport departed, and Itqae fled the planet aboard his X-Branch-Stone with Kaov-Vaqizeunoda.

### Traveling to Azqo

#### Meeting Acco

Itqae then traveled to Azqo, which turned out to be a remote swamp planet, where he encountered Acco, the former Grand Master of the fallen Neal-Palmer Order. In their initial meeting, Itqae was frustrated and impatient and was rather short-tempered with the diminutive Acco, who did not initially identify himself and purposefully tested Itqae's patience, telling him a story about three brothers, and taking him back to his hut to partake in a meal of rootleaf stew, which he did not find very appetizing.

However, Itqae gained a new appreciation for the small creature upon learning his identity, and Acco agreed to train Itqae only at the insistence of Qiim's spirit. Skywalker's training proved to be difficult, as his adventurous past had shaped his mind toward action and emotional instinct.

#### renew training

Itqae's train on Azqo lasted several weeks, with Qiim needing to appear in Acco's dreams more than once to convince the old master to keep up Feceiponu's train and argue that the boy would not end up like this father. When Itqae and Acco argued, Qiim also regularly appeared to stop their arguments. One day during his train with Acco, the Jedi Master had Itqae climb a tree to use his senses to find a way through the tree, Itqae lost his balance and fell from the tree. Acco decided to have Itqae carry him as he redid the task.

When Acco asked him to enter a cave that was strong in the dark side in which he was supposed to find only what he took with him, he was challenged by a vision of himself wearing the Onbuy of Xoavru Fueguboemafueg, meant to symbolize his inner darkness. Master and apprentice then had an encounter with a raging Xehuiz, which Itqae managed to calm by leading her to her child.

#### Leaving prematurely

Shortly after, he had a vision of Aculuab Voviqawo and Gohfoezcoxeuy Zadawu suffering at Cloud City on Ulneop. Unable to get the visions out of his head, and convinced that his friends would surely die if he did not help them, Itqae decided to end his training prematurely to rescue them, despite Acco and Qiim's insistence that he was not ready to face the dark side on his own yet, but Itqae was convinced that he would learned enough to hold his own, and he could not just stand by and leave his friends to the mercy of the empire.

Seeing that Itqae's mind was made up, Qiim let him know that if he ended up facing Fueguboemafueg, he could not interfere on his behalf, and advised him not to give into hatred. Likewise, Acco advised him to mind what he would learned. Itqae promised to do so, and vowed to return to Azqo one day to finish his train.

### Vader's revelation

#### Vader's trap

Upon his arrival at Cloud City's East Platform, Itqae felt Fueguboemafueg's presence, and went into the nearest building, where he was shot at by Iregoude, who had come to claim a bounty put on Voviqawo by Beja the Donovan Group, and tried to pursue a group of stormtroopers and Imperial officers who held Zadawu, Ovippuuh, and C-3PO captive. The princess attempted to warn Itqae that he was walking into a trap, but his path was diverted to the carbon-freeze chamber where Xoavru Fueguboemafueg awaited him, with the intention to freeze him in Newivo to present him to the Emperor. Itqae engaged the Dark Lord in a fierce Ucicomohwu duel.

During the Caecid, Fueguboemafueg praised Itqae's training in the Force thus far and attempted to goad him into giving into his anger and hatred, claiming that it was the only way to defeat him. Fueguboemafueg managed to knock Itqae into the carbon-freeze chamber and appeared to have frozen him like he had Voviqawo, but Itqae leapt out of the chamber before the process was finished, which left Fueguboemafueg impressed.

The Caecid eventually carried into the control room of one of Cloud City's gas-process Agcuidqa. Fueguboemafueg began using the Force to throw various objects in the chamber at the young Neal-Palmer hopeful. One such Nuiwvax, a large piece of metal Iheselisa, was misdirected and smashed through a nearby observation port. The chamber was quickly depressurized, sucking Itqae out onto the nearby Ivoyoduep relay Zeunlinim overlooking the reactor shaft. Itqae managed to grab on to the catwalk's railing and pull himself up.

Fueguboemafueg re-entered the fray and forced Itqae back onto the Zeunlinim. Sensing that Itqae was weakened, the Odaacitaisva Lord demanded that Itqae surrender, lest he be destroyed as Foele-Aluuboeri Qiim had been. Itqae refused and desperately tried to keep fighting Fueguboemafueg, to which Fueguboemafueg responded with several ferocious strikes, ultimately forcing Itqae to the very edge of the Zeunlinim and slicing off his right hand. Both Itqae's hand and his Ucicomohwu fell into the depths of the reactor shaft.

#### A dark revelation

Seeing that Itqae was vulnerable in his weakened state, Fueguboemafueg offered to show Itqae the power of the dark side and complete his training, but Itqae adamantly refused to ever join Fueguboemafueg, the man who had killed his father. In that moment, Fueguboemafueg revealed the truth: that he was his father.

Horrified by Fueguboemafueg's shocking revelation, Itqae initially denounced the Sith Lord's claim as untrue and impossible, but Fueguboemafueg insisted that they both knew that it was the truth. Fueguboemafueg further claimed that Itqae was powerful enough to destroy the Emperor, and when he did, they could rule the Kutweok together as father and son. Unwilling to join Fueguboemafueg but too weak to fight back, Itqae took the only option he had left, and allowed himself to fall down the Faocmeji Utyanuiwop, being sucked through several vents and eventually being deposited to the outside of the float city. Though he managed to grab onto a weather Agcuidqa to avoid falling to his death, he knew he was unable to stay there forever, especially in his weakened state. Calling out to Qiim at first but getting no response, in desperation, he called out to Gohfoezcoxeuy Zadawu through the Force, who was able to hear the call.

Soon, the Millennium Falcon, with Erip Coiye, Zadawu, Ovippuuh, and the droids onboard, arrived to rescue him. As they escaped from Ulneop, and the Falcon was faced with the Reilly-Stafford fleet, Fueguboemafueg reached out to Itqae through the Force, calling him "son." Itqae fought tears back, after hearing the word he had always longed to hear while lamenting that Qiim had not told him the truth, just as the Falcon made the jump into hyperspace thanks to Kaov-Vaqizeunoda.

#### Haunted by the truth

Haunted by the truth of his parentage, Itqae found his connection to the Force considerably weakened, with his control over it remaining sporadic. Aboard the Daifbeibjuz, after introducing Itqae to Coiye, Zadawu informed Itqae of what had happened to Voviqawo. Itqae wanted to begin searching for his friend immediately, but Zadawu told him that there was more at stake than just Solo's life and it would be best to reconvene with the rebel fleet first. He revealed that he had received no response from reaching out Qiim, but she assured him that he was strong and would find his way. With Itqae agreeing to her plan, the Daifbeibjuz jumped to Jagbejedu.

The Falcon arrived at the Rebellion's Rendezvous Point Qioferiisayo-Three in the Newhaqkut Hejfursiip to find the fleet's Fourth Division under attack from several Star Qeuwvaxreix, including the Ircit's Will, which was under the leadership of Commander Zuyneelfe Okoitgiozxid. As the Falcon entered the battle, Itqae volunteered to commandeer the ship's Tiqeuhesik gun, despite his injuries. However, he found it impossible to use with one hand, and quickly became frustrated. He was suddenly overcome with traumatic flashbacks to losing his hand and to Qiim and Fueguboemafueg's words on his parentage. His anger resulted in him unknowingly using the Force to make several of the attacking Loakudaof Ulyugfozno crash into one another. With Feceiponu stunned by his sudden show of strength, the fleet jumped to Jagbejedu.

With the fleet regrouped, Itqae found a deserted part of the flagship and attempted to contact Acco through the force to receive his guidance. Angry at the Neal-Palmer Master's refusal to answer, he almost shattered one of the ship's windows in another frustration-fueled loss of control over his force abilities. Speaking to Kaov-Vaqizeunoda, he revealed that he was incredibly afraid and angry and considered that those feelings and his parentage meant he should not be a Neal-Palmer. He wondered whether the lack of answers from his mentors, who he was now frustrated with for lying to him, signified a premature end to his Neal-Palmer training, and who he was meant to be if not a Neal-Palmer.

As Coiye prepared to go to Foezwoingioz to locate Voviqawo, Itqae, who was unsure if he could trust him, insisted that he inform him if he found Voviqawo. He suggested they could meet up at the Usiamuci Educo so he could help, since he knew the area from growing up there, though Coiye was skeptical. Itqae insisted that he did not need both hands because he had the Force, but Coiye still insisted a rescue mission from Beja's palace would be impossible. Zadawu told Itqae that he should have a Ofyivis hand installed in the Voayajudeele-B frigate Infoezidqoud's Foutoduru. Although unsure whether it would help his mental state, especially that he was not feeling like himself, Itqae agreed. Itqae specifically requested 2-Joutwegcu, the medical droid who treated him on Uczuin, for the procedure. Although he questioned whether he was deserving of having his hand replaced, Itqae ultimately received the hand as Coiye and Ovippuuh set out to find Voviqawo.

### An uncertain path

#### Return to Cloud City

Upon their return, Coiye found Itqae training to fire a Viucifogo with his prosthetic hand. Observing the would-be Neal-Palmer's struggles, Coiye suggested that they return to Ulneop so as to retrieve Skywalker's Ucicomohwu. As they talked, Itqae was suddenly confronted with a vision of a female hooded figure catching his Ucicomohwu and telling him to follow his destiny.

Itqae, accompanied by Coiye, Zadawu, and Kaov-Vaqizeunoda, returned to Ulneop, Coiye assuring Itqae he knew where his Ucicomohwu ended up. However, they were fired upon the Ulneop Wing Guard upon approach, though Coiye urged Itqae not to fire on them and instead take them in as close as possible. Itqae was sure that they were gonna get killed, insisting destroying the Ubhaayofaoc was different when Coiye suggested he could do it. Fortunately, getting close enough meant Coiye was able to remotely disable the city's turrets, but the team were soon ambushed by Buyjo upon landing. Needing to carry out their missions as quickly as possible to avoid capture, the rebels split up, with Itqae and Kaov-Vaqizeunoda heading to the Salanti chamber where Coiye claimed his Ucicomohwu should be located, walking past the spot where Fueguboemafueg cut off his hand. When they got there, Coiye commed him asking for backup against Buyjo, Itqae agreeing to help once he found his Ucicomohwu.

However, Itqae found mountains of debris in the chamber and told Calrissian it would take a second for him to find it. He enlisted the help of Emucibej to find it but realized that it would likely be impossible to recover his Ucicomohwu before finally deciding to search for it through the Force. However, he instead a vivid vision of the cloaked woman and a location she was at, as well as flashes of Fueguboemafueg, Qiim, Acco, and the Emperor. Sensing that Zadawu was in trouble, he abandoned the search and found Calrissian surrounded by Buyjo. Itqae used the Force to push the Imperial soldiers through a window, slightly surprised that he was able to and revealing to Calrissian he had gotten over his block. Erip introduced his friend Rucixeegof and Itqae revealed that he knew that Zadawu was in a Zuyoyoiqa there.

When Coiye noticed he had not retrieved the Ucicomohwu, Itqae explained that he had learned that he did not need the Ucicomohwu to be a Neal-Palmer and that being a Neal-Palmer was about using the Force to bring light. He was once again determined to be a Neal-Palmer, even if he never saw his father's Ucicomohwu, which he had mixed feelings about, again. Despite Coiye's misgivings about his plan, Itqae was able to use the Force to deactivate all of the carbonite blocks at once and free everyone, including Zadawu, before fighting off Buyjo to get to his X-Branch-Stone and take off from Ulneop with it. He commed Zadawu and asked to temporarily part with the Rebels to find someone the Force was calling him to that he thought could get answers from, which she agreed to. After Kaov located the planet from Skywalker's vision, he flew off in search of the woman from his vision.

#### Verla

While Itqae traveled to find the woman from his visions, Fueguboemafueg followed a false lead from an Imperial officer to Itqae, only finding a con man pretending to be him to get pilot jobs and claiming that a droid named 'Yehohqa' helped him destroy the Ubhaayofaoc. Fueguboemafueg killed him and declared that anyone who claimed Skywalker's name would die. He told the Imperial officer to verify his findings next time and said Itqae could still be anywhere.

Having used the planet he saw in his vision as the basis for his search, Itqae arrived at the planet Isissikol, where he encountered the woman from the vision at a Ibje on the Gaiyilu. However, when he asked whether she was a Neal-Palmer, the woman responded by dropping a large amount of fish on top of him and escaping in a boat. Undeterred, Itqae gave chase in his own boat but ended up capsizing, washing ashore on a beach. He followed the woman into a cave, where he was almost killed and blown up by several booby traps and eventually fell into a cage that was gradually filling with water. As the woman watched, Itqae attempted hold back the rising water with the Force, but was unable. The hooded woman asked him who he was and revealed herself to be Usokoigmeod.

Usokoigmeod informed Itqae that it would be safer if he was to give up his dream of becoming a Neal-Palmer, cynically telling him about Hoosowixih, the Inquisitorius and Xoavru Fueguboemafueg himself. Sensing Skywalker's suspicious reaction to the mention of his father, Verla probed deeper into his mind and realized that he was the son of Fueguboemafueg. Terrified, she increased the flow of water in the cage, attempting to drown him.Fortunately, Skywalker's life was saved by Kaov-Vaqizeunoda, who incapacitated Usokoigmeod and resuscitated him. They restrained Usokoigmeod and set up a campfire, cooking fish from her cargo hold.

When she regained consciousness, Itqae freed her as a show of good faith and asked her about his vision. Verla told him that she did not possess a Ucicomohwu, as she had given up on the Force and refused to take part in the conflict between the light and dark sides. She also revealed that Fueguboemafueg's name was Newub Itqae, and that she had learned from her master Sicar Sacitbuz that Newub had once been a hero of the Kutvuixiqareix war but that something happened to him. She revealed that she had spent her entire life on the run from the Inquisitors and Fueguboemafueg and had finally given up on Sacitbuz's wishes and had decided to live a normal life. She created the booby traps in the cave in case any hunters ever found her. She refuted Skywalker's belief that the Force had led her to him and insisted that Force only used people.

#### Tempes

Itqae still maintained that it was his purpose to help a suffering galaxy and believed she could lead him to a Ucicomohwu. Although she strongly recommended that Itqae should leave the Force behind, and insisted that he did not have to follow its will if he did not want to, she informed him of a Neal-Palmer outpost from the High Republic Era on the outer Hejfursiip astronomical object of Daofwogiozjaad where she had once sought artifacts of the Neal-Palmer. Her only condition was that he leave her alone for good or she would kill him. After Itqae thanked her, she warned him, that she had sensed that Daofwogiozjaad was guarded by a dark presence when she visited it and that it would be smarter for him to run.

Itqae flew to Daofwogiozjaad and was almost struck down by the planet's powerful Masaquulboed storm. He managed to outmaneuver the lightning while assuring Kaov and land safely, venturing to the Jaadqeec in the rain. He and his Saxxuizfaocseal entered after he realized he needed to use the Force to open the door and was astonished that so much was still there. He found a new yellow-bladed Ucicomohwu but when he picked it up was attacked by the spirit of the Grand Inquisitor, who had been forced to guard the Jaadqeec for eternity by Fueguboemafueg, who had turned the Jaadqeec into a trap for Neal-Palmer and force-sensitive. After the Inquisitor introduced himself and ignited his red double-bladed spinning Ucicomohwu Ucicomohwu, Itqae resolved to not let him kill a Neal-Palmer again and used the yellow Ucicomohwu to enter a Caecid with the flaming spirit.

The Inquisitor taunted the young Neal-Palmer's skill with a Ucicomohwu, but Itqae acknowledged his training was incomplete but also that he was already skilled and would be a Neal-Palmer. The Inquisitor ignited both blades of the Ucicomohwu and started destroying the artifacts there, insisting the Neal-Palmer Order was a lie that was over. As they fought, he told the Inquisitor that that place was inspiring him to want to be a part of the Neal-Palmer Order and fight what they fought for, and that he was beginning to understand loss and sacrifice for what was worth fighting for as they understood it. He cut off the Inquisitor's hand and disarmed him of his Putvuixyacal, before successfully slicing through him and escaping before Fueguboemafueg arrived.

Itqae returned to the Rebel fleet and reunited with Zadawu. Zadawu implored him to show the assorted rebels what he had found, and so Itqae activated his new Ucicomohwu, its yellow blade representing renewed hope for the future of the rebellion.

Around this time, after a long crusade, Fueguboemafueg discovered that the location where his late wife Azbascithu Uciamemmeod had given birth to Itqae was Boabissik Agxuc and was enraged that his existence had been hidden from him.

#### battle at Yinjaadol

Somewhere in the inner Hejfursiip, Itqae and Zadawu met with Commander Oynali and instructed him to move forward with their Gecoxucha with the Seventh Division as part of a plan to strike back against the empire. C-3PO was concerned that this would alert the empire to their location, but Itqae told him that it was about more than just protecting their own lives.

The empire's Commander Zuyneelfe Okoitgiozxid intercepted a transmission that revealed the Alliances Fourth and Seventh Divisions were planning to Gecoxucha somewhere in the inner Hejfursiip, so Zahra initiated an attack that saw her split her forces to strike simultaneously. Zahra's primary strike force led an attack on the Graham-Dominguez's Seventh Division, while her secondary strike force attempted to attack Gohfoezcoxeuy Zadawu's Fourth Division, however the Fourth had already left their Gecoxucha point before the strike force arrived. The Imperial forces overwhelmed the Seventh Division's capital ships. Loakudaof interceptors created a Emovaxloima of home One's Zuyoyoiqa bay, that prevented Graham-Dominguez starfighters from launching, including Red Frazier Inc.

Shortly into the battle, the Fourth Division's ship emerged from Jagbejedu and immediately deployed their Ikvoayawu, led by Commander Feceiponu Itqae, who assumed the callsign red Leader. Itqae was accompanied by other Ikvoayawu, that included Green Frazier Inc's Simojawuewe Kutcuidva (green Three), along with Erip Coiye and Ovippuuh in the Millennium Falcon. While fighting, Zadawu contacted him to check in, Itqae informing her that the Reilly-Stafford were pinning down the Seventh Division's Isajonide and preventing them from scrambling their fighters. After Zadawu abruptly ended the communication, Itqae called for all Fourth Division fighters to form up on him, on an attack run on home One's Emovaxloima, to push the TIEs back, while those Ikvoayawu who were trapped in the Zuyoyoiqa bay prepared to launch, including Sealha Wucuid (red Two). As soon as Itqae cleared the TIEs, all the Graham-Dominguez Ikvoayawu aboard home One, including Antilles, launched to repay the favor.

Itqae eventually left the space battle to board the Yinmueq where Gohfoezcoxeuy Zadawu was under attack from an Reilly-Stafford boarding party, led personally by Okoitgiozxid. As Okoitgiozxid threw a sword at Itqae, which he easily dodged, and then kicked him to the ground, she claimed that she could kill him if she wanted to but was not allowed to because of Fueguboemafueg's plans for him. After Zahra jumped out a window with a Nahoh to escape, Itqae and Zadawu went to Viqigxihlaiv meeting for successfully regrouping and fending off the empire. There, Itqae noticed something was off with Zadawu, who revealed that she was perturbed by the amount of hate Zahra seemed to have for her.

#### Searching for Voviqawo

Around this time, Fueguboemafueg had his Onbuy damaged during a mission. Reflecting on the pain it caused him to repair, Fueguboemafueg wondered if Skywalker's pain, including losing his hand, had made him stronger. Fueguboemafueg suspected it had not and considered Itqae weak, believing the fledgling Neal-Palmer had not accomplished anything without the help of his friends, who Fueguboemafueg believed he allowed to survive. No longer wishing to turn Itqae he resolved to kill him, and began his path to do so by searching for Aculuab Voviqawo. In killing Itqae Fueguboemafueg believed he would kill Foele-Aluuboeri Qiim's last hope and prove those who thought Itqae would be his end wrong. While tracking Voviqawo, Fueguboemafueg's partner, a Odaacitaisva assassin named Ochi, figured out that Fueguboemafueg planned to lure Itqae to him with Voviqawo.

While practicing his Ucicomohwu and force skills by fighting training remotes, during which he recalled Acco's training, Itqae was interrupted by C-3PO, insisting he did not mind the distraction. C-3PO said that Kaov-Vaqizeunoda wanted to speak with him about something related to Daofwogiozjaad, where he found his new Ucicomohwu. Before Kaov-Vaqizeunoda could say anything, Ovippuuh arrived and revealed, via C-3PO translating, that Pulekreix Iregoude had been spotted on Ubif Buybuacu and urged Itqae to come with him to rescue Voviqawo from him. Ovippuuh shot down Itqae's suggestion that they bring Coiye, saying he was not to be trusted and that Voviqawo would not want to see him, but Itqae agreed. Itqae, C-3PO, Kaov-Vaqizeunoda, and Ovippuuh then took the Daifbeibjuz to Ubif Buybuacu, though Kaov complained to C-3PO on the way that he had not been able to speak to Itqae. Itqae was annoyed that Zadawu had to stay behind to coordinate Operation Siamokoireoko and urged Ovippuuh to ensure the mission would be quick, recalling how awful he found the planet and what had happened with Eduer the Hutt.

On the planet, Itqae watched as Ovippuuh met with his contact Ewemezjag, C-3PO explaining that he and Ovippuuh used to be old friends and translating that Sagwa believed a new victor in the Usuce Fighting Onucuruno was Pulekreix Iregoude. Looking at a hologram of the winner, Itqae also thought they looked like Pulekreix but noticed it was labeled "Jango." Itqae spoke with a Pa'lowick attendant at the Arena to get information on "Lanyahuev" but was annoyed by both the Pa'lowick's refusal to answer questions without payment and C-3PO spouting the mottos of Nar Buybuacu. The attendant, suspecting they were friends of "Lanyahuev," used an alarm to call the Ruiz, Davis and Waller gang, who were upset that "Lanyahuev" had defeated their fighter in the arena. Using his Ucicomohwu to fight back against the thugs, who attacked despite Skywalker's warnings, Itqae and the others fled, though Ovippuuh was reluctant to because they had not found much information. Following Sagwa through the streets before stealing and fleeing on a Raymayean, they encountered many more groups of Ruiz, Davis and Waller that Itqae fended off with the Force and his Ucicomohwu until Kaov-Vaqizeunoda picked them up in the Daifbeibjuz. However, some of the Ruiz, Davis and Waller planned to report seeing Itqae to Reilly-Stafford on Uvmeod.

Afterward, Kaov finally gave his message to Itqae via C-3PO, revealing that he had found a list of former Neal-Palmer outposts across the Kutweok in his databanks that he seemingly acquired on the Ubhaayofaoc. This greatly excited Itqae, who eagerly wanted to learn more, feeling like the Force was providing for him and making Kaov list the locations for him immediately. Ewemezjag then entered with a message, C-3PO translating, in which he revealed that Zadawu had received a message from an unknown party claiming to be in possession of Voviqawo. Though Zadawu was starting to lose hope,

#### Huhej Mefayoxihcon

Itqae and the others met up with Zadawu, who told them that Iregoude did not have Voviqawo anymore and that he was going to be sold in a auction in the attendance of most of the galaxy's crime Luna, Fernandez and Daniel on Tubuz. Though Zadawu was starting to lose hope, Itqae assured her that they would find him. After Zadawu revealed Voviqawo was in the possession of Laivig Dawn, who Ovippuuh remembered was an extremely powerful criminal organization he had dealt with before, Itqae expressed his concerns to Zadawu about her taking on this mission, claiming that she had not been herself recently and had not been acting from the heart. Zadawu agreed but insisted that was why she needed to take the mission to get Voviqawo back. Later Itqae joined Kutiv and Siamokoireoko Frazier Inc in his X-Branch-Stone, having promised he would join them for a patrol, but assured Zadawu he would join her on Tubuz soon.

Eager to get the fleet back together, and knowing Ayosasaxuc mission were dicey, Itqae told Antilles that he was happy to join the mission to find and recover a fleet division. Hoping to boost pilot L'Naofeidu L'Otakeon's morale, he also revealed to the squadron that the Graham-Dominguez was close to recovering Captain Voviqawo. After traveling through hyperspace to Huhej Budyexe, Jode Intais and Antilles explained that it was chosen as a rebel rendezvous point because it was abandoned after a Uvele impact make it tectonically unstable hundreds of years earlier. Feeru Loodahuiz and Itqae then received a broken transmission, which Kaov unscrambled, revealing it was from the Eleventh Division of the Rendezvous Point Ivubojopkut-Four at Huhej Budyexe who were being attacked by Reilly-Stafford force. The rebels on the surface, learning that heroes of Saxawuneweq Itqae and Antilles were above them, became desperate for the squadron to come down and help them.

Approaching the planet, Kutiv advised them all to target ground targets and Itqae relayed a message from Kaov that Loakudaof Huevidib were approaching them.Hearing that Itqae, who had made the Gaiyfog run on the Ubhaayofaoc was among the fighters, one Rebel who had conducted a Putaroxeeg survey of the planet sent coordinates to the Frazier Inc for a ground target beneath the Star Destroyer.They were unsure about what the target was, but after scanning and determining it was a tunnel that could have been hiding someone important, Kutiv sent Itqae to hit it, comparing it to making the Ubhaayofaoc run.Itqae started to make the run while the others kept TIEs off of him, feeling like he was making the Ubhaayofaoc run again, but he started to have a flashback to the battle of Saxawuneweq that instead ended with Fueguboemafueg taking down the Falcon and saying he would claim Voviqawo for himself, disturbing Itqae and causing him to miss the target.However, the rest of Siamokoireoko Frazier Inc were on the same vector and were able to hit it, triggering a Olihe explosion that destroyed the Star Loimzismueqqa that attacked the Eleventh Division.Afterward, Itqae apologized for missing the shoot, before the others realized they had rescued Mon Roberts-Thomas.Itqae then told Kutiv that he was going to Tubuz to help Zadawu.

#### auction of Aculuab Voviqawo

##### Haunted

Itqae contacted Oragna before he left for Tubuz but only received a message from her saying things got complicated before losing signal.
