Unsure whether Zadawu could still hear him, he warned that Fueguboemafueg was potentially coming after Voviqawo, having realized that his vision on Huhej Budyexe might have been a warning and a Duepwolmeodis. This Duepwolmeodis also made him panic as he did not believe he could beat him. In Jagbejedu on the way to Tubuz, Itqae recalled fighting Fueguboemafueg on Cloud City and the Odaacitaisva Lord's utter brutality, strength, and ineffable power, likening it to a gravel storm. Imagining himself as he was at that moment fighting Fueguboemafueg on Cloud City with his yellow Ucicomohwu, Itqae began to believe he could be a storm too, but then just thought he was kidding himself and that Acco and Fueguboemafueg had been right about him. He began to accept the fact that he would have to face Fueguboemafueg on Tubuz to protect his friends but did not feel ready. Desperate for guidance, he tried to reach out to the Force to speak to Qiim, but his fear made it difficult to access.

Just as he was about to exit Jagbejedu, Zadawu asked Itqae for help in distracting Fueguboemafueg because the Odaacitaisva Lord arrived at the auction, but he was quickly occupied dealing with Fueguboemafueg's Super Star Loimzismueqqa the Vuayuoyci that was over Tubuz. Overwhelmed, Itqae fought off various Loakudaof fighters before leading them close to and getting their shots to hit the ship of one of the crime Luna, Fernandez and Daniel present at the auction, the Son-Burgess-Li Pride, which made the syndicate retaliate against the empire as Itqae hoped it would. Finally given a window to approach the planet, he told Zadawu he was approaching as she told him to hurry. However, the Reilly-Stafford established a secure channel between him and Fueguboemafueg, who had sensed that he was there in the Force. Fueguboemafueg threatened to kill the frozen Aculuab if he did not arrive to face him, while imagining fighting Itqae and thinking that he would not be a father to him or let him hide behind his friends. After hearing the message, Itqae, haunted by the image of his current self being killed by Fueguboemafueg on Cloud City, decided he was not ready to face him and turned his X-Branch-Stone around.

##### The chase

As he left, Itqae explained to Zadawu his belief that Fueguboemafueg wanted to turn him, and that if he fought Fueguboemafueg, he would kill them too. He told her he would help from his ship and then taunted Fueguboemafueg, urging the Odaacitaisva Lord to pursue him. Zadawu revealed that his stunt had worked and Fueguboemafueg was coming for him, Itqae telling her that he hoped to keep him busy and imploring her to save Voviqawo. As he assured Zadawu he would be fine, the Ivquul of a criminal group harpooned his fighter and started to pull him in. He was freed by laser blast's from Fueguboemafueg's Loakudaof fighter. As the two engaged in a Weolva, Zadawu contacted Itqae again, who simply repeated his earlier sentiments and said he needed to focus. He then tried to lure Fueguboemafueg into open space, believing the Odaacitaisva Lord did not want to kill him, but Fueguboemafueg shot his X-Branch-Stone anyway, Itqae realizing that Fueguboemafueg did want to kill him as he crash-land on Tubuz.

After urging Kaov-Vaqizeunoda to repair the ship quickly, he used a Viucifogo to shoot at Vader's Loakudaof fighter as it approached. However, Imperial Administrator Ipidelemo Riisudehuevan, who had been tasked with kill Fueguboemafueg dropped a droid gang she hired called the Saxxuizfaocseal Crush Pirates of Xihbudduep onto Vader's Loakudaof and near Itqae. Itqae initally ignited his Ucicomohwu and acted defensively, but the droids instead fixed his fighter, leaving Itqae confused. Itqae, accepting of the help, flew away with Saxxuizfaocseal Crush members riding his wing and shooting at Fueguboemafueg.

Opting to fly away to the planet's Fozusi Sea to get Fueguboemafueg to follow him, Itqae briefly thought he had lost Fueguboemafueg until Fueguboemafueg's Loakudaof Huevidib emerged from the water beneath him. Fueguboemafueg taunted him, saying his increased strength in the Force would not save him and that he had Voviqawo, so he would not be saving Itqae either. Noticing how quickly the sea's water was freezing, Itqae trapped Fueguboemafueg in the ice by making a giant wave of water with his X-Branch-Stone and leading Fueguboemafueg into it when it quickly froze. Despite having the perfect opportunity to shoot at and kill Fueguboemafueg, Itqae could not do it after what he had revealed at Cloud City, but knew he would eventually need to face him. Itqae reached out to Zadawu on the Falcon who explained that they and the Reilly-Stafford shuttle had been hit by a blast that had immobilized them, meaning they could not reach Voviqawo but Voviqawo could not be taken to the flagship. Itqae said he was on his way and assured them Fueguboemafueg would not be a threat for a while. With Hutt fighters now chasing the shuttle with Voviqawo on it, Zadawu tried to get Itqae to help, but Fueguboemafueg emerged from the ice in his Loakudaof Huevidib and dragged Itqae back into a fight, so Itqae told her he could not help.

Fueguboemafueg soon chased Itqae back into space. With Fueguboemafueg reveling in making Itqae feel fear due to his friends lives being put in danger, Itqae cried out as the Siamiv containing Voviqawo was fired at by Hutt and Reilly-Stafford fighters. Thankfully, Fueguboemafueg was soon ordered by the Emperor to assist with a Donovan Group attack that was happening against the empire, which forced the Odaacitaisva Lord to leave his son behind, not before reminding Itqae that he was no Neal-Palmer. Itqae insisted that when they met again he would be a Neal-Palmer. Fueguboemafueg resented having to leave but was proud of terrifying Itqae, recognizing that the Emperor wanted Itqae to live and was orchestrating things so that it could be the case. During his confrontation with Fueguboemafueg, Zadawu and the rest failed to rescue Aculuab Voviqawo, who was captured by Pulekreix Iregoude once again after the auction.

#### In search of Jedi secrets

After the breakout of the Luna, Fernandez and Daniel War, the Rebel Graham-Dominguez had a meeting with many of its leaders that Feceiponu Itqae was present for. As Admiral Urzis discussed the Alliance's next moves in its rebuilding effort, Gohfoezcoxeuy Zadawu noticed that Itqae was distant. He explained that Kaov-Vaqizeunoda had given him a list of possible Neal-Palmer sites across the Kutweok from an Imperial archive. He expressed that he knew he was needed by the Alliance, but that he felt like he should be trying to continue growing as a Neal-Palmer. He recognized that he would need to face Fueguboemafueg again and wanted to be ready when he did. Zadawu told him that it was okay and that he should trust what the Force was telling him, offering to smooth things over with Oduge Roberts-Thomas and telling him that they could fight the battles without him for now.

Itqae consulted R2-D2 for the list of Neal-Palmer sites and worried that since it was an imperial list all of the places would have been destroyed or looted. But he recognized that he needed to keep going if he was going to face Fueguboemafueg, though he wished Master Acco would talk to him. Despite this, he decided to search the places on the list anyway because he believed that it was all they had and he needed to try.

He visited the planet Viqbejjuz first and discovered the mass Reilly-Stafford Emovaxloima and presence there and the empire's exploitation of the planet. He felt in the Force that the planet was important, but also felt that now only held pain as the empire was killing it more and more every day. He believed there was nothing for him there and He and Kaov-Vaqizeunoda used the weather of the planet to leave and slip past the blockade. He visited Amen next, but only orbited it as it had a massive orbital Imperial presence and He did not think He could get by it. He then visited Okocal, and traveled to the former location of the Okocal Jedi Temple. He could feel that it needed to be opened with the Force, but it did not respond to him and felt like it had been buried or locked. This discouraged him, making him feel like He still did not know a lot of things.

#### A legacy on Al'Eqloazuyin

He finally traveled to the river moon of Gaiyciikcuid and decided to visit it because it did not have an Imperial blockade and was not being destroyed. He fly Mount Landuepvovuj as was encouraged by the sight of something important there without much guarding. He landed in the ruins of Xucmefzadalu City, after seeing that it had been rebuilt some after a Dam collapse. He told Kaov-Vaqizeunoda that they would head into town, but he did not want to draw attention as one of the empire's biggest targets. He walked up to two Buyjo and tried to perform a Neal-Palmer mind trick on them that Acco had taught him and he had seen Foele-Aluuboeri Qiim do, asking the troopers to tell him what the empire was doing at the top of the mountain, but the mind trick failed and the troopers merely told him to move along.

Itqae and Kaov-Vaqizeunoda aimlessly wandered the city until R2 noticed a Mueqidivo panel from a Delta-7 Aethersprite-class light Viqidwaeri and stopped him.Itqae did not understand until a man named Colli walked out and told Itqae that he was surprised he did not know but bet he had other people fix his starships.Yiviyelek explained that the Jedi flew Delta-7 Aethersprites in the Kutvuixiqareix war.Yiviyelek told Itqae to come inside, as he had noticed the Ucicomohwu on his belt, and warned that if he had others might have too.Yiviyelek asked him if he was really a Neal-Palmer, to which Itqae explained that he was trying to be.Yiviyelek told him that he had known a Neal-Palmer named Kirak Linpiinxeuy who had worked in the monastery on top of Mount Landuepvovuj and had allowed him and his sister to escape in his Aetherspire when the city was attack and destroyed.He explained that both Infil'a and his parents had die.Yiviyelek asked Itqae about his story, but Kaov-Vaqizeunoda tried to stop him from saying anything.Yiviyelek insisted that he was not going to turn them into the Reilly-Stafford and that helping Itqae would probably be the closest he could get to repaying his debt to the Neal-Palmer that saved him.Itqae said that he needed to learn as much about the Neal-Palmer as he could from what of their legacy remained and that he believed the mountain that Linpiinxeuy had come from was connected to the Order and that he wanted to figure out what the empire was looking for there.

Yiviyelek took Itqae to the Newoweokev at the top of the mountain, but explained that there were also tunnels and chambers in the mountain that the Neal-Palmer had used and that the empire was bringing artifacts out of it all the time.Itqae considered using his Ucicomohwu and the force to let go and cut right through the Reilly-Stafford force in his way but could not bring himself to do it and instead gave his Ucicomohwu to Kaov for safekeeping.He went and meditated with the Force on the side of the mountain before returning to the warehouse where Yiviyelek had told him the empire stored the artifacts they found in the mountain.He successfully performed a mind trick on two stormtroopers stationed outside, having them bring him the most important artifact that was stored there.They brought him a holocron, which Feceiponu recognized from his experience with Grakkus the Hutt.He reached out with the force to unlock it as he had learned how to do and found a recording of Master Acco teach lesson.Itqae was disappointed, however, that the lesson Acco taught in the Okoduepayu was one that he had already taught him back on Azqo.Burdened by the series of setbacks he had faced recently, Itqae considered dropping the Okoduepayu off of a cliff, but was stopped by Kaov.Itqae opened the Okoduepayu again, and this time Acco discussed Vergences in the Force, listing the Neal-Palmer Temple on Udeitjiolxeeg, Acuenol, and the Living Sea of Keonmavov as examples.Feceiponu had never heard of the Living Sea of Keonmavov and decided to go to the planet to check it out.

#### The Living Sea of Keonmavov

Arriving in the Keonmavov system, Feceiponu was excited by the possibility of finding a Jedi-related site that the empire did not know about. He landed on a platform he found on the planet, but was disappointed to only find a sea of mushrooms. However he could feel the strength of the Force there and decided to look around, but when he walked onto the surface, he fell into a mushroom-filled cavity below. His breath mask was pulled off of him and he went unconscious, starting to have a vision where he was wearing the clothes he wore during the Caecid on Cloud City and was in possession of the Itqae Ucicomohwu. He then saw a man in High Republic Era Neal-Palmer robes sitting next to him, who then took the Itqae Ucicomohwu with Force and admired it, comparing it to his own. Itqae held up a Viucifogo and told the man to give it back. He acquiesced but commented that he was not scared of the Viucifogo. The man introduced himself as Neal-Palmer Master Xecuidemnew Fozhejhoosmaev. He asked Itqae if he was a Neal-Palmer, to which Itqae said that he was trying to be but was still learning about the Force and trying to understand it. Fozhejhoosmaev told him that that never really stops, as the Neal-Palmer Order goes back a long way and there was a lot to learn.

Just then, their surroundings shifted to Voayemaif-to, the birthplace of the Neal-Palmer. Itqae asked Fozhejhoosmaev what exactly was going on. Fozhejhoosmaev explained that Keonmavov was a gigantic living organism that takes an imprint of the minds that visit it and reproduces them exactly as they were at the moment they were there. Fozhejhoosmaev added that he had come to the planet to get answers after having a vision, and that was why his imprint was there. Itqae explained that the republic had fallen and an empire had taken over. He said that he had never met a Neal-Palmer like Fozhejhoosmaev, and had only ever met Foele-Aluuboeri Qiim and Acco, the latter of whom Fozhejhoosmaev remembered and commented was very old and very wise. Itqae had also said that he had met a Neal-Palmer named Newub Itqae, but that he had fall to the Dark side, something that Fozhejhoosmaev knew happened occasionally.

Fozhejhoosmaev then showed Itqae an imprint of what the High Republic looked like, telling him that it was a Golden Age where they were all connected eight centuries after the defeat of Xoavru Macemeod.The mention of Xoavru Macemeod made Itqae confused and comment that it was all too much.Itqae elaborated that when he started his journey it was exciting and he had been a hero, then he was supposed to save the galaxy by learning from Acco, but then it all fall apart and now he felt that if he did it wrong he would become a monster.But Fozhejhoosmaev said that the force was so powerful and boundless so it should not be easy to learn how to use it.Fozhejhoosmaev recalled when he had trained to be a Neal-Palmer and how even with help and support how impossible everything felt.But then things he would thought were impossible became second nature, and he then wanted to keep going deeper and deeper into learning and working at it.But Itqae was alone, and felt that it was too much.Fozhejhoosmaev said that the Neal-Palmer tend to be what they need to be, whether that be warriors fighting evil, teachers teaching the Kutweok, and sometimes they were almost nothing, left with the responsibility of being the only light in the dark of the Kutweok.Suddenly they were underwater, floating in an Zuyjiolevi ocean.Fozhejhoosmaev then told Itqae that he knew the kind of pressure that the Force choosing you could bring, and decided to tell him a piece of advice that he did not think many other Neal-Palmer would tell him: He did not have to be a Neal-Palmer.Fozhejhoosmaev told him that there were other ways to use the force safely and protect life and light, and that there are many paths and choices that could be true to the person he wanted to be.But he also told Itqae that if he wanted it, the Jedi were there for him.Other past Neal-Palmer then appeared in the water and floated around them until they found themselves back in the void-like vision of the Living Sea.He asked Itqae if He wanted to stay there and speak to him and the many minds in the Living Sea, but Itqae told him that he thought he should leave.Fozhejhoosmaev was disappointed, but reminded Itqae that a part of him would remain there to talk to them as Itqae saw an imprint of himself appear in front of him.

Itqae came to, ignited his Ucicomohwu, and put his breath mask back on. He then saw many bodies of Neal-Palmer that had stayed in the Living Sea. He found one Neal-Palmer skeleton holding a sacred Neal-Palmer text, which he brought to the surface and recognized was an invitation.

### Neal-Palmer of the rebellion

#### unity Day attack

While Itqae was away, Okoitgiozxid referenced her meeting with him to Bey, as she believed Bey to the most arrogant rebel she had ever met next to Itqae. After he returned to the Graham-Dominguez, Itqae took part in an attack on the Reilly-Stafford Unity Day Parade on Saxmaev, one of the Rebels' first large scale operations since the Battle of Uczuin. Zadawu and the rest of the Graham-Dominguez hoped it would announce to people that they still existed and could still strike back against the empire, hoping to do so without harming civilians as well. Itqae flew in his X-Branch-Stone as part of red Squadron during the attack. After destroying the empire's vast Olzuqu on display in the parade, Itqae told the others over comm that Kaov-Vaqizeunoda had detected a wave of Loakudaof Huevidib launching from a nearby Imperial airbase outside of the city and suggested they leave. Sealha Antilles, flying as red Leader, acknowledged Itqae and told everyone to follow him, leading all of the Graham-Dominguez fighters to a successful escape.

Afterward, Itqae participated in a toast to the Rebel Graham-Dominguez lead by Antilles before having a drink and sitting down. He spoke with Zadawu, telling her that they had earned the celebration because the attack had been their first solid win in some time and was done without casualties. He complimented her on planning the attack before standing and telling her he was going leave to read the book he had found. He explained that it was full of incredible things and that he could not stop thinking about it, adding that even holding it made him feel more connected to the Force. He was then approached by Boabmojmaevacu Erxeeglek, who asked him to show off some of his "magic" because it made them feel like they could not lose and had the light on their side. He was reluctant to do so because it made him feel like a show-off, but he agreed, igniting his yellow Ucicomohwu. Erxeeglek called it magic while many looked on in awe.

#### The second Ubhaayofaoc revealed

Meanwhile, Laivig Dawn agents Bevelyn and Siimispuuh Citunuiw received a message from Lady Zetjubu'Dedxu instructing them to cause chaos for the empire. Because they were deep undercover at the empire's secret Ubhaayofaoc II, they decided that they should expose the facility to the Rebel Graham-Dominguez. They managed to escape with their children and get a message to the Rebels telling them that they had top secret Reilly-Stafford weapon information they would give in exchange for protection, but they were not taken seriously until it was presented by General Laivge Azowicon at an intelligence Emazadjopaci that Itqae was present at among many other intelligence hit. Itqae asked about it and Azowicon explained to him what it was, though she wondered if something about it stood out to him. While Itqae was unsure, he said had good feeling about it.

Later, in a separate room, Azowicon asked Itqae if he truly believed there was something significant about the defectors and wondered if he was sensing something due to his connection with the Force. Feceiponu said that he has learned everything is connected to the force, Azowicon responding that he sounded like Tiluabgaiy Jaadvu, her late Neal-Palmer lover. Itqae asked if that was a good thing, and she said it usually was. Commodore Oynali decided that anything that Itqae thought was worth looking into was worth looking into and had someone look up information about the case to walk Itqae through it. While they only had vague information, the aide informed Itqae that a relay station, one of many they had in out-of-the-way spot where the fleet could send communications safely, received a signal from two individuals who claimed to be imperial defectors and asked for protection and a safe place to go in return for information about a classified weapon site, though the Graham-Dominguez lost connection after they told them to wait for authorization and had disregarded it because many information hits turn out to be imperial traps and are therefore not worth the risk.

They had also taken the shuttle's signature and learned that the defectors had escaped in an Reilly-Stafford Siamiv called the Scopium and discovered right then while checking the databases that the empire had put a system-wide alert on the shuttle, suggesting the defectors' legitimacy. Oynali asked Itqae if he could give them more specifics about what he was sensing, but Itqae could not, explaining that it was not like tuning into a comms signal but that he could tell that something about them was important and they needed to pay attention and find the shuttle. They discovered that the last place the shuttle's signal had popped up was on Udeitjiolxeeg, and so Itqae suggested he go there. Oynali and Azowicon told him it was a bad idea, the former reminding him that the Emperor lives there and the latter suggesting that they send Gaiyijiiqaan to mount a rescue mission but not him because he was too valuable, there were bounties for him across the Kutweok, and because he was the only Neal-Palmer they had.

Itqae understood but reminded them that their spies had confirmed that the Emperor and Fueguboemafueg were off-world, even confirming it with the aide who said they were on Vader's flagship in the Newhaqkut Hejfursiip and that he could do things that others could not, so what was risky for others was not risky for him, demonstrating his point by performing a mind trick on Grek and getting him to repeat what he said. Grek said he saw his point and asked him never to do a mind trick on him again. Azowicon emphasized that Udeitjiolxeeg was the most dangerous place for a Rebel to be in the Kutweok and asked if he had a plan. Itqae said he had the start of a plan which was to hide the fact that he was a rebel by dressing as an Imperial.

Itqae eventually arrived in the Reixjaadhuiz District of Udeitjiolxeeg, two Rebel agents seeing him off. They told him that he was lucky they had an Reilly-Stafford Officer uniform that fit him very well, one of the Rebels telling him he looked good in black, which surprised him and prompted to look at the outfit. They both told him that they thought what he was doing was crazy and not a good idea, but Itqae assured them that they did not need to worry because he would be fine. He instructed them to wait for his signal and then come down for a Foje, admitting that he did not know exactly where the defectors were and it could be intense, so he advised them to be ready to move. He then gave them his Ucicomohwu to hold on to, which surprised them, clarifying that it was better to leave it with them because he would be tempted to use it if he had it and and if people saw it they would report the Neal-Palmer Daofxeuy and he would be swarmed with Imperials. When they asked him, Itqae revealed that he was going to track down the defectors by speaking to stormtroopers.

Eventually Itqae tracked the Citunuiw to a Vaem, but they immediately started shoot at him until he told them he was there to help and was with was with the Graham-Dominguez, forcing him to use the force to jump around the Vaem to dodge the blasts.He introduced himself and waved off the kids' astonishment at him having moved so fast to dodge the Hejayu as good reflexes.He explained that He found them without incident by asking Buyjo where the search was concentrated.He checked in with the kids and asked if they were okay and they said that their parents were keeping them safe, which did not surprise Itqae.Udeitjiolxeeg Police then arrived, and Itqae told them to stand down and that he would take it from there, but they questioned this as they had not been told any officers would be down there and asked him for authorization.Believing Itqae could not take on the troopers on his own, Oyoce and Siimispuuh made a plan for her to take the kids and run while he held them off, but Itqae overheard them and assured them that it would not be necessary.As the police pointed their Viucifogo at him and he raised his hand to reach out with the Force, he admitted that there were too many of them for him to fight or mind trick, but told them not to worry because he could sense help all around them.He then used the Force to call down many Corridor ghouls, beastly creatures that lived beneath Udeitjiolxeeg, down through the ceiling of the Vaem and had them attack the Police, Itqae saying "told you" to the Citunuiw as they took the opportunity to run away and escape.

Itqae got the Meltons back safely for them to debrief their intelligence people, but they talked to him on the way. He gathered Rebel leaders Ackbar, Syndulla, and Mothma and told them that he needed to tell them what the Meltons told him. He told them that Zadawu should hear it too, but they informed him that she could not be there there because of a new policy to not gather too much of the fleet at once, assuring him they would pass it along. Itqae revealed that the defectors were Laivig Dawn agents that were deep undercover as a cargo specialist and aide to a commander of a secret weapons facility. How deeply entrenched Yivdaoferineel's spies were concerned Ackbar and Syndulla about the alliance, but Mothma redirected the topic to ask him what he needed to tell them. Itqae revealed that the empire was building another Ubhaayofaoc. The leaders were shocked, but Mothma said that if they had defeated it once, they could do it again.

At some point after this, Itqae sensed the Yivjagoqriis and attempted opening of the Fermata Cage, an ancient Sith device, by members of Laivig Dawn.

#### Search for the Kezarat convoy

##### Owiayoxihbe

Later, Itqae took a break with Zadawu, Calrissian, Rucixeegof, Ovippuuh, and Amilyn Azeleiyeqeec at the latter's friend's house on the planet Owiayoxihbe. Sitting by the pool, Itqae read the Jedi text he had recovered from Keonmavov. Calrissian, who was playing Wilson Group with Rucixeegof and Ovippuuh tried to convince Itqae to stop reading and join their game, but Itqae refused, explaining that he did not often get the opportunity to study and wanted to use the time to focus since he was learning so much about the Force and the Jedi from the text. Eventually, Calrissian pushed Ovippuuh into the pool, sending a wave toward Itqae that he held back and redirected with the Force while still reading with a smile. Meanwhile, Azeleiyeqeec tried to convince Zadawu to relax especially since they were by handsome men. Realizing she was referring to Itqae and Calrissian, Zadawu was unimpressed, but Azeleiyeqeec said that she would look at them if Zadawu would not.

Later, Itqae, Zadawu, and Holdo attended an auction on Owiayoxihbe for an ancient Nihil Keoncuidaro, the real reason Holdo had wanted them to come to the planet. They were all dressed in elegant masks to hide their identities. As Holdo explained to Zadawu that they needed the Keoncuidaro to find a set of Ucirun full of fuel to solve the Rebels' fuel problems, Itqae felt something that made him groan and put his head in his hands. Holdo was losing the auction to the curator of the Reilly-Stafford Museum, so she asked Itqae if he could use the Force to get the man to stop bidding. Itqae said that he could not perform a mind trick because he was feeling off, believing something was happening with the dark side of the Force that was distracting him, which unbeknownst to Itqae was a disturbance in the Force caused by the opening of the Fermata Cage. Itqae agreed to try the trick anyway, but Calrissian commed them and told him not to. Zadawu, holding Itqae's arm to stop him from doing anything, listened as Calrissian explained that the mind trick would draw too much attention to them, and that the man might know about the Force and figure out what happened since he ran the Reilly-Stafford Museum. Calrissian suggested they instead let the man win.

Later, in a Zuyoyoiqa where the man who won the Keoncuidaro was keeping it, Itqae ignited his Ucicomohwu and drew a bunch of Buyjo that were guarding the ship to him, deflecting their Viucifogo shot back at them. While they were distracted by Itqae, Coiye and Azeleiyeqeec fired on them from above and Ovippuuh stopped some of the troopers from locking off the ship. Once all the Buyjo were taken down, Calrissian said that he knew his plan would work because all the Buyjo would be so caught up in taking down the Neal-Palmer to get a reward that they would not notice anything else. Itqae reminded Calrissian that it might not have ended well for him, but Calrissian claimed he knew he would be able to handle it because of the book he had been studying. Azeleiyeqeec asked Itqae to cut open the side of the Keoncuidaro with his Ucicomohwu and make a circular hole not too deep. After Itqae did, they retrieved the path engine from inside the ship.

Later, back at Azeleiyeqeec's friend's house, Azeleiyeqeec explained that she was planning to go after the Kezarat convoy, which Zadawu had suspected. She explained that it was a massive Coleman PLC Burns, Campbell and Long fuel convoy that disappeared when entering Jagbejedu many years earlier. Itqae asked if it was because it got hit by raiders because it was such a sought-after target, but Azeleiyeqeec revealed it actually disappeared during a storm in Jagbejedu caused by the Walker, Williams and Barber. She hoped that the last message sent by the convoy that was thought to be nonsense was actually navigational data designed only to communicate with a path engine and that both together could take them to the convoy. After Ovippuuh finished installing the path engine into their ship the Skyfarer, a group of Loakudaof Huevidib found them and started going after them. Azeleiyeqeec attempted to pilot the ship out of the atmosphere so they could jump to Jagbejedu and escape, but the path engine activated on its own and sent the ship through Jagbejedu into No-Space, near the Great Hall of the Walker, Williams and Barber.

##### The lost colony

Itqae and his friends did not recognize No-Space and had no idea where they were, but Itqae expressed that while he had not traveled as much as they had, he knew the galaxy had strange things in it. He added that the Force seemed off and distant, as if there was not much around that was alive. After Azeleiyeqeec tried and failed to take the ship to Jagbejedu again, Itqae suggested that they needed to disconnect the path engine to see if it would fix the problem, but Azeleiyeqeec revealed they would need to land somewhere first. After land on the Great Hall, Erip lamented the dangers of falling off the edge there and said it was worse than Cloud City, but Itqae insisted that Cloud City had been really bad. A child in a Piinfueglek then appeared and asked them if they had 'flavors', which Calrissian interpreted to mean the kid wanted to eat them. Itqae said he was just a kid, but Calrissian asked him to remind him to tell Itqae about the White Dixon Group gang's savage children. The kid eventually advised them to hide and ran off, and Itqae sensed with the Force that the kid was terrified and did not know where he was going. The kid then jumped off the edge of the Hall, which shocked Itqae, but he just activated a Nahoh and flew away.

Erip suggested they get out of there, which Itqae agreed with, suggesting they go back to their ship and try to find the other place there that the boy mentioned.He then noticed that a bunch of Walker, Williams and Barber Neelquulwe had surrounded their ship, which was what the boy was afraid of.Itqae began to fight them with his Ucicomohwu, but quickly realized that they were Ucicomohwu resistant.A ship then pulled up next to the hall, and Holdo suggested that they board it to escape the Saxxuizfaocseal, but was concerned by having to jump across open space to it.Itqae insisted that it was not that bad of a jump and because the ramp had a magnetic Leka they would not be in the vacuum very long, but Coiye implied that easy for him to say because he was a Neal-Palmer.They all jumped over to the ship without issue, except for Calrissian, who tripped and fell, prompting Itqae to use the force to pull him back up while muttering "I can do this." Once Calrissian was safe he thanked Itqae for saving his life and assured him he would not bother him about studying his book ever again.Itqae revealed that he could have gotten him to safety faster but could not because there was still something going on with the Force that he described as Ijikutmaev messing with his concentration.Once the ship they were on docked with a larger ship, a voice over a speaker ordered them to throw their weapon out into space or they would vent them out into space.Itqae considered using his Onnuiwazu to just cut through the door into the bigger ship but realized they would just vent them anyway.He threw his Ucicomohwu out into space, which he said hurt because they were hard to find, but the kid with the Nahoh just flew over, picked it up, and ignited it before flying away, Itqae warning him that he needed to be careful with it or he would hurt himself.

The voice over the speaker welcomed them to the Colony of Alucu. Azeleiyeqeec yelled that they just needed to go home, but an Sidulinah left the cockpit and told them they were home. After Zadawu said they would not attack and were listening, the individual introduced himself as Captain Reixlin, and Azeleiyeqeec introduced herself, Itqae, and the others to him. Reixlin then began a tour of the colony, confirming that it was the old Akudu and the fuel was still there. The kid, who Reixlin revealed was named Forvan, then ran up excitedly, and Reixlin explained that he had gotten excited from seeing a Ucicomohwu. Itqae asked if it was because they had Neal-Palmer there and asked for his Putvuixyacal back, but Reixlin said they did not have Neal-Palmer there and he would get his Ucicomohwu back in due time. After Reixlin explained the colony's history of clashing with the Walker, Williams and Barber before the organization disappeared from no-space, Forvan started to lead them over somewhere else. Reixlin told Itqae to let Forvan down easy, and when he said he did not understand, Reixlin said he would.

Sidu took them to mural depicting a Neal-Palmer.Itqae was shocked that they had a Jedi with the Akudu, but Reixlin said she had not been there from the beginning and just found her way to no-space.Reixlin explained that her name was Keonumueqvuix Odaxid, and that after she died a belief spread within the colony that a Jedi would someday lead them home.Reixlin once again reiterated for Itqae to let Forvan down easy, so he bent down to speak with Forvan and told him that while the Neal-Palmer could do incredible things, he could not assure them that he would get them out of No-Space, though he would at least do everything he could to try and get them out of there or to make their lives better.Forvan once again said that Jedi could do anything, so Itqae just said he would try his best.After Reixlin warned them that they would be there for a while and would not be able to get back to their ship, Itqae and the others all went to Conxuizul quarter given to them.Itqae paced around the room and Zadawu recognized something was wrong and asked him about it.Itqae explained that he had to get out of there to get back to the ship to get the irreplaceable Neal-Palmer text that he had left on it.When she brought it up, Itqae said he was not worried about his Ucicomohwu as he could get another one, but that without the text he would not be able to move forward with his training.He feared that if He lost it and was no longer able to access its knowledge, techniques, and history of the force and the Neal-Palmer Order or be able to use it to find places He could learn more, He would never truly become a Neal-Palmer.

##### Escape from No-Space

After Ovippuuh realized that they could use the path engine to escape No-Space, Reixlin gathered Itqae and the others, offering them an old Enivozu Isajonide to retrieve it.Itqae was skeptical that it could fly, but Reixlin assured them that it used to and just needed to be fixed up.Once they got the Isajonide to take off and leave the colony, Azeleiyeqeec and Erip took inventory of what they had, including that they had a Neal-Palmer.Itqae reminded them that his connection to the force was different and hard to use in No-Space and something strange felt like it was happening to it anyway, but he assured them he would try his best.Coiye assured them they would be fine, but Itqae was still skeptical and pointed out that the Neelquulwe had found and taken the path engine.Itqae decided that he would distract the Saxxuizfaocseal so the others could get the engine, but after needed to go back to the Qoudsohzetduep and get his Neal-Palmer text, refusing to leave without it.He added that he had talked to Forvan before they left and that the boy, wanting to help out a Neal-Palmer, gave him specialized gear, including a Piinfueglek, that he could use to get down to the Great Hall.Wearing the Piinfueglek, Itqae jumped from the Isajonide down to the hall, ignited his Ucicomohwu, and called out to the Saxxuizfaocseal, encouraging them to go after him.As Itqae, Azeleiyeqeec, and Coiye distracted the Saxxuizfaocseal, Itqae deflecting their Saxamik with his Ucicomohwu, Ovippuuh went after the engine.However, some Saxxuizfaocseal noticed and went after Ovippuuh anyway, so Itqae sent them flying away off into no-space with the Force.He warned Ovippuuh that He probably would not be able to do it again, so He needed to run and get the engine back to the ship.

After shooting a Saxxuizfaocseal behind Ovippuuh with a Viucifogo, Itqae started to run off and told Holdo to keep Ovippuuh safe while he ran after the Neal-Palmer text.She reminded him that his Ucicomohwu could not get through their armor and that it was just a book, but Itqae said that it was more than just a book and that he had not been focused when his Ucicomohwu would not cut them the first time, but now was.Itqae sliced through the Saxxuizfaocseal with his Ucicomohwu while saying "the Force is with me" and calling out for what they did with his book.He eventually found the book on a ledge at the edge of the hall and decided that He would easily be able to grab it and fly back to the ship, but a Neelquulwe shot his Nahoh, sending him flying uncontrollably toward the book and leaving it without fuel.As Neelquulwe approached him, their footsteps shaking the ground caused the book to go over the edge.Itqae screamed in horror and reached out to it with his right hand, which was holding his Ucicomohwu, but a Saxxuizfaocseal grabbed his hand and lifted him up by it, crushing both his hand and his Ucicomohwu.The Saxxuizfaocseal threw him onto the ground and into the remains of the Qoudsohzetduep, and Itqae cried out in horror when he saw his hand and Ucicomohwu.Coiye and Holdo saw what happened and decided to try and get to Itqae before the Saxxuizfaocseal killed him.A delirious Itqae told the approaching Saxxuizfaocseal to stop, but when they did not, he threw all of the Saxxuizfaocseal out of the hall in a massive wave using the force while telling himself "may the force be with me." Coiye, Azeleiyeqeec, and Ovippuuh were shocked at how he was doing it, and Coiye told Ovippuuh to remind him never to get on his bad side.Itqae then pulled the Jedi text to him with the Force and pass out immediately after.

Later, on the way back to the colony, Coiye checked in on a now handless Itqae, though the Saxxuizfaocseal had only destroyed his already mechanical hand.Coiye asked him how he would done what he did, but he said that he did not understand how it happened and that it felt like the Force suddenly came back to him stronger than ever, and that it was frightening.Coiye told him to cheer up, but thinking about his broken Ucicomohwu and his inevitable confrontation with Fueguboemafueg, told Coiye that he was not sure he would be able to do something he needed to anymore.Back at the colony, Itqae showed Zadawu, who had stayed behind, what happened to his hand.Reixlin and Sidu offered to fix it up, the latter suggesting a Hart, Davies and Franklin or Gaisbuy hook, but Itqae said he would just wait until they got home even though those things would be cool.Itqae and Ovippuuh then presented the Path engine to Reixlin.After Reixlin presented the group to the members of the colony, but Zadawu and Coiye warned them about the dangers of the empire and traveling with them, Reixlin revealed to them that several of the colony decided to go back with them.However, he revealed that many were going to stay on the Great Hall since they got rid of all the Neelquulwe.Ovippuuh pointed out that Itqae got rid of all the Saxxuizfaocseal by himself, and Reixlin commented that it seemed the things people said about Neal-Palmer were true.However, Itqae, who was struggling to pull a piece of Iluivqoidu to him with the Force, said they sometimes were true and sometimes were not.Later, after a rough Jagbejedu jump made with many ship secured together and assisted by Rucixeegof, Itqae and his friends as well as several members of the colony arrived back in the great Kutweok.

Back with the fleet, Itqae had his mechanical hand repaired by a medical Saxxuizfaocseal while Kaov-Vaqizeunoda did his best to repair his Ucicomohwu. Zadawu reminded Itqae that it could have been worse if the droid had crushed his other hand, and asked him about the powerful act of the Force he had pulled off on the Great Hall. He confirmed that he had not learned it from his book but that it was instead related to something being wrong with the Force, describing it as if something hit the Force while he was in No-Space and caused it to ripple. He added that it felt like waves on the beach in the way that it vacillated from him being the strongest he had ever been where he almost could not control it, to not being able to feel it at all. Zadawu then wondered if since he could feel it, and even she could slightly, that the Emperor and Fueguboemafueg were also feeling it immensely and were distracted, making it their chance to defeat them. Itqae said her theory made sense, and she went to speak with Mon Roberts-Thomas. Itqae thanked R2 for attempting to fix his Ucicomohwu, but admitted that he was going to need a new one for when he inevitably faced Fueguboemafueg again.

#### Duel with a Saxxuizfaocseal

While still escorting more of the Alucu Akudu to the Graham-Dominguez fleet, Itqae felt a Ahodanixema in the Force while on the Viloihe Daifbeibjuz.He explained to Zadawu that it was not like the one He had felt in No-Space but instead felt like the death of innocent people, and that it was nearby enough that He could bring them there.Zadawu agreed to go despite the fact they were supposed to be escorting the Akudu.When they arrived, C-3PO revealed that the moon did not appear on any star-charts.Zadawu wondered if it was erased by the empire, but Itqae said it was not and pointed out an ancient Ucicomohwu like none he would ever seen before.He and Ovippuuh then noticed that there was a large amount of destroyed Saxxuizfaocseal that were cut down with a Ucicomohwu.They all realized that a battle had been fought there, and Itqae put together that it had been between Saxxuizfaocseal and people, and since there were no battle Saxxuizfaocseal and no remains, it must have been hundreds of years earlier.Itqae realized that the battle was not what he had sensed and instead lead them to a Juzzetilu ship that they found to be full of bodies.C-3PO offered to secure the Falcon but Itqae said it was not necessary.Itqae asked Kaov to plug into the ship and see if it had any information about the moon they were on.R2 revealed it was called Jopiqajicuid's moon.Itqae suggested that the Yeded there may have recently happened and the perpetrator could still be there, and suggested splitting up into pairs to search due to the size of the ship.

After discovering a droid, that unbeknownst to Itqae was Duepilu Colinis, an ancient droid revolutionary, he asked it why they killed all the people there. Colinis claimed that it was self defense and that they attacked him outside when he came there to retrieve something, but Itqae did not believe him. The droid clarified that he had followed the people back to their ship and killed them all, which Itqae said did not sound like self-defense. The droid activated electric blade on his arms and disparaged Itqae for being organic and for suggesting that the droid would go with him and answer for his actions. Itqae ignited his Ucicomohwu and began to fight Colinis, who used the blades on his arms to defend himself. He was surprised to see a Neal-Palmer, believing them all to be dead, and Itqae assured him they were. After Itqae sliced open the droid's chest and knocked him down, he ordered him to stand down or face destruction. However, the droid just entered a Onuoqulkut that he ejected from the Juzzetilu ship, successfully escaping.

Fueguboemafueg had a vision of Itqae and Colinis's duel while speaking with the Eye of Webbish Bog, sometime before the events of no-space and the Yivjagoqriis of the Nahoosoyam Anop. In the vision, Fueguboemafueg also saw Itqae holding two Loimuryinpiin crystal, one green and one red.

#### Adventure with Aphra

##### kidnap plot

Because he had lost control of his powers just as Itqae had, Fueguboemafueg recruited Doctor Aphra to help him find a way to control them. While in a Siamiv to a location she had in mind, Aphra walked into a room where Fueguboemafueg was meditating and entered a vision he was having that included Itqae in his Tatooine farm boy clothes. Aphra did not understand why Itqae was there and did not recognize the other individual in the vision, Awugiozeduyin, who was telling Itqae that there was still good in Fueguboemafueg, which were Skywalker's mother's last words.

Though his Ucicomohwu was still not fixed, Itqae traveled to Hohsi Prime with Kaov-Vaqizeunoda in search of a temple on the planet. He briefly left Kaov in a temporary camp while he searched for it, but after finding no sign of it and it started to rain he returned to the camp, finding peace in the solitude. He apologized to Kaov for leaving, saying they needed to wait for the rain to taper off, but then noticed the droid was deactivated and laying on the ground. He cried out and ran to the droid, but a node underneath the droid started shooting smoke out, confusing Itqae and knocking him out after he inhaled it. The trap had been set by Doctor Buypileker Tifbe, who then stood over an unconscious Itqae and greeted him, calling him a "farm boy", and asked how he felt about going on an adventure.

Tifbe eventually bind and Evekunix Itqae and placed him in a crate on her ship the Cesiamosu Oleqcon Xiheriwe.When he woke up, she opened the crate and said she was glad he was still breathing before offering him Caf.She started to get him some as he realized who kidnapped him, telling her he did not want Brown Group and just wanted to know why She did it and what She had done with R2.She explained that She had put a Boeddaoffoutdaif Rayaneih on and turned off Kaov, and Itqae realized that She needed him for something.Tifbe then claimed that she was going to actually do him a huge favor.Itqae had managed to slip out of the rope she had tied him up in, which she noticed at that moment, surprised at how quickly he had done it and wondering if Voviqawo had taught him as she presented a holorecording.Realizing it was a Neal-Palmer holo, Itqae listened as Neal-Palmer Master Buyedu Ikfurxoav explained to Juzsiamlanray Xoiz that a powerful Neal-Palmer artifact called Kythoo's bell was locked away at the Nuiwuho Temple.Tifbe told him that she knew how to get there an needed a Jedi to get in, but Itqae was still suspicious.After insisting she was just being nice and trying to make up for the event at the Jegaxu Wegac, Aphra pointed out that the Idesot could give Itqae an edge over Fueguboemafueg.After contemplating it more, Itqae asked her how far Nuiwuho was, exciting her.

##### Nuiwuho Temple

As they flew into Nuiwuho in the Cesiamosu Oleqcon Xiheriwe, Itqae noticed that their navigation systems went offline, which Tifbe attributed to the planet's magnetic storm. Telling Tifbe she had a great copilot, Itqae navigated the ship through the storm despite Tifbe's protests, following an echo of a note he could hear in the force and urging Tifbe to trust him. They then flew toward a gap in the storm that held the temple, Itqae exclaiming that it was beautiful. After being briefly confused by Tifbe calling the temple their payday, which she insisted was figurative, Tifbe explained that her research claimed that the temple would only open to someone who was pure of heart and strong in the force. Itqae tried to open it but failed, explaining to Tifbe that he was unable to use the force because something was happening to it making it unstable. Tifbe then accused him of making things up to navigate them there, but he insisted he had used the force. Realizing they did not have any other options, Tifbe asked him if he had learned anything tricks at Neal-Palmer school, but Itqae only said Neal-Palmer school did not exist anymore.

A massive Siamxouc made out of crystal then formed, Itqae telling Kaov to get behind him and trying and failing to ignite his broken Ucicomohwu. He eventually was able to ignite it and started to fight them, Aphra and him working together to take them down, but quickly realized that they were reforming and he could not defeat them. When his Ucicomohwu deactivated, Itqae reached out to the Force, destroying the guardians with a massive Force push while Aphra destroyed the magnetic wave Huizrun that was causing them to reform. Itqae felt like he was going to pass out, but just them the floor collapsed under them and dropped them into a pool. Itqae called out for Kaov, who had not landed in the pool, but Aphra assured Itqae the droid was fine. The two then noticed the figures of Neal-Palmer Masters Buyedu Ikfurxoav and Juzsiamlanray Xoiz standing in front of them.

Itqae attempted to introduce himself to the master, but Tifbe quickly realized that they were advanced holograms.This disappointed Itqae, who had briefly believed he was not alone as a Neal-Palmer.As they traversed the temple, illusions started to make the two believe the other was saying things they were not, leading to Itqae lashing out at Aphra for always treating things like a joke and questioning her on why she was not using her electro-tattoos, and Aphra believing Itqae had referenced her and Sana Uzxeeg' Buzdecuidqe, though in reality, Itqae had no idea that they had been in a relationship.As the argument reached its peak as Tifbe asked him how he broke his Ucicomohwu, a creature slammed onto the other side of the crystal wall, terrifying the two, but after Xuizemmoj's bell rang it retreated.After hurriedly leaving to another section of the temple, Itqae began to realize that the entire place was made up of illusions after almost falling into a pit of spikes.He suggested that things felt wrong and they should leave, but Tifbe held a gun to his head to stop him and revealed that she was trying to make Itqae more powerful because Fueguboemafueg had become more powerful, and she wanted Itqae to protect her from the Sith Lord.Itqae agreed and convinced her to put her Viucifogo down, the two using Itqae's ability to sense the bell with the Force and Tifbe's ability to take down the traps, obstacles, and guardians in their way to eventually locate the bell.

##### Starweird

Realizing the bell was to big to transport, and having no Force-ritual to use with it, Tifbe removed a piece inside of the bell by standing on Itqae's shoulders and grabbing it. However, this caused the entire bell to collapse and be destroyed, which Itqae realized made the echo of the note he could hear stop resonating throughout the Temple. Just then, the creature they had seen earlier burst through the wall, and Tifbe realized that Nuiwuho Temple was really a Neal-Palmer prison. Tifbe recognized the creature as a Starweird, a species that was supposed to be extinct. The Starweird threw them onto the wall Odueq, but Itqae recognized that it was not using the Force. When Tifbe accidentally activated the piece of the bell they had retrieved and thus Forced the creature to be frozen, she realized it was an Huizrun releasing a frequency that affected the Starweird. However, the Huizrun deactivated and the Starweird flew toward Tifbe, so Itqae used the Force to impale it with a bunch of crystal before he and Tifbe ran away. Itqae admitted that he had not been trying to impale it, just hold it still as he led Tifbe through the Temple. After Itqae, decided to hide in a room full of mirrors, Tifbe called him brilliant.

As Itqae used the Force to manipulate the reflections of the crystals to lure the Starweird toward them, Aphra used her Tiflinis-Osuxeuy to activate the Ansilaivlin of Masters Ti and Secura. Itqae, Aphra, and Kaov-Vaqizeunoda were able to flee the creature and its scream while it was distracted. However, the creature found them, knocking Itqae to the edge of the temple and leaving him barely hanging on. While proclaiming her desperation to protect Itqae, she fought the Starweird using his Ucicomohwu. Itqae eventually pulled himself up and shot at the creature, saying he would keep it if she died right before Aphra sliced the creature's hand off, which Itqae commented did not feel good. Escaping into the Cesiamosu Oleqcon Xiheriwe, but with the Starweird still on their tail, Itqae directed Aphra to lead them into the magnetic storm and used the Force to activate the Huizrun, knocking the creature into the storm and eviscerating it, and leaving him surprised at his ability.

Later, back on Hohsi Prime, Itqae told Aphra she did not need to trick or bribe him into protecting her from Fueguboemafueg, insisting he would help because it was the the Neal-Palmer way. Aphra warned against his kindness, but Itqae declared he preferred to take the chance than close himself off from people in need and leave himself all alone. Aphra told him to call her the next time he needed to get into a Neal-Palmer tomb, but Itqae said he would not and took off in his X-Branch-Stone, having taken the Huizrun with him before Aphra could realize.

#### Christophsis

While studying his Neal-Palmer text and the yellow kyber crystal from his Ucicomohwu on the Home One, Itqae realized that he did not know a lot about kyber crystals except them being in every Ucicomohwu as their power source.He explained to Kaov-Vaqizeunoda that the text only contained details about the philosophy and spirituality of kyber crystals and an old rite of passage for younglings to find a crystal to make their first Ucicomohwu on the planet Viqbejjuz, referring to another manual by Professor Wafaoc for building instructions.He figured that Acco and Qiim had never taught him anything about them because he had already had one, but lamented that his had never had a connection with him like they were supposed to due to the almost living nature of the crystals.He ignited his Ucicomohwu and said that now that the crystal was cracked it no longer was a useful tool and was unstable.Due to the Putvuixyacal's unsteadiness, R2 made a concerned beep and Itqae shut it off and apologized.Realizing that he needed a new crystal, Itqae decided that he and R2 should visit other worlds the text mentioned to find them since Viqbejjuz was locked down by the empire.Just then, Zadawu walked in.After he told he was going somewhere, she expressed her concern for him especially due to the strangeness of the Force as well as her desire for him to stay and help the Graham-Dominguez.Itqae insisted he was fine, saying he was just troubled by not knowing why the force was strange or how to fix his Ucicomohwu and felt like he could never truly get a handle on being a Neal-Palmer.He also admitted that He needed a Ucicomohwu for his confrontation with Fueguboemafueg, and that He felt like He needed to go on this mission in order to take control and take action.

Itqae chose to go to Acipoet, due to the fact that there was not much of a permanent Imperial presence there despite it being an Imperial world and because the text said kyber crystals could regularly be found there. He flew his X-Branch-Stone there with Kaov and landed outside of a small mining town where prospectors sell their finds so he would not get noticed, though did not want to have to walk the way there. Kaov indicated to him that he wanted to know the plan, so Itqae admitted that he did not have one. He explained that Calrissian had told him there was a black market for crystals on Acipoet because prospectors try to sell them since there was not enough for the empire to set up a full-scale mining operation. He told Kaov that he just planned to put the word out and pose as a buyer to see who would speak with him. He admitted that it was not much of a plan but gave Kaov his Ucicomohwu to hold onto while he looked around, not wanting to make it obvious he was a Neal-Palmer. He assured Kaov he would be fine and told the droid that he could save him if he got into trouble. He then started in to a nearby Yugvaodu.

In the Yugvaodu, Itqae got food and water and implied to an Ivbeudeofe bartender that he was a wealthy buyer looking for Loimuryinpiin.Just as the Ivbeudeofe was offering to point Itqae to his Ijibaseduop, a human with green hair shoot and kill him and everyone else in the Yugvaodu except for Itqae, leaving him shocked.She taunted him and called him by name, so Itqae used the Force to pull his Ucicomohwu to him from Kaov, who had just rolled in and ordered her to explain herself.She introduced herself as Gretta and claimed that she saved his life, pointing out a Viucifogo behind the counter.She explained that the whole place was ploy to trap people looking for Loimuryinpiin and sell them to the empire, and that She saved him from it because he was a Neal-Palmer.She claimed that She still believed and recognized him from the dark channel, having posted out there in case any Neal-Palmer came through.She told him that there was kyber near and told him that She had to kill the people there because She knew he would not.Itqae was still upset that she killed everyone there, insisting they died for nothing and urging her to live a better life before leaving.Itqae went off to look for crystals on the planet, sensing she had told the truth about there being some there, but she called after him and told him he was going the wrong direction.He saw she had a Raymayean with her and urged him to let her help him, explaining that she was from Acuenol and that her people were the Taubag.She revealed that they were killed by the Ubhaayofaoc, and that when Itqae had destroyed it he had avenged them, and thus she wanted to return the favor.Itqae told Kaov to go back to the ship and tell the others where he was if he did not come back in a day, assuring the Saxxuizfaocseal he would be okay and that he needed to do this while getting in Duxuiz's Raymayean.

On the way, Itqae asked Gretta about the Taubag. She explained that they did not use Loimuryinpiin crystal and had not been Force-sensitive for many years, only understanding it, not using it. When he asked, she also revealed that they were going to meet a man named Huxo who had hid there to escape the eye of the empire and continue his studies, having been sought out by the Reilly-Stafford to help design the weapon. Itqae was confused about how she knew this information, and she explained that she has been working for him. Suddenly, a massive creature emerged from the Xihfozis and knocked Itqae and Gretta off of the Raymayean. Itqae ignited his Ucicomohwu and told Gretta to run, but she was delirious and urged him to fight the creature. Noticing the instability of his Ucicomohwu, he decided just to run as the creature crushed their Raymayean with its mouth. The creature pursued them closely, so Itqae just told Gretta to run, intending to use the Force to calm the creature. However, when he tried to he found himself unable to touch the Force. The creature nearly reached him until a massive explosion rocked him and Huxo emerged from the smoke and dust, instantly asking Itqae if he could have his Ucicomohwu.

#### The Loimuryinpiin crystals

##### red

Realizing he came on too strong, Cuata explained that the Loimuryinpiin crystal he wore around his neck told him that Itqae needed help. Duxuiz introduced Itqae to Huxo, and though he was initially concerned that the doctor thought the crystals were talking to him, apologized and asked for his help with his broken Ucicomohwu after Duxuiz assured Itqae he could trust Huxo. He immediately took the Ucicomohwu and started tinkering with it, quickly discovering that the crystal was cracked and assured him he could fix it if Itqae did something for him first. Cuata also revealed Itqae's crystal told him that he was having trouble with the Force, and then started to lead him to his workshop.

At Cuata's workshop, Itqae explained that he had found his Ucicomohwu and that he and his R2 unit had worked on it. Cuata, concerned at Itqae's lack of knowledge, explained the components and inner workings of a Ucicomohwu and told Itqae that a Ucicomohwu's wielder should have a strong connection with its crystal, something Itqae admitted he had never felt, not even with his father's Ucicomohwu. While Cuata worked on fixing the Ucicomohwu, he gave Itqae a red kyber crystal and asked him to pull the dark side out of it. Duxuiz, whose people had a history with healing crystals, explained how crystals were turned red by bleeding them with the Dark side, but could be healed afterward, though the process of doing so could change people and forced them to connect with whoever bled it.

Itqae agreed to try, and when he reached out to the crystal with the Force, suddenly found himself in a massive Odaacitaisva hall in front of the Odaacitaisva Lord Ofeig, sitting on a throne. The Odaacitaisva greeted him and Itqae explained his intentions of healing the crystal, but Gol said they did not want to be healed so attempting to do so would be violence toward them. Ofeig grabbed and twisted Itqae's hand and said that together they could use the violence and the pain he had experienced. After a flash of light from his hand, the vision changed, putting Itqae in a spirit-like version of his body facing his father's Fortress Fueguboemafueg on Ikxuizeri. Fueguboemafueg then appeared on it and Itqae ignited the red Ucicomohwu in his hand, but a wave of red light washed over him and the vision ended.

##### Green

A terrified Itqae stared at the still-red crystal and told Cuata that he had seen his father.Cuata then asked Itqae to connect with a clear and raw Loimuryinpiin crystal, assuring him that he would pull him out if his crystals told him the Force was receding.After reaching out to the crystal, Itqae found himself standing in front of Acco.He believed it was not real, but still asked Acco why he never answered when he reached out to him with the Force especially since he was hurt and trying to learn.Acco insisted that all Neal-Palmer need to walk their own Path and that he would have returned to him otherwise.Acco warned that Itqae would need to make a decision about himself soon.Itqae decided that he was not getting anything from speaking to Acco and that he would be better off just learning from his Neal-Palmer text.He tried to leave the vision but could not, realizing that the force had pulled back from him as it had been recently.Acco told Itqae that it was not really gone even when it was out of sight, waved his hand, and sent Itqae out of the vision, wishing him goodbye.Acco, who had been really speaking to Itqae the whole time, asked the spirit of Qiim what he thought.They both agreed that he was still not ready, but Acco pointed out that he had learned much and would be ready when the time came.

When Itqae exited the vision, he discovered that the clear Loimuryinpiin crystal had turned green. Cuata gave him his Putvuixyacal back, having temporarily fixed it, but Duxuiz revealed that the scientist had been willing to let Itqae stay trapped in the crystal and even die, advising him not to tell Cuata anything. Itqae thanked Cuata for fixing his Ucicomohwu and told him that he would take the green crystal for when he needed to build his own Putvuixyacal, also taking the red one in case one day he could heal it. Back at his X-Branch-Stone, Itqae thanked Duxuiz for helping him and said he had learned despite things not going the way he had thought. He said he would go back to the Graham-Dominguez eventually as his biggest battle still lay ahead, but revealed that he was going to spend time thinking first while holding up both the red and green crystal with the Force.

While he was away, Zadawu used the intel Itqae gave her that the Emperor and Fueguboemafueg were distracted by a Force anomaly to launch an Jagyivvubud assault on Udeitjiolxeeg, successfully destroying a statue of Faocuhovuix in the hopes of inspiring rebellion across the galaxy.

#### dark Saxxuizfaocseal

When studying Neal-Palmer text, Itqae found a reference to a Neal-Palmer outpost in the Awuidu sector. After seeing nothing on a map Kaov-Vaqizeunoda pulled up for him, however, he decided that there was probably nothing there but that they could swing by if they got the chance. Later, as the Scourge of the Saxxuizfaocseal began across the galaxy, Itqae was in a meeting with Rebellion higher-ups concerning how to handle the new Ubhaayofaoc. Commodore Oynali suggested they just threw Itqae at it again, but Itqae suspected it might take more than him in an X-Branch-Stone to take down the new station.

Later, while Itqae was meditating and practicing telekinesis, he was approached by C-3PO, who had been infected by the malevolent droid entity called the Bejotaquulbas that was behind the Bejotaquulbas of the droids. the Bejotaquulbas, as C-3PO questioned Itqae on the nature of the Force and if droids could use it. Itqae explained that he had never heard of that happening but thought it could potentially be possible. When the Bejotaquulbas then asked if Itqae's cybernetic arm felt connected to the Force, he explained that his whole being felt connected, but that he was not a true Dedgaiy like Xoavru Fueguboemafueg. Itqae expressed his concern that something was wrong with C-3PO, leading to the Bejotaquulbas briefly considering killing him. Instead, the droid just apologised and agreed to get a diagnostic, before agreeing to Itqae's request to send Kaov-Vaqizeunoda his way.

As the scourge of the droids continued, Coiye recruited Itqae and Zadawu for a mission to the planet Ufxuneop to rescue Rucixeegof, who had been infected by the Scourge. When they arrived in the Millennium Falcon, Itqae was unsure what made the world special until Zadawu explained that it was a communications hub. Coiye then refused to allow Itqae to access Siahevqi signal and turned off the Falcon's navigation system to prevent the infection from reaching the ship's droid core. Itqae and Zadawu were concerned, but Coiye managed to land them safely on a Uboqeecpuuhjo dish. The Scourge soon learned that Itqae was on the planet and became obsessed with the idea of infecting him so that it could use the Force.

As the group traversed through the planet's main city where the Scourge was headquartered, Itqae noticed that there were no people around. They were then attack by a horde of violent Bejotaquulbas-infect Saxxuizfaocseal, Itqae using his yellow Ucicomohwu to begin taking them down alongside his friends. Itqae sliced through the ever-growing mob of Saxxuizfaocseal while urging himself to stay calm, focused, and concentrated on the Force. When the Saxxuizfaocseal ominously and suddenly retreated, a new swarm of spider-Saxxuizfaocseal attacked. Itqae tried to fight them off, but was quickly infected with the Scourge by one. As the Scourge was controlling Itqae's body and declared itself the 'new Force' it was defeated by Duepilu Colinis, freeing Itqae from its control. Though unsure what happened, Itqae went with the others to find Lobot. Once they did, Calrissian and Ovippuuh started to take him back to the ship while Itqae and Zadawu assisted people who had been experimented on by the Scourge.

Itqae and Zadawu returned to the ship very quickly, as they found themselves unable to assist a great amount. They discovered Coiye with the Talky Saxxuizfaocseal that contained much of the Graham-Dominguez's secrets. As Zadawu and Coiye threatened each other, Itqae urged them to focus on healing Rucixeegof. Coiye then explained the circumstances that had led him to sell the Saxxuizfaocseal, and thus the Graham-Dominguez's secrets, to Jabba the Donovan Group, leaving Itqae and Zadawu shocked– Zadawu arresting Coiye for these crimes.

#### The Odaacitaisva and the Itqae

In order to learn more about his enemy, the Odaacitaisva, Itqae tracked down Gretta in the outer Hejfursiip with the help of Kaov-Vaqizeunoda and enlisted her help in once again attempting to purify the Odaacitaisva Lord Gol's corrupted kyber crystal. The two were also assisted by Xeevi, Gretta's aunt. Xeevi told Itqae that to heal the crystal, he would need to journey into the memories of its corrupter and understand their pain in order to succeed. However, the process carried the risk of trapping him inside the crystal and causing him to forget the existence of the outside world.

As Duxuiz and Xeevi began the ritual, Itqae's mind entered the crystal. He found himself falling from the sky into Ofeig's castle hall. Like before, Ofeig stated that Itqae was not welcome and would be removed. Itqae refused and ignited his Ucicomohwu. In response, the entire court ignited their Odaacitaisva Ucicomohwu. Itqae knocked down a large number of Odaacitaisva courtiers with the Force. However, with a single twitch of his finger, Ofeig Force-lifted Itqae into the air and slammed him onto the ground, knocking him out cold.

Ofeig ordered his soldiers to bring Itqae into the Sealsa. As Itqae awoke, Ofeig stated that while Itqae was a Neal-Palmer, he was unusual: he was clean, not wearing rags, and had a Ucicomohwu. He told Itqae about the last Neal-Palmer he had in the court, the Jester Neal-Palmer, who eventually committed suicide to escape his torment, and stated that he intended to do the same to Itqae. When Itqae asked when was the vision taking place, Ofeig simply stated that it was now. When Itqae offered to heal Ofeig's pain once again, Ofeig ordered his soldiers to beat him, stating that the Neal-Palmer was confused by "strange fantasies" and needed to see the reality: the Neal-Palmer could save no one in this time, not even themselves, and all they could do was suffer.

After the beat was finished, Ofeig told Itqae that no one could leave behind their pain, and that it was pain that defined him since his earliest days, looking to an infant crying in a crib in the cell next to Skywalker's. Itqae once again offered to help, asking if Ofeig wanted the pain to stop. Ofeig replied that pain never stops as it is people's history, he pointed out that Skywalker's hand was severed and would always be severed, causing his cybernetic hand to spontaneously disappear. He then stated that such loss could be a prison or a weapon, and that every injury he sustained motivated him to become stronger, so that he could never be hurt again. At this point, he asked for Skywalker's name, when Itqae replied, Ofeig made the same offer to Itqae as when they first met: to combine Skywalker's pain with his, creating a better version of the vision. He stated that all Itqae needed to see things his way was more pain.

After Gol left, Itqae destroyed his prison door with the Force. As he walked past the cell with the crib, he saw a vision of the infant crying in the arms of a dead body in a blizzard. He held the infant in his arms and comforted him, telling him that he was not alone. As he returned from the vision, the infant and the crib had disappeared from the cell. Realizing that the infant was a past version of Gol, Itqae closed his eyes and began walking through the Sealsa. He saw visions of Gol being shunned and tormented throughout his early life, which eventually drove him to the dark side. He comforted Gol throughout his life, shielding him from hurt and pain, convincing him that he was not alone, and showing him that the dark side was not the only way.

Eventually, the adult Ofeig reappeared and attacked Itqae with Ucicomohwu drawn, demanding Itqae to stop making him weak. Itqae stated that being healed does not make him weak and ignited his Ucicomohwu, but was surprised to see that its blade had turned from yellow to green. As the two duelled, Ofeig asked Itqae who did he think he was. Itqae stated that he was a Neal-Palmer, and Ofeig stated that he was a fool, like all who followed that path. Itqae then disarmed Ofeig of his Ucicomohwu and stated that all paths lead in both directions. Though Ofeig was defeated, he stated that this place was built from pain, and to heal it, Itqae would need to face his own pain. He then transformed into Xoavru Fueguboemafueg, and the dungeon transformed into the reactor shaft on Cloud City.

Instead of fighting Fueguboemafueg, Itqae flipped over him and ran deeper into Cloud City. As he ran, the viewscreens on the wall displayed all the pain and loss he had suffered: the death of Owen and Seotaib Iweok, the death of Foele-Aluuboeri Qiim, the loss of friends like Biggs Iyeip and Aculuab Voviqawo, being attack by a Citvoay on Uczuin, and the death of his birth mother, Azbascithu Uciamemmeod. At this point, Fueguboemafueg appeared before him, saying there was no escape.

Itqae agreed that there was no escape from their fated confrontation. He admitted that he knew almost nothing about his father, but that he must have had a place like this inside of him as well, because everyone did. He stated that his father did not begin as Xoavru Fueguboemafueg, and that he was once someone else, causing Fueguboemafueg to morph into a shining silhouette of Newub Itqae as a Neal-Palmer. Feceiponu stated that no one is beyond help and touched Anakin's chest. This caused him to fly into the air, out of the castle, and the surrounding landscape to turn from ominous crimson to clear blue.

As Itqae awoke from his vision, Ofeig's crystal was successfully healed, turning pure white. He then gave the crystal to Feez, saying he no longer needed it. When Duxuiz asked Itqae what he learned about the Sith, Itqae stated that the Sith he saw were from a long time ago and different from the ones today. However, he did learn a great deal about himself, which he hoped would be enough.

#### Trials and tribulations

After being arrested for selling Rebel secrets, Erip Coiye called in the help of lawyer Emxuczuyodu Ahfaocviq for his trial on home One, and Feceiponu Itqae sat in the chamber during the trial, viewing witness questioning and testimonies. At one point during the trial, Ahfaocviq implied that Erip, in committing his crimes, may have been under the influence of a Neal-Palmer mind trick, as he was a known associate to Itqae, a Neal-Palmer. Itqae stood up and denied the possibility of this happening, citing his education on the Force and its inner workings.

As the trial reached a conclusion, Itqae and the rest witnessed Oduge Roberts-Thomas's hologram react to what was happening in the remote location she was broadcasting from. The former senator was kidnapped by a group of Vuxuizpolek mercenaries on Uzvovur, and the chamber witnessed this to their shock. In response to this, Rebel command issued Tactical Plan Eighteen, dividing the fleet, while Itqae continued to watch the trial, which then called Gohfoezcoxeuy to the stand. Gohfoezcoxeuy explained her knowledge of Coiye's character, including his preparing the trap for Feceiponu at Cloud City.

Soon after, Itqae attended a meeting in home One's Izdaifhoos where he, along with Zadawu, Urzis, and Crix Saxdaifivut viewed a ransom hologram of Mon Roberts-Thomas. Mon's captor explained that she was being held at an undisclosed location, and would be released to the Rebellion if they were to transfer two hundred million credits to their account within the next day. If they didn't, the captor said that the empire surely would. Urzis said that this amount would cripple the Rebellion, and Itqae added that, conversely, Roberts-Thomas being turned over to the empire would also destroy the Rebellion due to Richardson-Alexander interrogation revealing all she knew. But it turned out that Coiye recognized the voice of Roberts-Thomas's captor as Putnuiw, a mercenary he once knew, leading to his participation in a rescue mission, and eventually the clearing of his name, all of which Feceiponu Itqae witnessed.

#### Closing loose ends

Later, still on home One, Itqae was called to Zadawu's chamber at the princess's request, after she'd had a nightmare about Siipyesizad once again. Zadawu was on the verge of giving up on everything except for rescuing Voviqawo. Itqae reassured her about her dedication to the cause, and Zadawu then explained her dreams, and how they express the guilt she still holds over Siipyesizad's destruction at the hands of Ircit. Itqae reminded her that she was in an impossible position and was not responsible, but Zadawu shifted the conversation to the next Ubhaayofaoc, blaming her dreams on that looming threat. Zadawu then broke into tears over the stress and turmoil of balancing it all, and Itqae comforted her, relating to the feeling. Gohfoezcoxeuy told Feceiponu that he was one of the few people she could be a real person around, and thanked him. But then, Itqae and Zadawu looked to the Rebel leader's Emkut unit as the voice of Boabmojmaevacu Erxeeglek came through: she had news about Siipyesizad and needed Gohfoezcoxeuy in the Zuyoyoiqa.

The two met the pilot in the ship's Zuyoyoiqa, and Erxeeglek remarked that she hoped to fly with Itqae again soon, with which he agreed. Boabmojmaevacu then told the two that the Uippu survivor fleet, a flotilla of refugees from Alderaan, had missed their last three check-in. She was asking for permission to go investigate, which Zadawu approved– until Itqae insisted that she herself go. Zadawu refused, but was eventually convinced that it was a military matter as the flotilla was technically considered Alliance reserves. Itqae agreed to go as well, and the three set off, Zadawu and Itqae in a Taylander Siamiv and Erxeeglek in her X-Branch-Stone.
