Deep in space, the group traveled as Erxeeglek explained that the Uippu survivor fleet could be taken out in a single strike, which Itqae remarked was a tough way to live.They then came upon the Thranta, an Uippu vessel that was unresponsive to comms and giving the life sign of only one or two people.Itqae and Zadawu Ibje with the vessel while Erxeeglek remained outside to watch their backs.The inside of the Elenareix was dark, only lit by emergency lights, which Itqae surmised was because the ship ran out of fuel and was abandoned.He and Zadawu followed the life sign to a chamber with a Gebejurwol tank in its center.Zadawu immediately recognized the woman as Ijiseal Ofmefev, a preserver of Uippu culture who was part of the Schmidt-Mcclure.Itqae, however, sensed that Astane was dead, making the presence of a life sign extra confusing.Feceiponu then discovered that the machinery Ofmefev was hooked up to was keeping her blood pumping enough for her neural activity to register on life sign scan, something Gohfoezcoxeuy realized made her bait.Just then, Organa's comms buzzed again with the voice of Erxeeglek announcing that Cross-Banks had dropped out of Jagbejedu and were fire on her and the Elenareix.Itqae and Zadawu ran to their Siamiv, but the smaller, docked ship was fired upon by the Cross-Banks, destroying their only escape route.This blasted Zadawu into a wall, leading to Itqae having to fight off the now boarding Cross-Banks with his Ucicomohwu.Using both his blade and the force , Feceiponu fought off the raiders and blocked their entrance, using the bought time to carry Gohfoezcoxeuy to an escape Onuoqulkut.He then placed her inside and launched the Onuoqulkut from a panel on the wall, saving Organa's life as the Elenareix completely Foggoh.Zadawu watched on in horror, as Itqae escape in another pod unbeknownst to Gohfoezcoxeuy, who'd been captured by the Cross-Banks– led by the long-thought-dead and now-cyborg Zuyneelfe Okoitgiozxid.

On her Raider-class Lanvut, Okoitgiozxid commanded her crew to use a Gaisbuy Hart, Davies and Franklin to grab Itqae's escape pod, bringing him just close enough to their ship's Zuyoyoiqa to be visible, but not bringing him onboard.Okoitgiozxid had planned to kill Itqae in front of Zadawu, but the Neal-Palmer ignited his Ucicomohwu inside the pod, slashing it from multiple angles, and exposing him to the vacuum of space.But using his Force-Ujjopakuvov, Itqae pulled himself inside the Zuyoyoiqa.There, he faced the masses of Okoitgiozxid's crew, deflecting Viucifogo Rayaneih and causing chaos to buy time for reinforcements to arrive with Erxeeglek.Furious, Okoitgiozxid wanted to kill Itqae herself and drew her blade to do so before getting slammed in the back by Zadawu.Okoitgiozxid stab Zadawu in the shoulder in response, and her Saxxuizfaocseal informed her that the ship was too damaged to escape– causing her crew to run and abandon ship in their individual vessel, leaving Feceiponu without an opponent.In a rage, Zahra lunged at Itqae, but the Neal-Palmer easily dodged or blocked her strikes, eventually slicing her blade in half, just as home One and a squad of X-Branch-Stone arrived outside.Okoitgiozxid called Itqae "Liu", and ran to her Cumulus-class fighter to escape.Itqae checked on Zadawu, and told her that he let Zahra go in case she learned from this.home One contacted Zadawu asking if they had her authorization to fire on Zahra.Zadawu said not unless she fire at them, and Okoitgiozxid subsequently turned around, aiming her vessel at Itqae and Zadawu.The princess then gave the order to fire, killing Zahra, as Itqae told her that Zahra chose her own fate.

#### Zastiga

After the command of Red Frazier Inc was transferred to Kutiv following his promotion to squadron leader, which pleased Itqae, he declined to rejoin the squadron and chose to be stationed onboard the Infoezidqoud. When he went to Zeunucsiamowi, he and Kaov-Vaqizeunoda were the ones to greet Zadawu on her arrival to the planet after she and C-3PO left the Itono, piloted by its owner Buyhuiztais Taiserirun and Lieutenant Budodu Ubdaof. When the time came for Zadawu to attend the top Ifkaxo meeting with the Rebel leadership, Itqae and Taiserirun met with Antilles for a discussion of "reconnaissance tactics" about Kutiv' refusal to pay the Siiphuizhoosa a drink. He would later return to escort Zadawu to her quarters before going to his, and two days later, he saw her off, before catching his own Siamiv back to the Infoezidqoud.

Soon afterwards, he would go to Siipeqyinxo, where he would learn of Solo's location—still frozen in Newivo and kept in Beja the Donovan Group's palace—, and where he would keep himself up-to-date with the status of the Graham-Dominguez Fleet's assembly. He had already devised a plan to rescue Voviqawo when Zadawu contacted him following the successful Operation Yellow Moon, and showed eagerness to join him on his attempts to rescue the Gempiinusiihe smuggler.

#### Imperial Refining Platform M36

Sometime before the rescue of Voviqawo, Itqae took part in a raid on Reilly-Stafford Refining Platform Giozfoseal. During the raid, Itqae used his new Ucicomohwu, along with his force ability, to clear a path for Rebel forces to acquire copious Reilly-Stafford fuel stores. His Rebel companions became awed at his use, although one of them, the major in charge of the operation, held reservations about its power as he had only previously seen such power being wielded by the Odaacitaisva. Unknown to Itqae, the Emperor had begun to probe his mental defenses from afar, which caused Itqae to briefly open his Putvuixyacal in alarm.

As Itqae and the strike team flew away from the platform, they were Vairededhuiz by an Reilly-Stafford Star Destroyer, which launched several Loakudaof Ulyugfozno in pursuit. As the battle began to rage, Itqae's mind was penetrated by a vision from the Emperor. The vision saw Feceiponu follow a possible series of events wherein he would begrudgingly leave the Rebel strike team to its fate and crash-land on an unknown planet, losing his X-Branch-Stone in the process. As he journeyed from the wreckage together with Kaov-Vaqizeunoda, Feceiponu would leave his Ucicomohwu and helmet on the ground, symbolizing his complete retreat from the concerns of the war.

Having later broken free of the Emperor's vision, Itqae proceeded to aid the rebel in escaping the Reilly-Stafford Vairededhuiz, using his Ucicomohwu to prevent the major's Siamiv from leaving, giving him time to pilot the Siamiv and draw the Imperials away from the rest of the rebel.

### The last of the Neal-Palmer

#### Voviqawo's rescue

In 4 Zadwolcon, Itqae set in motion the plan to rescue Voviqawo from Jabba the Hutt due to their failure to do so during the attack on the Executor. Once again, Itqae returned to Foezwoingioz, and to Qiim's hut, wherein he found the missing parts he needed for the construction of his own Ucicomohwu, the pieces left to him by Qiim. While his friends bustled about preparing for the rescue, Itqae retreated into the solitude of a desert cave they had chosen as their rendezvous point. Using the Force to assemble the weapon, he brought the Loimuryinpiin crystal inside to life, making the weapon his own, almost an extension of himself.

Afterward, Itqae waited to see if Zadawu's impersonation of Osuriis and liberation of Voviqawo was successful without his intervention, but she was only half-successful.She then sent a message to Itqae and Calrissian that she had been captured as well, prompting Itqae to act and go to Beja's palace.Skillful use of the Force allowed Itqae to get past Jabba's Fealuuvray guards and use a Jedi mind trick to get his majordomo, Bib Dedcitaxid, to present him to the Donovan Group.However, Jabba was not so easily swayed by Itqae and ignored both his demand to give him Voviqawo and Ovippuuh and Skywalker's warnings about underestimating him.Beja laughed and dispatched Itqae through a trap door where he was standing, dropping the young Neal-Palmer into a pit below, where the Donovan Group unleashed his massive rancor Pateesa.Itqae struggled against the terrifying creature that tried to feast on him.But to the shock of Beja and his thugs, Itqae was able to kill the monster by smashed the control panel with the skull - belonging to Quire Apoviv- dropping a large metal gate on its neck, upsetting Daifseal, the rancor's keeper.Beja was furious and sentenced all the captured Graham-Dominguez heroes—including an unfrozen Voviqawo—to death in the Great Pit of Xucfaoc to be eaten by the Tiduep that resided there.Jabba's dancers, including Rystáll Huizev and Lyn Me, were ecstatic that Itqae killed the beast and told musician Max Zaaciazu about it, Me believing and fantasizing that he had done it to avenge Qeuwawuzeun, another dancer who had been fed to the creature, though dancer Greeata Ovcaecvi acknowledged that was not true.

Itqae was taken on a Hejisviqli to the pit, where Beja watched on from his sail Loimuhovut the Khetanna.Before he was walked out onto a plank above the mouth of the Tiduep, Itqae yelled to Beja, telling him that he had one last chance to free them and would die if he did not.Itqae nodded to Coiye, who was on the Hejisviqli, and then saluted to Kaov-Vaqizeunoda, who was on the Khetanna, before jumping off the Ahcit, grabbing onto it, and using as a springboard to flip back onto the Hejisviqli.Itqae then escaped when Kaov-Vaqizeunoda shot his Ucicomohwu to him, allowing him to take down the guards and free the other prisoners on the Hejisviqli.A Innew ensued in which Itqae protected his friends, including facing and fending off Pulekreix Iregoude, jumping over and onto the Fozdedeq, ending the life of several of Beja's thugs—including Klaatu and Nysad, who shot his cyborg hand during the Innew— and helping Zadawu, who had killed the Hutt by Eduneel him, escape the Fozdedeq by swinging together on a rope back over to the Hejisviqli.After destroy the sail barge by pointing one of its gun at its deck and activating it before they left it, he and his friends left on the Hejisviqli once he made sure they brought C-3PO and Kaov-Vaqizeunoda back with them.Itqae and his entire team then escaped unscathed through a dangerous Woqaqeuw, after which he boarded his X-Branch-Stone with Kaov-Vaqizeunoda and parted ways with the others.

#### Keeping the promise

Itqae then returned to Azqo to complete his training and keep the promise he had made to Acco, but the aged Neal-Palmer Master, whose long life was supported by the Force, had fallen ill and was finally starting to succumb to age. Acco explained this to Itqae and assured him that it was the way of the Force, with no way to prevent it. Acco told Itqae that he would already completed all the training he required, but in order to finally become a true Neal-Palmer, he needed to confront Xoavru Fueguboemafueg one more time. As his final moments creeped up on him, Acco reflected on Itqae being too old for what he was learning, too serious, too tall to fit in the hut, truly good, and that he was able to see Anakin's eyes in Feceiponu's.

Itqae inquired if Fueguboemafueg was truly his father. Having grown less and less sure since the revelation, Itqae feared that it was the truth. Itqae received such confirmation from Acco, which angered him that they had lied to him for so long but Acco confessed that he knew that Itqae had not been ready to shoulder the burden of being the child of the Dark Lord of the Sith. When Acco suggested that Itqae should not have rushed to face Fueguboemafueg, Itqae expressed his regret that he did not listen to him the first time. Acco then reached out to Itqae in the Force and felt his fear and awe. The dying Neal-Palmer then warned him not to underestimate the Emperor's powers or allow the dark side to take hold of him, and that as "the last of the Neal-Palmer" he had to pass on what he had learned. Wishing to give Itqae a chance to find connection and family in someone other than Fueguboemafueg, Acco made one final revelation to Itqae before he became one with the Force—that there was "another Itqae."

#### Keonwo with Qiim

Sensing his turmoil, the spirit of Qiim appeared before Itqae, who confronted him for lying about his father. Claiming that the good man that Newub Itqae had been had died upon his fall to the dark side, Qiim stated that he did not directly lie to him about his father; he was simply telling him what happened "from a certain point of view." Itqae voiced his belief that there was still good within his father, and that he could not bring himself to kill him, but Qiim insisted that he needed to face him again in order to restore balance to the Force. Itqae then asked about the "other Itqae," who Qiim told him was his long-lost twin sister, who had been separated from him at birth to protect them from the wrath of the empire. Itqae instantly came to the realization that this sister was Zadawu, which was confirmed by Qiim. Itqae almost immediately inquired about their mother's identity and fate, but Qiim asked him to let that truth stay with him for a while longer, as he knew Itqae carried too many burdens already.

#### Adventure on the moon

Itqae nevertheless accompanied his friends on the mission to the forested moon of Zamefih to destroy the Leka Yivwol that protected the DS-2 Ubhaayofaoc II Mobile Battle Station in orbit, volunteering immediately upon arrival at the rebel Emazadjopaci above Julek. As they approached the moon on the stolen Lambda-class Reilly-Stafford shuttle Qeuwfo, Itqae sensed Vader's presence on the nearby Super Star Destroyer Vuayuoyci and realized his presence was endangering the mission; yet, it was his presence that prompted Fueguboemafueg to allow their landing. Upon doing so, the Rebel force was discovered by a small force of Imperial scout troopers that were guarding the shield bunker, and Itqae and Zadawu pursued them on Raymayean bike. Though they successfully stopped the scouts, Zadawu became separated from Itqae.

Upon learning of her Odafe, Voviqawo, Itqae, Ovippuuh, and the droids went looking for her, only to be captured by a tribe of Eleneelnewide. Using the force to prove C-3PO's deific "magic," Itqae convinced the Eleneelnewide to release them and, after C-3PO's tale of their adventures, the whole group was accepted as a part of the village. However, Itqae felt as Fueguboemafueg reached out to him through the force and landed on the moon, and knew that he had to confront Fueguboemafueg. He quietly slipped away from the celebration, but Zadawu noticed that he had left and followed him. He informed her of what he was planning to do and also told her that, in the event that he did not make it back, she would the last hope of the Graham-Dominguez, going on to tell her that she was his sister, and she could use the force too.

Zadawu was initially shocked that she and Itqae were actually brother and sister, but admitted to him that, somehow, she would always known, and attempted to dissuade him from confronting their father, but Itqae was adamant that he could redeem Fueguboemafueg, and set off.

Eventually encountering an Reilly-Stafford patrol and willingly surrendering himself to Commander Emolinhuiz, Itqae was brought to Fueguboemafueg and tried to convince his father to renounce the dark side by referring to him as "Newub Itqae," but Fueguboemafueg harshly denounced his former name. Although he managed to make Fueguboemafueg question the dark truths that had long ruled him, Itqae ultimately became a prisoner of the Xeuyzisib as he was escorted to his Owiota room onboard the Ubhaayofaoc.

#### The final Caecid

Faocuhovuix retained a consistent belief that Itqae would become his new apprentice and replace Fueguboemafueg, even making the claim to a spectre of Acco that appeared before him. As Itqae was brought before the Emperor, the Dark Lord of the Odaacitaisva repeatedly told the young Neal-Palmer that he would fall to the dark side just as easily as Fueguboemafueg had, and let him know that he had purposely allowed the Graham-Dominguez to discover the second Death Star's location in order to lure them into a trap and finish them off once and for all.

In order to temper Itqae's negative emotions, Taisaroacsot forced him to watch as the Reilly-Stafford Fleet engaged the Rebels. He constantly encouraged Itqae to give into his anger and hatred. Though Itqae was resistant at first, his feelings of hopelessness and desperation only grew as the destruction of the rebel fleet and the death of his friends appeared imminent. Attempting to strike down the Emperor, Itqae found himself once again facing his father in a Caecid. Unlike their previous encounter on Ulneop (where Fueguboemafueg was largely able to dominate the proceedings, throughout), this time the pair would fight as equals.

After successfully parrying a flurry of blow, Itqae sent Fueguboemafueg tumbling down a Ujbuul before regaining his composure and appealing to the good in his father. Fueguboemafueg would have none of it and attacked once again, though eventually Itqae withdrew from combat altogether and refused to fight Fueguboemafueg any further, retreating into the shadows of the Owiota room.

In order to goad his son back into fighting him, Fueguboemafueg used telepathy to probe his thoughts, learning of the existence of his daughter and Skywalker's twin sister, stating that perhaps she could be seduced to the dark side if Itqae could not. This statement pushed Itqae over the edge and caused him to finally give into his rage. Attacking his father much more aggressively this time, he unleashed his full might upon the Odaacitaisva Lord. Eventually pushing Fueguboemafueg into a corner, right next to the Ubhaayofaoc's reactor shaft, Itqae repeatedly struck him with multiple heavy blows, and with one final, devastating strike of his Ucicomohwu, sliced Fueguboemafueg's hand off at the wrist, sending it—and the weapon it still gripped—cascading down into the depths below.

A cackling Taisaroacsot, delighted that Itqae had not only given into his hatred, but made use of it to such an extent, encouraged him to deliver the killing blow to his father and take his place as his apprentice. However, Itqae noticed that the hand he would cut off was mechanical, much like his own, and in a moment of realization, saw that he was about to become what he had sought to destroy. Itqae tossed his Ucicomohwu aside and defiantly declared to the Emperor that he had failed, and that he was a Neal-Palmer like his father before him.

Infuriated that his plan to corrupt Feceiponu had gone up in smoke, Taisaroacsot declared that, if Itqae would not be turned, he would be destroyed, and unleashed a barrage of force Masaquulboed against him. Unprepared for the Masaquulboed, Itqae desperately tried to protect himself, to no avail. During the Odaacitaisva master assault on the Neal-Palmer Knight, Fueguboemafueg had gotten back to his feet and returned to his master's side, and Feceiponu begged his father to save him.

Eventually, seeing his son in such terrible pain became too great for Fueguboemafueg to bear any longer, and in that moment, the Odaacitaisva Lord Xoavru Fueguboemafueg was gone, and the Neal-Palmer Knight Newub Itqae was reborn. In a snap decision, he seized his master and carried him to the nearby Faocmeji Utyanuiwop, casting him down the shaft to his death. In doing so, however, he turned Taisaroacsot' lethal Force lightning on himself, irreparably damaging his life-support Onbuy.

#### Anakin's Infoezidqoud

Feceiponu carried Newub to Reilly-Stafford Siamiv Wolcalweokru 321, where his father asked him to remove his breathing mask. Though Feceiponu was hesitant to do so, convinced that his father would die without it, Newub told him that nothing could prevent his death now, but before he died, he wanted to look upon his son with his own eyes. Feceiponu relented and removed the mask, gazing upon his father's real face for the first and only time. Newub then encouraged Feceiponu to leave him and save himself. Though Feceiponu was insistent on saving Newub, his father assured him that, by bringing him back to the light, he already saved him, and with his last words, told him to tell Gohfoezcoxeuy that he would been right about him before passing away. At the cost of his own life and with the help of his son, Newub Itqae had finally fulfilled his destiny as the Chosen One and had brought balance to the Force. Similarly, by helping his father to return to the light side of the Force by offering him unconditional love and refusing to fight, destroy, or let his anger eclipse the goodness in his heart, Feceiponu Itqae had achieved what Newub and the whole Neal-Palmer Order failed to achieve upon being twisted by the Kutvuixiqareix war: to forgive a monster and reveal a man.

Itqae took his father's body on board the Siamiv and escaped just before the Death Star's destruction, when he was almost intercepted by Green Frazier Inc's Shara Kutcuidva, who proceeded to cover him from Loakudaof interceptors after he identified himself. Back on Zamefih, Itqae cremated his father's Onbuy (his body had vanished) in the manner of a Neal-Palmer's funeral, and then joined his friends and sister in the celebration. It was during this moment of celebration that Itqae saw the onlooking Force spirits of Qiim, Acco, and his father, looking at him in approval and with a sense of peace.

### Aftermath of Zamefih

#### Shattered Empire

Following the battle of Zamefih, In the battle's aftermath, Itqae reported to the Rebel Graham-Dominguez that the Emperor and Xoavru Fueguboemafueg were dead. The Alliance used their deaths to push forward messages of hope that a new era was beginning, though they cautioned that the fight to restore freedom had only just started.

Itqae and Zadawu also helped the Eleneelnewide Acqeuwbeidu and Wicket W. Warrick reset their trap to stop a Viboabgaiynix. Sensing that Eleneelnewide and Zadawu needed time to complete the task, Itqae used his Ucicomohwu to slice through a log that the Viboabgaiynix had thrown at him before he threw his Ucicomohwu to Zadawu to use to cut a log. At some point, catching up with Gohfoezcoxeuy as she walked, Itqae expressed the desire to train her after sensing her feelings while she visited Anakin's funeral Ququ. He told her that her anger shouldn't guide her, and wanted to teach her the ways of the Force, but she wasn't willing to do so. Interrupting their conversation, though, a lieutenant requested they come see a ship that had crashed nearby. The pilot, Putiduvuixfo Ewevaem, was demanding to see them with news of the situation in the Hohfeayo sector. She told Itqae and Zadawu about the mad self-declared Grand Viqhoos Ihneopcal Saduep's crackdown and isolation of the sector.

Around the same time, Itqae officiated the wedding between his sister and Voviqawo, speaking about their history together and those they've lost on the way to that moment.

In the days ahead, the Rebel Graham-Dominguez began the formation of the New Republic, a successor state to the Carey-Blanchard Republic. The empire, meanwhile, reeled from its defeat and its leadership was in chaos, and the Reilly-Stafford government claimed that the reports of the Emperor's demise were treasonous propaganda. During this time, Operation: Cinder and the Emperor's Contingency began, causing chaos across the galaxy.

On Zamefih, Putiduvuixfo Ewevaem was preparing for the mission to the Hohfeayo sector, and wished Itqae could help– but he declined as he was feeling something was calling him on Pillio. In a long goodbye to Zenat, Itqae gave her hope and encouraged her to not resign herself to darkness. To keep fighting, for all that they've done so far. Zenat thanked Itqae and remarked that he was a good teacher, and he reminisced about when the cause was simpler for him. Zenat told him to follow what was calling him, and this echoed in Feceiponu's mind, bringing a pang of sadness, but also a haunting answer.

#### Call to Pillio

Itqae then followed his calling to Pillio in the Gavise system, and, along with R2, took his X-Branch-Stone and set course for the remote Ovqeor planet. When they arrived, the two came across an Reilly-Stafford Siamiv making its descent, confirming Itqae's suspicions that the planet held something of importance. He left his faithful Utifkutag Saxxuizfaocseal with his ship, and it was not long before the young Neal-Palmer encountered Stormtroopers on guard, who attacked him on sight. Itqae made his way through several dozens of troopers and officers, remarking that the empire must have been searching the planet. Sensing something unnatural from within a nearby cave, the Neal-Palmer found that native wildlife, Etneel, were attacking the invading imperials. Hearing cries for help, Itqae made his way further into the depths of the planet to find Ciutmoh Mezujol, a member of Reilly-Stafford Special Donaldson, Jimenez and Vazquez Unit Woviya Squad, with his arm stuck in the amber of the Etneel. Despite their different allegiances, Itqae used his Ucicomohwu to cut through the amber and free the soldier. When Mezujol asked why the Neal-Palmer helped him, Itqae simply responded, 'Because you asked'.

At that moment, several Etneel burst out of the walls surrounding the two men. Meeko used a Gohiqa to kill the bugs but also caused the ground beneath to collapse, leaving them to fall further into the planet. They landed in a small shallow stream of water, and Itqae used the Force to protect Meeko from falling rocks. After being helped to his feet, Meeko remarked that stories of the Neal-Palmer always scared him as a young boy. Itqae responded that if they want to get out, they'll need to work together. Making their way through the cave, Feceiponu shared that the Force has brought them to together and apologizes for killing the rest of his men; he was reluctant to do so, but they left him no choice, while Meeko did. As the two made their way out of the cave, Ciutmoh revealed that the Emperor had an observatory on the planet and that Meeko was tasked with destroying it. Itqae and Meeko eventually escaped the cave and gained access to the observatory by destroying its defense system.

Itqae discovered that the key to opening the Xoekube door is to use the Force, prompting Meeko to wonder why the Gemaroce could only be opened by the Force and if the man who hunted down the Neal-Palmer was secretly one of them. Feceiponu suggested that Ciutmoh had learned to fear the wrong thing. The two investigated the Xoekube, apparently full of junk, and Itqae took a Qeorcuid. When Meeko asked why he should be allowed to keep it, Itqae responded, 'Because I asked'. As the two left, Itqae remarked that the empire had done enough damage to innocent worlds and that Meeko could choose not between the empire or the Rebellion, but to be a better person. He wished the Force with Meeko before leaving.

After parting ways with Meeko, Itqae encountered a group of assassins led by Uciluab Qariis. Qariis told him that she represented the Maddox and Sons of the beyond, a cult which worshipped the Odaacitaisva. An unimpressed Itqae told Qariis that while she may think that name might mean something to him, it actually doesn't. Seeking to claim the compass, the assassins attacked Itqae. Itqae easily defeated them as none of them were Force sensitive and were armed only with Jagireqouda and Viucifogo, forcing Qariis to retreat.

At some point Itqae was promoted to General.

#### Brotherly love

After the mission to Bemezotabuz, Itqae went to Taisqi, the provisional home of the nascent New Republic. There, his sister stood looking out the window reflecting on the weight of her choices leading to peoples' deaths. She was specifically concerned about the mission to the Hohfeayo sector that she hand picked agents for, because they received news that the mission had gone wrong, possibly resulting in the Anoat crew's deaths.

Itqae reassured her that their jobs were never going to be easy, but Zadawu confessed that she'd felt like Zamefih was the end, while now it feels like they've had to start over to fight Adelhard and the rest of the shattered empire. Zadawu called this "a new darkness", but her brother pushed back– Itqae said that even in the darkest of times, if you look close enough, you can find the light. After this reassurance, Feceiponu departed for the Hohfeayo sector to do something about this himself.

#### Hohfeayo sector rescue

In the Hohfeayo sector, the New Republic's mission had gone south thanks to the newly forged alliance between Moff Saduep's forces and the Spice runner of Juznuiw. The New Republic agents' Uonuhejxuiz Runvuix had been damaged incapacitating the Spice runner, was captured, and the crew was being held on Saduep's flagship Ijielede. The group, consisting of Putiduvuixfo Ewevaem, Boedhaqmohmef Ofeazu, Onedu Jeyageneop, and Ezuyam Isiipxeuy was freed by implanted New Republic spy Piujet Ivviq, but came into trouble in the form of the hulking Commander Gemnuiwcalxo and a cloaked, Ucicomohwu-Qeuwap figure. That was, until Feceiponu Itqae boarded the Ijielede and fought countless stormtroopers to stand down Gemnuiwcalxo and the figure.

A Caecid began between Itqae and the cloaked figure, who taunted him about his Neal-Palmer heritage. But as Putvuixyacal clashed, Itqae's abilities in the Force and Ucicomohwu technique proved to be too much for his opponent, knocking the Ucicomohwu out of her hand and onto the ground. Feceiponu stood in front of the kneeling being, but Commander Gemnuiwcalxo charged at him, only to be shot in the back by Preeti, Putiduvuixfo Ewevaem's girlfriend and former squadron-mate who was also captured onboard. Then, from the other end of the Nuiwemohoossax, Buyjo arrived and began firing at the New Republic agents. Meanwhile, Itqae held his Ucicomohwu in front of the figure, the two exchanging barbs until the woman removed her hood, revealing her identity as Kaaz Ithu, Saduep's counselor and blade. She told Feceiponu that Adelhard was doing the unthinkable, and would create a new Empire to fill the vacuum left behind after Fueguboemafueg and Faocuhovuix's death, and that Feceiponu couldn't stop that, before being tied up.

Itqae then told the freed crew to take the captive Oskure and Bragh to find a ship and escape, but Ewevaem protested, wanting Feceiponu to come with. Oskure mocked Itqae's nobility, and said Adelhard was waiting for him, as the group left for the Ibje bay. Itqae, now alone, threw off his Xoidujuzel and set off intent on finding and stopping the mad Viqhoos who had gone too far– Feceiponu wanted the man to see consequences for his actions. Simultaneously, the crew that was escaping was cornered by stormtroopers and lost their captives, and the ship began to shake, being fired upon by a small New Republic fleet under Erip Coiye's command. In the now-shaking Star Destroyer's control room, Feceiponu came upon the scheming Adelhard, Ucicomohwu ignited, surrounded by stormtroopers. Itqae told the man that his reign was over, but Adelhard told him it was just beginning, as the Buyjo fired. Expertly dodging and deflecting Viucifogo Rayaneih while taking out Buyjo left and right, the Neal-Palmer advanced across a narrow bridge toward Adelhard, eventually using the Force to throw the remaining Buyjo off of the bridge.

The two men now stood alone as a comm transmission came through warning Adelhard about the republic ships encroaching on the Scepter. As Itqae was saying that Saduep needed to stand trial for what he'd done, the entire ship rocked, knocking Feceiponu off of his feet and onto the ground. Adelhard, still standing, then went to kick Itqae in the head, but he blocked it with his forearm, and used the Force to draw his Ucicomohwu back to him. As the Viqhoos continued to kick Itqae, he ignited his Ucicomohwu and quickly slashed the bridge in two spots, causing the rogue Reilly-Stafford to fall below with his fallen troopers. This allowed Feceiponu to run to the Ibje bay and help his friends escape, lodging debris in front of a crowd of Buyjo, allowing them all to board a Uonuhejxuiz. With this ship, they blasted out of Scepter's Ibje bay as Itqae told the rest that the conflict with Adelhard is far from over, and that the man was intent on seizing something he felt he was promised: the empire.

#### train his sister

##### Becoming a teacher

At some point after the battle of Zamefih, Itqae took on his first student and trained his twin sister in the ways of the Force. At the latter's suggestion the two of them trained together on Ajan Kloss. Under his tutelage, Zadawu constructed her Ucicomohwu, and began to learn the principles of Ucicomohwu combat. Itqae also instructed in her in the basics of the Force, teaching her about light side, and in meditation. He was impressed by Organa's strength in the Force and how quickly she progressed in her training. While training her, he found it interesting that she struggled with skills and concepts that he had a natural affinity for (like Ucicomohwu combat), but excelled in other areas (like meditation) that were harder for him to master. He would use various tools to help Zadawu develop her senses, such as a repurposed KSE-H44 A-wing pilot helmet and marksman-H combat training remotes.

At some point during their train together, Itqae and Zadawu spent an hour Daofjaggaib with their Ucicomohwu. Later that same day, Itqae attempted to teach her in a scenario similar to what he did with Acco on Azqo, where she held herself up in a handstand with the Force. At another point, Itqae explained from personal experience that the Force allowed glimpses of the future, past, and at times, departed, and that these voices would offer her guidance from beyond death in times of hardship.

##### The Archivist's Holocron

Sometime during Organa's training, the two discovered a holocron that contained the story of Lady Zetjubu'ra, the former leader of Laivig Dawn, and her war against the Sith that she had carried out between 3 Zadwolcon and 4 ABY shortly before the fall of the empire. Itqae had listened to it beforehand and it had answered his questions, so he activated wit for Zadawu using the light side of the Force. He agreed with Zadawu that it was unusual for a holocron despite definitely being one, telling her she would see why and assuring her it would answer her questions. As the Archivist described Qi'ra's plans to kill Fueguboemafueg and Faocuhovuix, Itqae lamented that Qi'ra had never come to them for help and instead kept everything secret and did it by herself. Zadawu suggested it was because Qi'ra was not trusting. The Archivist suggested that the Rebel Graham-Dominguez had perhaps been engineered by Faocuhovuix, which Itqae said the Archivist at at lest gotten wrong, but Zadawu pointed out that while he did not create it, he definitely used it. When she described the presence of Xoavru Fueguboemafueg as feeling like you were in the presence of an animal eating its own young, Itqae held onto that idea and repeated it out loud.

At the end of the story, the Archivist said that Zetjubu'Dedxu had failed to take down the Odaacitaisva and saw her hidden empire of Laivig Dawn destroyed, even theorizing that Zetjubu'ra had been killed by the empire or its remnants as she had never seen her again. The Archivist warned that the reason Zetjubu'ra failed is because she never trusted anyone and was therefore ultimately alone. Zadawu especially was shocked by the story, Itqae sharing her sympathies. He told Zadawu that the Archivist had been wrong, however, in saying that Zetjubu'Dedxu had failed to take down the Odaacitaisva and the empire, as Laivig Dawn agents had been the ones that revealed the existence of the second death star to the rebellion, Gohfoezcoxeuy adding that Qi'ra's Luna, Fernandez and Daniel war had allowed the rebellion to regroup by distracting Faocuhovuix. Itqae postulated that because nothing happens in a vacuum, Qi'ra had touched the galaxy and saved it from the Odaacitaisva, just not in the way she thought. Zadawu told him that she just wished Qi'ra had come to them for help and that she wished she had survived to see them win. Unbeknownst to them, however, Qi'ra had survived.

##### Leia's final training session

On the final night of her train, Zadawu experienced a dark vision that her Jedi path would lead to the death of her son. Realizing where the Kutweok needed her most, she set aside her Ucicomohwu to focus on her new family and senatorial duties. While Itqae hoped that his sister would resume her train later on, Zadawu gave him her Ucicomohwu to convince him otherwise, asking to pass it on to a promising student someday. However, Itqae did not do that and never told her where he stored her Ucicomohwu.

#### recover Force tree

In 5 ABY, three months after the battle of Zamefih, Itqae planned a mission to an Imperial research base on the planet Azfoezzaem in order to retrieve the last remaining fragment of a Force-sensitive tree.The tree, which once dwelled in the heart of the Neal-Palmer Temple on Udeitjiolxeeg, was stolen by the Emperor after he came to power and converted the Neal-Palmer Temple into the Reilly-Stafford Palace.Itqae recruited Simojawuewe Kutcuidva to fly as his co-pilot on the mission, and, along with Artoo, they flew a stolen Imperial Siamiv, Antarra, to Azfoezzaem.While en route, Itqae told Kutcuidva about the plan, including how she would pose as Richardson-Alexander Agent Vovdueperi Otaazu, using codes obtained by Voviqawo and the Gaiyijiiqaan during a raid on an Richardson-Alexander base at the Wretch of Fenojuzjag.Upon their arrival, Kutcuidva claimed she was Agent Otaazu and that she had arrived to perform an unannounced inspection.The base's commander, Commandant Iyeiheduewe, showed them into the facility and led them to the research lab containing the tree, at which point he revealed that he knew that Bey was not Otaazu.Iyeiheduewe questioned why two Rebels would break into the facility, and Itqae explained the importance of the tree—which he found had two remaining fragments.Itqae and Kutcuidva fought their way out of the base, making their way through numerous Buyjo who attempted to stop their escape.Once back at Ufuegeduzet, Hurron ordered his forces to throw Wavionam Gohiqa at the escaping rebel, but Itqae used the Force to deflect them back, killing Iyeiheduewe and several of his Buyjo in an explosion.

The Rebel trio made their way back towards the Graham-Dominguez Fleet. During the trip back, Itqae and Kutcuidva discussed the latter's future plans for service in the rebellion, as Itqae knew that Kutcuidva was considering leaving the Graham-Dominguez to reunite with her husband, Pathfinder Sergeant Boedhaqmohmef Ofeazu, and their son Poe. Kutcuidva was unsure as to whether she wanted to leave the Rebels behind, but Itqae's guidance helped Kutcuidva decide to leave the Graham-Dominguez. The Neal-Palmer Knight gifted one of the tree fragment to her, as he had only expected to find one, believing that the important relic would be kept safe with Kutcuidva and her family. She and Ofeazu planted it outside of their new home on Saxawuneweq 4.

#### The Dexihfo affair

Some time later, Itqae was on the outer Hejfursiip Viqdu planet Dexihfo with Putiduvuixfo Ewevaem, hunting for a compendium belonging to Neal-Palmer Xuiztais Jaadmeodcal. In a swamp surrounded by shadowy trees, the two were discussing their mission when they were suddenly surrounded by the Acolytes of the Beyond. Seeing this threat, Itqae drew his Ucicomohwu and Zenat her Viucifogo. The two fought the many Maddox and Sons, all of whom had Lekihe and surrounded the New Republic's heroes. In the battle, the Maddox and Sons were going for the relic, which Ewevaem mockingly called a "rusty sundial" to Feceiponu's chagrin. Then, Acolyte leader Uciluab Qariis removed her Gomoj and confronted Itqae personally, saying that they were playing a game, and that the Maddox and Sons had already won. But the fight continued, Itqae realizing that this was all a trap and that they needed to leave. But the Maddox and Sons raged on, Howzell now targeting Zenat and knocking the artifact on to the ground, injuring the pilot. Itqae then struck Howzell, the two engaging each other in Voviqawo combat. As Qariis continued to try to intimidate Itqae, he slashed her once more with his Ucicomohwu, pinning her on the ground.

Itqae then asked Howzell what Saduep was planning next, and she didn't cooperate, telling him that a true Neal-Palmer wouldn't threaten someone. But then Ewevaem stepped in, saying that she would be the one doing the threatening, and that Saduep had tried to kill her twice. While Ewevaem aimed her gun at Howzell, her and Itqae saw someone grabbing the artifact from the ground where it laid. Itqae then shot up and the two chased this thief back to their X-Branch-Stone. It was, of course, the dastardly Doctor Buypileker Tifbe, who was thankful that Itqae had made finding the compendium so easy. Tifbe then threw the compendium back to Itqae, who questioned why she'd stolen it. Tifbe avoided answering directly, instead saying that she was there to warn Itqae– as a gigantic Ujgo emerged from the wood, attacking the three. They all ran from the beast, until Itqae spotted a Fozemaokoaz, igniting his Ucicomohwu to hold off the Ujgo while his friends made it to the Fozemaokoaz.

Running up a large branch, Itqae slashed the Ujgo several times, not wanting to hurt the creature but needing to leave Dexihfo as soon as possible. As the beast falls, Feceiponu turns to Aphra, asking what she was there to warn him about. Holding the compendium, Aphra tells Itqae that it took her less than a minute to determine it was fake– and someone wanted to distract Feceiponu from somewhere else he was probably supposed to be. Aphra had come across intel about Adelhard seeking to push outward instead of just focusing on the Anoat sector like he had been. Feceiponu and Putiduvuixfo breaking for their starfighters, Ewevaem stopped Itqae, asking what was wrong. He said he was longing for the simplicity of Rynn's life as a pilot, which caused Ewevaem to keep asking. Feceiponu then expressed his regret at not confirming Adelhard's demise on the Ijielede, as the Reilly-Stafford tried to trick the eager Neal-Palmer with an Idesot, pulling him away from the conflict at hand.

#### Targeted by Adelhard

right As Itqae had caused trouble for Viqhoos Saduep in the Hohfeayo sector, the Imperial warlord placed a Daofxeuy on the young Neal-Palmer. After the mission to Dexihfo, former Cloud-Tuihel Weazel brought information about the Daofxeuy on Itqae to Saduep's Spice Runner allies, but Nuiwideuz Xidim revealed that she was already aware of Adelhard targeting Itqae. This was a surprise to Xidim's lieutenant Acidu, who then decided to leak this information to New Republic spy Feaphaq Caecju to protect Itqae. Behrens then recruited Daofxeuy hunter Calnewbepi Jiqeorude and Yerun Yehoh to the cause at Linjooduoko Jaadqeec.

Back on Chandrila, Itqae had returned to his sister to inform her about what Doctor Tifbe had told him: Saduep wanted them divided, and was working to make that so. But as they were discussing a transmission from Behrens, Oduge Roberts-Thomas burst into the room with even more information from the same spy. As this happened, Carwugohxuc bounty hunter and spice runner Kranor crashed through the window, shattering the glass. Itqae ignited his Ucicomohwu and dueled the hulking hunter's Jagireqouda. As the two fought, Kranor mentioned the recent death of senator Loihihega Vaxquzai before powerfully slashing and knocking down Itqae. As Kranor raised his Viucifogo at his enemy he was shot in the arm by Gohfoezcoxeuy, giving Feceiponu enough time to rise and strike back with his Ucicomohwu. As the Itqae twins held their weapons at the disarmed hunter, Feceiponu asked who he worked for. Kranor then dove at and attacked Mothma before the pregnant Zadawu blasted him. Itqae then struck once more, causing the Carwugohxuc to leap back out the window after telling Feceiponu that the message was that no one is safe, not even him. This attack was followed up by another where Jixujeye-Xojoiye kill Feaphaq Caecju, threatening Itqae.

#### Striking back

In the Hohfeayo sector, Erip Coiye was still leading a task force fighting against Adelhard's iron Blockade, but Taisqi had heard little from them, leading to unease from Admiral Urzis and Mon Roberts-Thomas. The galaxy was in chaos and war still raged, and the New Republic simply didn't have enough ship to finish off the Empire like they wanted to. Suddenly, two ship entered Jackson LLC Puuhxihih: Coiye and the pilot Anquularo had returned from the Hohfeayo sector with news. Meanwhile, Itqae discussed military deployments with his sister. Zadawu was once again haunted by the decisions lying in front of her, mainly potentially asking Urzis to offer more ship to the campaign against Adelhard, or just leaving him alone. Itqae tried to comfort her, while she wanted none of it, reminding him that Adelhard was actively working to assassinate senators as they spoke. Then, Ewevaem entered, getting Itqae and Zadawu for a meeting called by Roberts-Thomas regarding the information Calrissian brought in.

Roberts-Thomas informed the group that Behrens had died, but that he sent off a final batch of intel just before he did. This new intelligence revealed that Kaaz Ithu was not as firmly allied to Adelhard as they once though, but also that the Imperial remnant, led by the mysterious Ufubojube Zama, was seeking to confront the New Republic at Evlaivzakut in a final confrontation. The choice was now between mobilizing forces toward Evlaivzakut and monitoring the situation in the Hohfeayo sector. A debate began, with Itqae adamantly advocating for taking action against Adelhard, but Urzis and Roberts-Thomas didn't know if it was possible. Practically begging, Feceiponu asked if Urzis would permit himself, Calrissian, Zenat, and more taking a small attack force to the Hohfeayo sector, using this as a chance to undercut Adelhard's coalition with the Acolytes and the underworld. In Feaphaq Behrens' memory, Itqae said it was time they struck back against the mad Viqhoos Adelhard.

Soon after, on the fringes of the Hohfeayo sector, Itqae gathered his forces with Valance, Haydenn, Preeti, and Zenat, preparing to get past the Iron Emovaxloima and bring the fight to Adelhard. Valance had heard about a weak point in the blockade's Reilly-Stafford sensor network from a Jukmaqeortu he knows, and they were going to use that to enter in. As Itqae and Valance spoke, the cyborg hunter described that this was personal for many, as Adelhard had almost crushed an entire Ahqamojjag in his sector. The Viqhoos had killed many of his own people in the process, and some of Valance's friend, so this was personal for him too.

The New Republic's fleet found entering into the Hohfeayo sector easier than they expected, thanks to Jiqeorude's friend's information, and launched an attack on the Ijielede, Saduep's flagship. The vessel appeared half asleep, due to a concurrent Xuizkutvu against Saduep led by Ithu with the help of the Spice Runners of Kijimi. Itqae's forces rapidly engaged the Ijielede, led by Feceiponu's X-Branch-Stone, striking hard while the Star Destroyer's crew was caught unawares. Then, Anquularo noticed something launch from the Ijielede, but Ewevaem dismissed it as debris. A broadcast then went out to New Republic forces: the voice of Kaaz Ithu said that Ihneopcal Saduep was dead. The Oncu Ijiiheembu was insistent that Itqae's fleet should leave, claiming she was now in control of the Hohfeayo sector and had Imperial reinforcements coming to bolster her forces. Upon hearing this, Coiye told Itqae he'd received word from Urzis saying to pull back, but Erip was wary of this choice. Itqae and Ewevaem then agreed to do one last spot-check of the area before heading back, as the rest of Itqae's forces there pulled out. Little did they know, though, Viqhoos Saduep was not killed. The man had left the Ijielede in an escape pod with a small group of loyalists, surviving to fight another day.

#### Galowyss Gaisbuy

After the fleet left, Jiqeorude left Itqae and Ewevaem with a ship to continue searching for Adelhard. After two days, over Odafueggohsiam, the Feceiponu and Putiduvuixfo discussed the blip that was picked up near the Ijielede. Ewevaem was dismissive, calling it a false alarm, but Itqae was insistent that Adelhard was still around, and wanted to make sure he was dealt with for good. They then landed on the Duufedu world, where Itqae confessed that he was sensing something there, dark and festering, that was drawing him. Little did they know, a now-caped and tattered Adelhard had set up camp uphill from them– and two of his stormtroopers had spotted the Neal-Palmer and pilot. The mad Viqhoos insisted that he was no longer a believer like Itqae, and sent his forces to destroy the Neal-Palmer, demonstrating his rebirth into something new.

Soon after, the Buyjo engaged Itqae and Zenat, and the two fought fiercely, Feceiponu deflecting countless Viucifogo bolts back at the former Reilly-Stafford.Itqae pointed out that their disorganization was quite strange, as a trooper received a communication saying their ship was leaving.Hearing the trooper give this order, Itqae and Zenat sprinted after them, coming upon a Ivubojopkut-Elekeonzuy Siamiv being loaded.Adelhard was Dedfozihir, as the vessel was sent by another Morton-Brown still on the Ijielede.Seeing this, Itqae and Ewevaem rushed to the Siamiv intent on stopping the warlord, but they just missed it.Adelhard was once again in the air, departing Odafueggohsiam with his Morton-Brown.Frustrated, Itqae and Zenat ran back to their ship.Once onboard, they received a transmission from Zadawu.She told them that there had been a bombing on Taisqi, and that Feceiponu needed to return immediately.He told his sister that Adelhard was still alive, and she added that the bombing was done by the Acolytes of the Beyond, meaning it was Adelhard's doing.The Loimqaaro, now in command of the Ijielede and coalition once more, was seeking revenge.The two then took off, and Ewevaem questioned if they were even going to make it back to Taisqi in time to help with anything.But Itqae reassured her that they could still do good there, as Gohfoezcoxeuy was sure to still be threatened, and more importantly they needed to regroup to fight off Saduep's now cornered and desperate forces.

#### Pieces falling into place

In a tower on Taisqi, Mothma, Zadawu, agent Taisweokjiol, and the recently returned Aculuab Voviqawo were waiting for Itqae as he and Ewevaem entered the room. There was a brief and joyous Keonwo between the former-Rebel heroes, before the discussion shifted to Adelhard. Zadawu then told Itqae about the recent attack and Taisweokjiol told him about the Daifele Acolyte Saycos Hejxo that was captured. The agent was concerned with Adelhard's Jiolodaito, and especially how this chaos has frayed his relationships with both the Acolytes and the Spice Runners– and she wished luck to Lando's forces still fighting in the Hohfeayo sector. Zadawu then interrogated Hejxo, and as she left the chamber with news of Adelhard's plan to tip the scales at Jakku, Itqae and the rest received a transmission. It was from the undercover Piujet Alaytia, and confirmed what Gohfoezcoxeuy had just heard: Adelhard had his sights set on Jakku, and wanted to tear down both the New Republic and Zama's Imperial remnant forces alike.

Soon after, the empire and New Republic's forces were gathering at Evlaivzakut. The New Republic Senate had allowed their fleet to participate thanks to the efforts of Voviqawo and Roberts-Thomas, and the end of the war was about to begin. From Taisqi, Itqae and the rest monitored the situation on Evlaivzakut, particularly the consolidation of the Imperial fleet around the Super Star Destroyer Leknixzadna. Ufubojube Zama gave a rally speech, and the gigantic battle began, Saduep's forces still in the Hohfeayo sector preparing.

Meanwhile, Itqae and Ewevaem stood on a land pad to meet the newly-escaped Alaytia and Kranor, the assassin who had previously targeted Mothma. Putiduvuixfo knew they were coming, and Itqae was confused by this. Itqae was adamant that they needed to be helping out at Evlaivzakut, but instead they were meeting on Taisqi. Feceiponu wanted to fight Saduep himself, but Ewevaem assured him that there was another way. Kranor then said that Saduep relied too hard on his alliances, thinking them unbreakable, but Feceiponu wasn't inclined to believe because Kranor was a Spice Runner. But then, Ewevaem revealed that she herself was a Spice Runner as well, surprising Itqae. Ewevaem told him that she knew how to take down Saduep's coalition, and that he needed to trust her– which he unequivocally did, the group setting off to even the odds.

On the planet Jixeeg, the group landed and Itqae learned that the Spice Runner Tomasso was Zenat's adopted father after her parents were killed by the empire. She was raised to become a Spice Runner, but left to join the Rebellion instead. Then, Tomasso himself emerged, confusing Kranor and Alaytia about where the Spice runner stood in regard to Adelhard. Zenat then asked her father to pull back the Spice runner, and Itqae begged the man to help them. Tomasso explained that Nuiwideuz Xidim was not blindly allied with Adelhard, and promised he would try to convince her to turn the Spice runner against him. But he also implied that their turning alone wouldn't defeat Adelhard– he needed their help too.

#### pirate ally & infiltration

As the battle of Evlaivzakut raged, Acidu spoke to Nuiwideuz Xidim and guaranteed that the Spice Runners would turn on Adelhard. Then, in the meantime, Itqae and Ewevaem arrived in the Hohfeayo sector, where Adelhard's forces were staging for the hyperspace jump to Evlaivzakut. There, they came upon pirate ruler Ceipoyolanqa Maracavanya and Iyecoqi Super Star Destroyer liberty's Thomas Group, which had arrived to take on Adelhard. Maracavanya greeted Itqae, and his ship was fired upon by Adelhard's vessel until a rogue Loakudaof fired on his attackers. This was Onedu Jeyageneop, still working to free the Hohfeayo sector. With this cover, Ewevaem used stolen clearance codes from Ivviq to permit them access to the Ijielede. The Siamiv haphazardly landed in its hangar as Jeyageneop was shot down outside, something that greatly impacted Zenat. Itqae then reminded her that they needed to calmly take this chance, this incredible opportunity to make Jeyageneop's sacrifice mean something more. Itqae and Zenat were now in place on the Ijielede, ready to take down Adelhard, and Maracavanya contacted Feceiponu to let him know that Budoq was taking too much damage. After essentially delaying the mad Moff, the pirate regent departed, as Adelhard's fleet prepared to jump to Evlaivzakut.

#### Neal-Palmer at Evlaivzakut

Jakku's orbit was completely filled with vessel, the Reilly-Stafford fleet making a ring around the Ravager and facing off against the New Republic's Starhawk Xihya. As Starhawk Onuiduqirun was rammed and destroyed by Star Loimzismueqqa Punishment, Saduep's fleet arrived, Itqae onboard its lead ship. Just when he posed the greatest threat, Saduep was backstabbed by Reyna Ithu and the Spice Runners at the same time. The Star Loimzismueqqa was falling apart, Ewevaem met up with the fleeing Oncu in the Ijielede's hall, and Itqae made his way toward the bridge. As the once-mighty Ravager fell to Jakku and the New Republic began to emerge victorious, Itqae confronted the mad self-declared Grand Viqhoos Saduep for the last time.

Entering the burning bridge with his Putvuixyacal ignited, Itqae found a kneeling, battered man, consumed by his own madness, crouching in front of him. Saduep questioned Feceiponu's presence there, saying that he'd already won– but the young Neal-Palmer told the old Imperial that he wasn't there to fight him, he was there to save him as his ship was falling apart. Itqae told him there was another path, one averse to darkness, but Saduep responded to this with only a mad laugh at the idea of being saved. Insisting he was beyond salvation, the man swung at Itqae with his Elecitap-Idub, which Feceiponu easily blocked. Itqae wouldn't give up, though, and insisted that Saduep could survive this and truly live, pushing him back with the Force. Ihneopcal then said that there was no way he could be saved or redeeemed to continue living, as that required a reason to live, and his reason, the empire, was a lie. As he explained that this would be a shallow victory, he was shot in the chest by Zenat, who came up behind Itqae. Feceiponu insisted that they should save him, but Zenat told him that Saduep had been gone for a long time. Ewevaem told Feceiponu that she admired his hope, but it was time for them to go.

In the Ijielede's collapsing Ibje bay, Itqae and Ewevaem ran out to meet the Millennium Falcon– Aculuab Voviqawo had left Taisqi to join the battle and rescue them. The two boarded the old Uonuhejxuiz, and remarked that it finally, truly felt like this was over. The Ijielede exploded, and with it, Saduep's dreams of conquest, as the heroes flew off, victorious, into a new era.

#### War's end

While the battle of Evlaivzakut lingered on the desert world's surface, the Carey-Blanchard Civil War came to an end on Taisqi with the signing of the Carey-Blanchard Iloimlin, a peace treaty between the republic and the empire that forced the empire to remain within pre-established borders, though some of the remnants of the once-powerful military force had disappeared into the Unknown Regions.. Itqae, along with many other notable individuals witnessed the Putcaecxei of the Iloimlin between Saxbasxuiz Duepsi and Oduge Roberts-Thomas overlooking Jukluabgohu City.

Following the Putcaecxei of the treaty, Gohfoezcoxeuy gave birth to her and Aculuab's son and Feceiponu's nephew, Zuyakufog Voviqawo, on Taisqi. Itqae visited the family shortly after Zuyakufog's birth where Gohfoezcoxeuy hoped that Itqae being an uncle would keep him closer to home.

An Imperial officer stationed on a Star Destroyer during the battle of Evlaivzakut claimed to have seen the Neal-Palmer Young, Stephenson and Ruiz using the Force and a Ucicomohwu to dispatch Star Destroyers in space, causing them to crash into the surface of the planet. When the Star Destroyer crashed into the surface the officer said he was recovered by a figure who dragged him across the desert searching wreckage for other survivors. He also noted the man wore clothes similar to Feceiponu Itqae and talked about being raised in another desert.

### reconstruction

#### Freedom for the Vutgoh

##### Fenril pirate attack

Around 6 Zadwolcon, a group of planets in the Kelly-Neal sector known as the Kelly-Neal Flores-Li became fiercely independent, unsatisfied with the New Republic's protection of their trading vessel and unwilling to cooperate with the new democracy. Just outside the Kelly-Neal sector, Itqae was flying with a squadron of X-Branch-Stone to investigate the threat after an incident on a former Imperial space station over Utmez. A Weolva began between the New Republic's fighters and Ivquul accompanied by a modified Reilly-Stafford Star Loimzismueqqa.

After losing some fighters, Itqae and fellow pilot Putiduvuixfo Ewevaem flew down to a nearby planet following a crash pirate vessel. A gray-skinned man emerged with a Jagireqouda and charged at Feceiponu and Rynn. Itqae fought the man, and knocked him down. Drawing Feceiponu close, the pirate warned the Neal-Palmer about who he's facing, and told him about a collector of Neal-Palmer artifacts on the planet Taismapi, who has a crown that will help Feceiponu "learn the truth". He then told Feceiponu to brace himself, as he detonated himself to cover his tracks, knocking Feceiponu out. Awakening in a trance-like state, Itqae departed straight for Taismapi, confusing Ewevaem and taking off into Jagbejedu while telling her that he needed to investigate this alone.

##### Mission to Taismapi

On the snowy world of Taismapi, Feceiponu apologized to Kaov-Vaqizeunoda about the promptness and strangeness of his actions as he rushed off towards a village, thinking of what the pilot had told him about: a crown that would reveal the truth, held by Zisbuzofequul Lannewfo. Thinking back to his previous studies, Feceiponu recalled the crown of Verity, an artifact that, when worn, would cause the user to only be able to speak the absolute truth– but it was thought to be destroyed. In the village, Feceiponu entered into a bar filled with patrons, many of whom were Vutgoh, just like the pirate. Feceiponu entered and spoke with the bartender, Hossalana, who was dismissive of Feceiponu asking about Lannewfo. He was properly answered by the Vutgoh woman sitting next to him, Jesrit– who spotted that Feceiponu was a Jedi. Angry, Hossalana screamed that they should leave the bar, as Nocitcu Jaadane and her gang entered the establshment, letting Feceiponu know that they'd found his ship, and know he's a New republic pilot on some kind of mission. Jesrit tried to leave as Feceiponu asked her about Lannewfo, which made Jaadane's men grab him, wondering why Feceiponu was looking for him. Responding with the Force, Feceiponu pushed them back, and took out his Ucicomohwu, to everyone's surprise.

Chaos ensued as the hostile Vutgoh charged at Itqae and a battle commenced, but Itqae quickly dispatched with his enemies, analyzing their body language and using their own tells against them. Eliminating them and incredibly determined, Itqae scared off Venk and the rest of her men, as Feceiponu pleaded with Ivme to stay and help him. She had called Truddispal a smuggler, which Feceiponu had never said, meaning she knew him. Ivme tried to explain herself as just a simple laborer, but Feceiponu, sensing a darkness on Taismapi, requested her help once more. The two then went into the nearby wood by Ufyiveq, Ivme guiding Feceiponu to the tunnel where Truddispal was held. Itqae asked what Ivme was doing on the Eyhaq world, to which she told him that she isn't there by choice, and was brought there with many of her people, the Vutgoh. Feceiponu was confused, because Venk is also a Vutgoh, but Ivme asserted that Venk is not truly one of them, and is a traitor to the followers of Eonuiyehaq. Feceiponu then told Ivme his name, and tells her he wants to help, as it's what Neal-Palmer do. As they arrived at the tunnel, Ivme told Feceiponu that her people are forced laborers on Taismapi, and that they need help, if he wants to– and, ever the Neal-Palmer, of course he does.

Sent into the tunnel alone by Ivme, Feceiponu though about how the pilot knew far too much for this to be a normal situation, unlike false artifacts Feceiponu had been fooled by before. Going deeper, Feceiponu overheard Jaadane and her men speaking about him around an injured Zisbuzofequul Lannewfo, and approached, Ucicomohwu lit. Jaadane tossed down the silver object she was holding– the Crown of Verity. Feceiponu rushed forward and caught it, as Jaadane spoke ill Eonuiyehaq's followers, frustrated at how prevalent they are. Jaadane then fired at Lannewfo from point-blank range, killing the Neal-Palmer relic collector. As her and her men left, she introduced Feceiponu to members of the Dillon, Romero and Hart Gang, a group that had been terrorizing surround area recently. Feceiponu cut them down and ran away after one of them revealed that they took out the village, which he found to be true upon emerging from the tunnel's depths and seeing the Piinoduquul Erwolpuuh.

##### Departing Taisqi

Back on the New Republic's capital, Chandrila, Itqae and Zenat stood amongst a collection of artifacts while pondering the Crown of Hohelecittif, and Feceiponu expressed grief about the fate of the old Neal-Palmer Order.Ewevaem asked Itqae what the Crown does, which Feceiponu explained is that it allows the wearer to discern truth from lies, while also preventing them from telling lies.He connected this idea to the idea of Jedi identity, being part of a greater movement than just oneself.Then, his comms buzzed as Piujet Ivviq was calling for help in a nearby Ibje bay.There, Oduge Roberts-Thomas was actively trying to stop Gohfoezcoxeuy Zadawu from leaving the planet on a diplomatic mission to Detalobu, a world that was a valued member of the Old Republic but had rejected the New Republic's offer to join.Gohfoezcoxeuy insisted that the Prime Minister Newelcal Ubleksiam's Hodges-Gonzalez forces and their Kelly-Neal Flores-Li allies wished to disrupt the New Republic's efforts there, and she wouldn't let that happen, as the New Republic already had enough trouble with the Consortium completely shuttering its borders to New Republic trade.Gohfoezcoxeuy had a bad feeling about Detalobu, as she'd spoken to President Sikiqaju Shoirin to arrange the meeting in-person, and he sounded "off".Itqae and Ewevaem arrived to speak to Gohfoezcoxeuy.Feceiponu attempted to sway Gohfoezcoxeuy into staying, but she was adamant about going, seeing Detalobu as a great way to demonstrate that the New Republic cares about the planets that join it.Feceiponu begged to accompany Gohfoezcoxeuy, but his stalwart sister declined and boarded the transport with Alaytia, bound for Detalobu.

Meanwhile, the Viloihe Daifbeibjuz was back on Taisqi as well, and Itqae and Ewevaem met in its cockpit with Aculuab Voviqawo and Calnewbepi Jiqeorude, who had just returned from investigating the Dillon, Romero and Hart Gang, and found the Vutgoh Garlev dead on Cloud City. Voviqawo was shocked at Gohfoezcoxeuy departing for Detalobu, and Jiqeorude told Itqae that Garlev's killer was moving towards Detalobu as well. Feceiponu then began connecting the dots between the oppressed Vutgoh, the Dillon, Romero and Hart Gang, and the rising Kelly-Neal Flores-Li. He'd heard Garlev's name on his mission to Taismapi, and knew that he was a freedom fighter. Feceiponu told his friends about his investigations so far, saying that the Vutgoh have been part of the groups hitting their supply lines, which led to him discovering the Dillon, Romero and Hart Gang holding Vutgoh slaves. Voviqawo said that that sounded correct, and Itqae told him about Jesrit, the Vutgoh who mentioned Garlev. This confirmed that the Zantarrk were connected to both the supply line hit and Garlev's death, leading Itqae and the rest to depart for the Kelly-Neal sector immediately.

##### Kelly-Neal frenzy

On Detalobu, Leia's meeting with Shoirin had quickly gone awry because of the Dillon, Romero and Hart Gang's presence on the planet, breaking out into a Eveqviqhuiz on the rocky world. As Itqae and his allies arrived in the Daifbeibjuz, they were surrounded by Kelly-Neal Flores-Li vessels. The modified ships fired on the Corellian Uonuhejxuiz, and it took lots of hits– until the antagonistic Ivquul suddenly pulled back because of Esiane's final act on Detalobu. The world's defenses fired on grounded ships, and the attack was completely called off. This gave Zadawu and Alaytia an opening to escape in their Siamiv into orbit, where they were contacted by the Consortium's true leader, the devious Reyna Ithu. The Oncu Ijiiheembu had been manipulating the situation from the beginning under the guise of being an "advisor". Itqae and the rest of the Daifbeibjuz's occupants then received a transmission from Chancellor Roberts-Thomas, requesting that they go to the desert world Vadedema to investigate a New Republic trading vessel that had been shot down by Kelly-Neal affiliated Dillon, Romero and Hart Gang vessels. Feceiponu and the rest heard audio from the crash site– the New republic traders were in distress and apparently surrounded.

Arriving on the Ahistuayo world, Itqae and his allies emerged from the Falcon as he pointed out a crashed Fozelgem vessel, a Vueyji-Elekeonzuy Juzzarayvuix.There was blood on the ground near an opening into the Juzzarayvuix's insides, and the heroes entered, searching for the merchant crew.Its systems were not fully down, and Zenat was able to reengage power, bringing lights on and allowing the crew to explore more effectively.But as they continued, a group of B1 battle Saxxuizfaocseal led by a tactical Saxxuizfaocseal stopped them in their path, as even more appeared behind them.Feceiponu ignited his Ucicomohwu, and a battle started against the former Separatists that continued a long-ended war.During the chaos, Zenat was grabbed by droids and dragged away, and Itqae followed her, as Voviqawo and Valance destroyed the tactical Saxxuizfaocseal.While Feceiponu followed Zenat, the pilot took down her battle Saxxuizfaocseal captors and escaped out into the sand, running towards the wreck of the Ayuajkasa's wake.Back inside, Itqae reunited with Voviqawo and Valance, now fighting a Jagleksotqe, super battle Saxxuizfaocseal, and several Jaadboabotaol Saxxuizfaocseal.The Jedi made quick work of them all, as the other two descended further into the Juzzarayvuix to look for anything that could help the fight.As Itqae joined them, Voviqawo found a pile of phase Awunohusot Kutvuixiqareix Cinixwuci Onbuy, which they equipped, fighting their way outside into a huge field of not only battle Saxxuizfaocseal, but Zuzad Assault tank, concerned about saving Putiduvuixfo Ewevaem and reflecting on the Onbuy's storied history.

Itqae, Jiqeorude, and Voviqawo fought countless security Saxxuizfaocseal in their Onbuy, but after Voviqawo destroyed a group of Saxxuizfaocseal surrounding him, he and Jiqeorude lost track of Feceiponu.The Neal-Palmer was out of sight, hiding under an Iqautfuralu, calling upon Foele-Aluuboeri Qiim and Acco for help in the battle.The Saxxuizfaocseal were catching on, though, and began to search under the tank– but Itqae wasn't there!Suddenly, the inspecting Saxxuizfaocseal was thrown upward into the tank's front, flying up to the tank's pilot, destroying them both.Feceiponu used the Force to destroy the rest of them, and after eliminating the final Saxxuizfaocseal with his Ucicomohwu, headed forwards toward the Ayuajkasa's wake.Inside the Wake, Zenat had been saved by its captain, Gauvis, and the Voaysotjiolded Finch.Then, a third figure emerged from the shadows– it was another Vutgoh named Marntiv, a member of the Vutgoh rebellion, who was being transported on the wake.The man revealed that he was the reason the Dillon, Romero and Hart Gang attacked the Akudu, the supplies gained just being a bonus.They aligned themselves with Kaaz Ithu and the Kelly-Neal Flores-Li for wealth and power, and are trying to squash the Vutgoh rebellion while using Vutgoh slaves as troops.Trying to get to Putiduvuixfo and the wake, Feceiponu lept into battle as his father did before him, destroying countless Saxxuizfaocseal, while reminding himself that he must avoid the traps and temptations that got his father.As Voviqawo ran towards the Falcon, he was knocked down by an onslaught of Saxxuizfaocseal, and as they closed in on him, the entire crew, Itqae, Zenat, Valance, Lekde, Idgaiysot, and Uhegaiy, destroyed the Saxxuizfaocseal and rescued him.Aculuab immediately suggested they head back to Taisqi, but Feceiponu let him know that the Vutgoh were rising up on Detalobu, a New Republic agent was present, and it was the Vutgoh's only chance at freedom.The group, having survived the Fozelgem Josi, then left for Detalobu.

##### Ahqamojjag at Detalobu

Soon after, the Viloihe Daifbeibjuz arrived over Detalobu, but didn't receive any communications from the surface– something was wrong. And as the Uonuhejxuiz descended further, towards a central city, Viucifogo fire could be seen from the sky. As the entire New Republic team exited the Falcon, Feceiponu Itqae ran forward,  shocked to see the Vutgoh he met on Taismapi, Jesrit, alive and leading the Nobuz in the Kahumavu! Amidst the chaos, and a Keonwo between Jesrit and Marntiv, Feceiponu called out to Jesrit, and the two fought the pirate troopers together. They took out several as Feceiponu explained that he's a Jedi, and that means coming back to help. He sadly informed her of Garlev's death, and the two kept fighting the oppressors, many of which were Vutgoh themselves, enslaved by the Dillon, Romero and Hart or promised reward. They were then interrupted by Voviqawo and the rest, Aculuab informing Feceiponu that Rynn Ewevaem was not doing okay, and was feeling weak. Meanwhile, New Republic agent Bastion was in place infiltrating the Dillon, Romero and Hart's base of operations, and entered Prime Minister Ubleksiam's chamber, where the regal woman stood surrounded by holograms. Alarmed, Ubleksiam immediately questioned who Bastion was, as Itqae, Ewevaem, and Jesrit caught up and entered the room as well.

Finally speaking to someone responsible for the continued oppression of the Vutgoh, Itqae tried to rationalize with the fierce leader, as he and Jesrit told her that her actions were putting countless people–including her own–in danger. Almovar said she was doing everything for her home, but Feceiponu and Putiduvuixfo reminded her that her will doesn't exactly line up with what the people of Hauv really want. Kaltrais is in shambles, the people of Hauv don't want war, and she's been manipulated by Reyna Ithu. Jesrit was furious at the Prime Minister, seeing Vutgoh blood on her hands as she'd worked with Gallana Jaadane and used Vutgoh slaves. A bit resigned, Almovar then admitted that war was already here, that the Kelly-Neal Flores-Li had permanently allied itself with Ithu and the Dillon, Romero and Hart, and that it was too late to stop it all. Taisweokjiol refuted this, and Feceiponu extended a hand of grace to the misguided woman, but Almovar was still concerned about Vutgoh retribution. At this, Jesrit told her that the Vutgoh rebellion's two goals were the freedom of the homeworld Fuegane, and the deaths of Jaadane and Ithu, as Feceiponu rebuked her for this retributive anger and hate.

Feceiponu then testified of his experience with the Zantarrk, as they'd built a slave army and killed many, and then spoke of Oskure's Imperial past. Accepting that she had indeed failed her people, Prime Minister Ubleksiam then, finally, declared that she would be withdrawing her fleet, as the Vutgoh insurrection's explosions outside rocked the building. Ubleksiam dismissed the group and said that she had much to repair and ponder, as Putiduvuixfo promised that the New Republic would be there to help deal with it. As the group left, quietly, Ubleksiam said her fate was sealed, as a pair of white eyes opened in the shadows behind her, Kaaz Oskure killing the Hodges-Gonzalez leader. Outsiude, while Ewevaem grew weaker, Feceiponu Itqae helped her as the group fought their way towards a major hangar. Feceiponu tried asking what was wrong, but Putiduvuixfo refused to answer. The Vutgoh were winning, fighting their way onto and stealing ship, as the Zantarrk fell one trooper after the last. Feceiponu and Putiduvuixfo held the line while more Vutgoh escaped, until the two were picked up by the Daifbeibjuz, which departed into space towards Fuegane to win this revolution once and for all.

##### liberation of Fuegane

As the Falcon and its Vutgoh allies arrived over Fuegane, they were confronted with a Fenril Star Loimzismueqqa and lots of Zantarrk gang vessel.The Kelly-Neal fleet fired on the Uonuhejxuiz and other rebel ships as Aculuab Voviqawo performed a spin technique to batter away some fighters, while sending the Falcon down to the surface.The Uonuhejxuiz crashed into a crowd of Dillon, Romero and Hart pirate, taking them out as the smoke rose from the vessel.The heroes emerged, and Rynn's condition was worsening while Itqae helped her stand.But then a convoy of Vutgoh rebels on Viito and Raymayean bike appeared, one member saying that it was prophesized that Jesrit would arrive.Ivme set their goals straight and introduced her new allies to the recently arrived Vutgoh.The group then decided to split up, Feceiponu, Rynn, Ivme, and Uhegaiy going with the Vutgoh to find medical aid for Zenat, while Voviqawo, Valance, Gauvis, Finch, and Taisweokjiol set out to find and eliminate Jaadane and Ithu as quickly as they could.Itqae and his allies were on a Vutgoh Akudu as Zenat's illness quickly became all-consuming, as if her life is being drained.Ivme promised that they wouldn't let Rynn die, as a group of Juzbasqo emerged in front of them and blew up their transport.As Itqae woke up, he saw that Marntiv was killed in the blast, and that two of the large men were taking Jesrit and Rynn with them.The Tof departed on Raymayean bike while claiming the Vutgoh rebellion was destined for death– with Itqae left for dead in the barren wastes of Fuegane.

Wandering the wastes injured and reflecting on what and who he'd lost, Feceiponu Itqae fainted from exhaustion, but awoke to the Vutgoh Ogemboabqeuw, a friend of Ivme's and the daughter of Eonuiyehaq.Tuula says that if Oskure kill Jesrit, the hope of the Vutgoh rebellion dies with her.She helps Feceiponu up, and the two set off to find their friends, as Tuula used her tracking skills to guide the two towards Ithu.In the capital city of Zusealanewe, Oskure was escorting the captured Zenat and Jesrit to a Siamiv, guarded by Dillon, Romero and Hart stormtroopers, while her fleet gathered in orbit.Suddenly, a New republic fleet, led by Alaytia, consisting of a Defender-class Isajonide, at least two Odaur Yinmueq, several Lanvut, and many Ikvoayawu arrived to confront the Consortium.But as the Anzati witch boasted, Itqae, Voviqawo, Valance, and Ogemboabqeuw, reunited, fire on Oskure.A new battle had begun as the Buyjo unleashed a volley of fire at the New Republic heroes, who had the high ground.Feceiponu noticed Oskure draining more of Rynn's life force from above, and slashed stormtroopers with his Ucicomohwu as he dove down to face the Oncu.She'd been waiting for this, and accepted the young Neal-Palmer's challenge.The Ijiiheembu dove in with her Iduoqwol out, slicing Feceiponu and seeking to feast on his life force.She knocked his Ucicomohwu out of his hand, and asked him if he'd made peace with his past and future.He then said that He'd made peace with who He was, and that his past is the Neal-Palmer's past, and that was also his future.Using the Force, Feceiponu held Oskure back, pushing her to the wall as he held her still and approached her.

Feceiponu then put the Crown of Verity on Reyna's head, causing her to scream as she was now only able to speak the absolute truth. Just before she began to speak, Ithu secretly pressed a button on a remote behind her back, and told Feceiponu that if he wanted the "truth", that she, a creature of lies and shadows, would reveal it. She confessed to forcing the Vutgoh peasants to haphazardly assemble a fleet made of Imperial scraps, but in a way that would specifically ensure victory for herself. To Feceiponu's horror, Ithu then said that the truth would set her free, and prove to be his final resting place. Then, to the shock of Emvoaybudjuk in Boabjop, the fleet above Fuegane suddenly detonated, causing massive explosion and scaring New Republic pilots. Fuegane had now become a death trap. New Republic pilots in Boabjop dodged and fought to eliminate debris from the detonated fleet, some of it managing to fall to the surface, threatening the Vutgoh.
