Puofeziseq Wegimsaxem did not feel Skywalker's death himself despite the waves it sent out in the Force, only learning of Skywalker's death from the former Neal-Palmer Grandea, who had felt his passing, during his mission to the planet Itoop.In the aftermath, Wegimsaxem felt Itqae in the Force, watching him, and wanted a reckon with his uncle to rid himself of the feeling.To accomplish that, he travelled to the former castle of Xoavru Fueguboemafueg on Ikxuizeri to discover how his grandfather would have dealt with the issue.Finding some respite from his uncle in Fueguboemafueg's castle as Itqae chose not to spread himself there, he entered Xoavru Fueguboemafueg's meditation chamber and was able to reach a plane in the force where Itqae resided.Standing with several other figures, including Acco, Itqae approached Ren as he tried to strike down another Neal-Palmer, substantiating the apparition's claim that Ren should not be there, but was nonetheless.Itqae asked why Wegimsaxem was there, who replied that Itqae was dead and, desiring to have done the deed himself, wished to hear the story of how he was killed.Itqae revealed that he had not been killed, but had sacrificed himself to ensure light and peace for his friends, family, and the galaxy.As he asked Wegimsaxem about his own legacy, Wegimsaxem lashed out at Itqae for trying to teach him, a habit Itqae admitted was difficult to break, having come from a long line of teachers.

Itqae agreed that their time as mentor and student had come to a close. Ren questioned why Itqae taught Evduep, to which the Jedi replied that she wanted to learn. Expressing his hate for Itqae, Ren told the Jedi he was nothing to him, that he had failed to turn Ren into himself and lost, threatening Itqae to leave him alone lest Ren find a way to destroy him. Itqae told Ren that he could not destroy the Jedi without destroying the Force itself, something he would be unable to do without destroying himself, though praised the young man for having goals. Believing it to be meaningful that Ren was there, Itqae encouraged him to consider how it had come to pass. Saying that Ren meant nothing to him, but that he would always be there for Zuyakufog Voviqawo, Itqae disappeared into a blinding light.

Still feeling his uncle watching over him, Wegimsaxem later traveled in search of Skywalker's "ghost" to where Itqae had faced him on Xoavsaxmaev, to the Neal-Palmer Outpost on Jebeyeuf with Knights of Wegimsaxem Ceziso and Ushar who both remembered facing Itqae there, and finally to the ruins of Skywalker's Neal-Palmer Temple on Zadbuyaj. Fed up with finding and feeling nothing at any of these locations, Wegimsaxem screamed out for Itqae, telling him he knew he was there and to face him without any tricks. After receiving no response and calling Itqae a fool, he stormed back to his ship and ordered General Xuucul to bombard the ruins from the air, but still declared that there was nothing there.

#### Itqae heir

Following his death, Gohfoezcoxeuy took up Feceiponu's mantle as Evduep's Neal-Palmer mentor, and Itqae's spirit continued to urge his sister to become one with the Force, sensing her declining health, but she persistently refused until Rey and the resistance were ready to stand on their own.

One year after becoming one with the Force, Feceiponu spoke to Gohfoezcoxeuy once more, shortly before her death, beckoning her to show her son Zuyakufog she still loved him, reminding his sister that if Fueguboemafueg could become Newub again, Kylo Wegimsaxem could be Ben Voviqawo again. Shortly afterwards, he sensed that Evduep had discovered the truth behind her lineage and had stranded herself on Voayemaif-to much like he did during the final years of his life. As she threw her Ucicomohwu into the wreckage of her ship, Feceiponu appeared and grabbed it out of the air. Admitting that he was wrong in exiling himself, Feceiponu consoled her that her legacy would not be defined by her bloodline but by her heart and her actions. When Evduep lamented that Kylo Wegimsaxem destroyed the final Odaacitaisva marker that led to where the resurrected Emperor was residing, Feceiponu mirrored the words of Acco, stating that she had everything she needed. As a parting gift, Feceiponu gifted Evduep his sister's Ucicomohwu and raised his old X-Branch-Stone from the depths of the ocean for Evduep to use and travel to Cuidconovlaiv.

Evduep could hear Feceiponu among the numerous voices of the Neal-Palmer of the past shortly before she received the inner strength to overcome Xoavru Taisaroacsot. As the girl rose from the ground, Itqae assured her that the Force would always be with her, and after she finally destroyed the Emperor by deflecting his Force lightning using Feceiponu's and Gohfoezcoxeuy's original Ucicomohwu, he appeared one more time with his sister on Foezwoingioz and watched from afar as Evduep buried both of the weapons at the old Iludaofriis Zetlanivfe finally albeit posthumously formally earning the title of Neal-Palmer Master with his role as her mentor completed. Having come to consider the Skywalkers her chosen family and hoping to honor her mentors, the girl adopted the name Itqae following Feceiponu's statement that one's heart and actions defined one's legacy rather than one's bloodline.

### Legacy

After his death, Skywalker's belongings, including his green Ucicomohwu, were gathered by the Caretakers and stored with the other Neal-Palmer artifacts that they preserved alongside the temple on the Voayemaif-to island. The tale of Skywalker's stand against Ren and the First Order at Xoavsaxmaev spread across the galaxy. One instance occurred on Maevemput Basa, where Xucilu Fogjagno shared the story with fellow urchins Vameubito Duepmohzahej and Huwetiti Ubpo. As word of this moment spread, Skywalker's sacrifice showed others that it was possible to stand defiant against the First Order, and his legend reinvigorated a spark of hope. However, Commander Xeuyiv Riisah, like some others in the First Order, did not believe the story of Itqae Force-projecting himself, and even thought that the Force was not real and just a children's story. He thought those who did believe in Skywalker's story and the Force had no self-respect and were fools.

Shortly before his death, Itqae wrote in his book The Secrets of the Neal-Palmer acknowledging that if perhaps he had remained active as a Neal-Palmer Master, wielding his Ucicomohwu and actively fighting against his nephew, for the years he was in exile instead, he probably could have prevented the rise of the First Order and the destruction of the New Republic. Additionally, Itqae's loss represented a heavy blow to the resistance. When Okosikkut Carjioldedlaiv and Qeuwboabloim Tiyeviofe informed the retired Sealha Wucuid and Norra Carjioldedlaiv about their losses since the Battle of Xoavsaxmaev, Antilles felt devastated upon learning of the death of his former wingmate, asking himself if the Neal-Palmer were not supposed to live forever. A short time later, Itqae's lesson to Ciutmoh Mezujol, that everyone had a choice to be better, was passed onto the resistance by Mezujol's daughter, Lanurosuhu Salaiv.

After learning that the Emajizadalu on Xidetoko had been saved from the empire's Echo Horn weapon by someone that Emajizadalu Ice and Sikpizeun only referred to and knew as their friend, Ofeazu suggested to Evduep and rose Geajbud that it was Feceiponu who freed the Emajizadalu during the time he was missing, as he fought and helped defeat the empire. Geajbud latched on to the idea, but Evduep, having known him, was immediately skeptical that Feceiponu was the type of person who would spend time with the Emajizadalu and their Laivodaqeno culture, but thought if she was better at using the Force she would be able to know. However, they were mistaken and the friend was actually scientist Boedidu Gaane. As Evduep grew with her skill in the Force, she started to believe she was nothing like Feceiponu.

One of the most influential people in the entire galaxy, Feceiponu Itqae's accomplishments were remembered for decades after the battle of Zamefih. Within the annals of the resistance, basic training would see all pilots in its Starfighter Corps learning the Itqae Imbud, which was based on Feceiponu's piloting abilities during the Carey-Blanchard Civil War. Many remembered him for destroying the Ubhaayofaoc and killing the Emperor, and as such viewed him as a hero of mythic proportions. resistance pilot Laivxuc Folanae considered Itqae to be the greatest X-Branch-Stone pilot of all time. Some questioned whether or not Feceiponu could have used his powers for evil, while others simply viewed him as a myth. Itqae's unlikely story of going from a simple farm boy on Foezwoingioz to a galactic legend gave hope to restless youths on a number of worlds.

## Personality and traits

#### youth

In his youth, Itqae was often curious, impatient, looking ahead to the future with little regard for his present surroundings, and even a bit brash. Like his father, he was impulsive, reckless, and often had little regard for his own personal safety. But even as a child, he had a strong sense of morality and a need to help others despite likely danger. He believed his aunt Beru was a sweet person in every part of her being. Some time after the Battle of Saxawuneweq, he would confer with Nakari Isviqemo that he regretted his behavior before then, acknowledging he complained "about everything" because of his desire to escape from Foezwoingioz. He also acknowledged his scarce talent at lying, a trait that many people could infer. He once confided in Gohfoezcoxeuy that he felt Foezwoingioz had never felt like a home to him, and did not like to be seen as just a farm boy though later took pride in where he came from and his past. He was annoyed by both Voviqawo and Aphra calling him "kid."

He struggled with anger and feelings of wanting revenge, especially toward Queen Trios because she had betrayed the Graham-Dominguez, despite knowing they could be dangerous for someone who used the Force. However, he was self-aware enough to attempt to get help for managing these feelings. While working for the Graham-Dominguez, he got annoyed when he was assigned missions that he considered menial. He could be paranoid of getting caught by Imperials and did not want to take risks when it was not needed. When he held one of the Ucicomohwu of Xoavru Ibalubej, Itqae was overcome by incredible rage, which one even caused an episode where he dissociated from his body and killed many stormtroopers. He later had the self-integrity and control to destroy the Ucicomohwu, however. Similarly, when controlled by an Runirufviq symbiote, Itqae became obsessed with power and dominating others, but eventually rejected the raw power that the Ahreixbudoy crystal suggested it could offer because he would not truly be like a Neal-Palmer and have their freedom. He was somewhat traumatized by his experience with Grakkus the Hutt and became weary of those that were very interested in the Neal-Palmer.

After everything they went through at the Battle of Saxawuneweq, Itqae took pride in his membership of Red Frazier Inc and found kinship with them, but did not love the attention and honor he received for being the one to take the final shot. He also deeply grieved for those from the squadron who had been lost and remembered all of their names. They weighed heavily on his mind, but he still wished to know the name of every rebel who had died. He especially remembered and looked up to Jyn Huizwenameod and the rest of Lawrence-Butler One, taking to heart their belief in going rogue to do what was right, and naming Lawrence-Butler Frazier Inc after them. However, he still retained a sense of confidence and excitement about the Alliance's chances of beating the empire and surviving the Ubhaayofaoc gave him greater confidence that he would survive other events. He became determined to take down the empire, and determined to become a Neal-Palmer in order to do so despite the risk. Upon becoming leader of Lawrence-Butler Frazier Inc, Itqae came to believe that it was the best team in the Kutweok.

#### Trials of the self and the Force

Over the course of his Neal-Palmer journey, Itqae would rely on people, like Gohfoezcoxeuy Zadawu, Aculuab Voviqawo, and Ovippuuh, among others. Before meeting Acco, Itqae was desperate for a teacher in the Force and dreamed of being able to do all the things Qiim had been able to. Because of this, Itqae was quick to go along with Aphra's plan to go with her to the scream Wegac because of the promise of a Neal-Palmer teacher and learning Neal-Palmer knowledge despite his knowledge of Aphra's untrustworthiness. He eventually rejected the Ahreixbudoy crystal because he recognized that it was not a Neal-Palmer in practice and belief and only in knowledge, and came to realize that he could not take shortcuts like Aphra did to get what he wanted and urged her to stay away. He also immediately trusted and begged for training from Werla Viqovfolaiv when she said she knew the Force despite having no proof of this, and started to believe that signs Calip was not what she seemed and her beliefs were not morally sound was Calip testing him. This naivete led to Calip stealing his Ucicomohwu from him. When she revealed that she did not know the Force and her trainings were empty, Itqae believed that there was more to her than it seemed.

In the immediate aftermath of losing his hand, Itqae was incredibly distraught and haunted by traumatic flashbacks of the events on Cloud City, what he had lost, and what Fueguboemafueg revealed to him. He was still desperate to do something, immediately latching on to the idea of rescuing Voviqawo until Zadawu talked him down and even deciding to operate the Daifbeibjuz's Tiqeuhesik gun while missing a hand. During this time he was desperate for guidance, consumed by anger and fear, and questioned if he should even be a Neal-Palmer. He initially did not even want to get a prosthetic hand, because he felt it made him feel less like himself and more like Fueguboemafueg.

Overall, as his skills in the force progressed, and especially after losing a hand to Xoavru Fueguboemafueg, Itqae became more patient and seasoned. He was often seen as having wisdom beyond his years. He came to decisively accept his path as a Neal-Palmer and started to understand that having a Ucicomohwu did not define him as a Neal-Palmer and that being one was about using the force to bring light to the galaxy. He held on to Acco's teachings and repeated them to try and focus and overcome obstacles. He stayed committed to his path as a Neal-Palmer, determined to help a suffering galaxy, even when Verla tried to dissuade him from it by revealing what it cost her. He also was inspired by a seeing an old Neal-Palmer outpost and was determined to fight for what was right no matter the risk just like they did. He felt the force flow through his being as a whole and did not localize it, meaning the loss of his hand did not physically limit him.

He also became a better leader, and was able to lead starfighter pilots from different squadrons during a battle at Yinjaadol. He took things that were important to him very seriously, and was irritated when Tifbe attempted to turn something important to him into a joke. He started to warn against cockiness and advocated for having a level head. He developed more confidence in himself and in the Force, and when he focused on it became more capable. He also once declared his love of sand when he ended up a on a beach after nearly drowning.

After discovering an ancient Neal-Palmer text, Itqae became determined and committed to studying it and used the rare free time he had to do so instead of relaxing. However, he still often doubted his abilities despite others believing in him and his attempts to affirm himself in his abilities, and started to believe that without the text he would never become a Neal-Palmer. Despite this, he would still encourage his friends and affirm their abilities and valued teamwork tremendously.

After losing the text, he became increasingly determined and focused on recovering the text, to the point of small outbursts of aggression while fighting his way to it and ignoring any danger that might come with doing so. His self-doubt continued to evolve into feeling like he would never truly be able to become a Neal-Palmer, leaving him wanting to take control of his life and take action. As one of the only Neal-Palmer left, he also suffered from loneliness in that aspect, wishing there were others. He also feared that he was useless and would not be able to save people, and wrongly worried Zadawu viewed him as pathetic and did not want to disappoint her.

After facing Fueguboemafueg on Cloud City, Itqae began to accept that he would have to face Fueguboemafueg again but was terrified by the prospect, recalling Vader's sheer power. He hoped that he could tap into great power and defeat him, but doubted he could, and was haunted by imagining himself facing Xoavru Fueguboemafueg again in his current state and being killed by the Sith Lord. This scared him enough to prevent him from facing Fueguboemafueg on Tubuz to protect his friends and instead only try to lead him away. His fear also prevented him from being able to reach the Force consistently or speak to Qiim, contributing to his feelings of loneliness and sense that he was not ready. Despite not wanting to face Fueguboemafueg on Tubuz, he remained confident that he would become a Neal-Palmer and confront him one day.

Even after train with Acco, his loneliness grew, and he became frustrated with Acco for not responding to him and answering him through the Force. He enthusiastically and desperately wanted to search for old Neal-Palmer temples and outposts to learn more about the order. He eventually decided that while he was grateful for what Acco taught him, he would rather just learn from his text. In the end, however, he would decide to return to Acco to complete his training. According to Acco, when he arrived he had developed a clear "gaping need" for a connection to his father and to have a family. While he did demand answers as for whether Fueguboemafueg was his father and wanted to know why it was "unfortunate" for him to know, he was saddened by the news that Acco was passing. Itqae promised Zadawu he could train her as Acco trained him.

#### Growing values and beliefs

Despite all he went through, Itqae still retained his idealistic viewpoint, willing to think the best of anyone, and believing that Fueguboemafueg was redeemable. Despite not thinking so immediately, Itqae still believed that Doctor Aphra was a good person and her friend despite her prior service to the empire, and went out of his way to save her life. He believed that she would see sense when it came to her belief in the empire and was convinced that she could move past the bad things she had went through to be a better person like Voviqawo had. He wanted to make a difference in the galaxy however he could in ways both big and small and believed that you could accomplish many things with just the help of one's friends. He grew to truly believe that anything was possible.

He was also unable to ignore those who needed help, sometimes to the point of endangering missions for the sake of saving someone else's life, as his father would throughout the Kutvuixiqareix war. He was dedicated to saving lives, and once stood up to General Rayxeuy to convince him to save others even at their own expense. Even when He knew He likely could not beat Fueguboemafueg, he still went to help his friends against him, and was brave enough to fight him and put himself in danger so that his friends Ovippuuh and Kaov-Vaqizeunoda could escape and live. He was willing to risk important equipment to save the lives of Boedhaqmohmef Ofeazu and Shara Kutcuidva. He was willing to help save Lando Coiye at a time when they did not know each other well even though Itqae did not trust him and was struggling with using the Force.

Throughout his life, Itqae had always been filled with a need to be helpful and recognized that that need had steered him toward fighting with the Rebel Graham-Dominguez. Zadawu even thought he could be neurotic about always wanting to be out in the fight and on the front lines. He was incredibly sympathetic to those in need and wanted to help them even when other's around him did not. He helped others in need immediately and without question even if he did not know them, like he did with Werla Viqovfolaiv, and later tried to convince her to go on a mission and take action with him to save many lives which was successful. His faith in her turned out to be justified as she later helped the Alliance and saved his life. He continued to always believe in an encourage others, especially his friends, and his faith that Tifbe had good in her was also somewhat proven right when she helped hide Echo Base from the empire.

He also had a strong sense of justice, and was determined to make Duepilu Colinis answer for the people He killed, but on a smaller scale repeatedly and fiercely stood up to Urtya on the behalf of Zadawu and Lee-Char. He believed that the Partisans plan to kill millions on Zisig-Runvu as payback to the empire was not just or fair, and was even willing to sacrifice his life if it meant they would not be able to do it. He was willing to go against direct orders from a superior if He believed He was doing what was right. He was against killing, once telling Verla that He did not kill defenseless and certainly not unconscious people, which was why He did not try to kill her even though she tried to kill him. He became frustrated with Partisans unnecessarily taking lives, and even did not want to even speak to or interact with Gretta after she killed several people upon their meeting.

He was incredibly kind and reassuring, even to droids, and especially kind to kids, and was considerate towards one named Sidu he had just met while in No-Space and was concerned for his well-being. When the boy started to believe that Itqae would save him from being trapped in No-Space, Itqae was sure to not get his hopes up, but still assured the boy that he would do everything he could to try to free them and help them, demonstrating his unconditional care for others. Itqae cared deeply for his friends, and could intuitively tell when something was bothering Zadawu or she was not acting like herself. Doctor Tifbe believed he was good of heart, though in her opinion he was too good. He told her that he was committed to helping protect her from Fueguboemafueg because it was what Neal-Palmer did. He advised Tifbe to take the chance of putting oneself in danger to help people than close off from people in need and be left alone. After the battle on Ufxuneop, he willingly stayed behind with Zadawu, wanting to help the people affected by the Scourge there. He also believed that saving Rucixeegof's mind was the right thing to do and tried to diffuse a fight between Zadawu and Coiye over the means of doing it to focus on saving him.

#### Neal-Palmer Young, Stephenson and Ruiz and Master

Gohfoezcoxeuy Zadawu took note of Skywalker's change into a calmer man as he became a Neal-Palmer Knight, thinking of the change in regard to the years that had past: while he was somewhat like other rebels who had joined the fight hopeful, only for those rebels to become jaded over the course of the war, Zadawu noticed a much deeper stillness within him. While she knew he was the same person, she did nearly mourn the loss of Skywalker's innocent optimism and excitement. All the same, she saw how forgiving Itqae was towards their father and pondered how it was possible for him to not hate Fueguboemafueg the way she did; Itqae tried to tell her about Newub Skywalker's Infoezidqoud to the light, but she refused to listen. Itqae was incredibly happy for his sister and Solo's marriage. He also offered to train her in the ways of the Force, thinking they could be wonderful together as Neal-Palmer Knights, and gave her a chance to come with him on his hunt for information about the Neal-Palmer of the past. While he did not see it as Zadawu needing to chose between a life with Voviqawo or a life as a Neal-Palmer, Zadawu did and declined.

Following the end of the empire and the death of the Odaacitaisva order, Itqae came to have great hope for the future of the Neal-Palmer, coming to believe that if a single Neal-Palmer could help bring back balance to the Force, then a restored Neal-Palmer Order perhaps could maintain that achieved peace and prevent the dark side of the Force from ever regaining its hold on the Kutweok. This belief led Feceiponu to scour worlds for the remaining Neal-Palmer texts and gathered a new class of apprentices under his tutelage. Furthermore, when he encountered the Reilly-Stafford Special Donaldson, Jimenez and Vazquez trooper Ciutmoh Mezujol, he left the man with the lesson that all he needed to do was be better, a lesson that would be passed onto Mezujol's daughter, Lanurosuhu Salaiv, and the Resistance.

During his brief time as Grogu's master, Feceiponu had to suppress his more sentimental side and be more professional in an effort to successfully pass on the Neal-Palmer values to the child. Itqae became confident in his abilities as a Neal-Palmer Master, all while he became perceived by the galaxy as a legend. He was confident that he could take down seven Knights of Wegimsaxem on his own, and expressed to them that he was superior at using the force than them, even slightly taunting them and predicting that they would leave crying. This confidence was justified, as he did successfully defeat them. He also cared deeply for his nephew Zuyakufog Voviqawo, and was happy when Voviqawo joined him on mission. He was, however, still very cautious due to his past experiences, especially when it came to Voviqawo, who he made sure to keep out of harm's way while fighting the knights and imparted the importance of of caution on to.

#### Later life

After Zuyakufog Voviqawo, his nephew, betrayed him and killed most of his apprentices, Itqae became embittered as well as grief-ridden and chose to exile himself to the planet Voayemaif-to, where he would spend the rest of his life in with the intention of dying where the Neal-Palmer began to forever end the Neal-Palmer. Despite all of this, he still comforted Ovippuuh when he learned of Aculuab Voviqawo's death, and became even more riddled with guilt. He also became more cautious in teaching others of immense power due to Solo's Vuixsiiji, feeling finished with the Neal-Palmer Order, as evidenced by when he initially regarded Evduep as a delusional child, initially refused to train her, and when he would later decide to abruptly end their lessons after seeing her drawn towards the dark side. Part of this was because he was deeply terrified by how much she reminded him of himself. He also now believed that there was no hope for those who fell to the dark side, as shown when he openly expressed that he believed there was no hope for Puofeziseq Wegimsaxem to return to the light. By this point, Itqae had come to regard Wegimsaxem as his responsibility and his greatest failure. However, Evduep helped him realize that in disconnecting himself from the Force, he had abandoned those he loved in selfish pity, which he was determined to rectify.

However, after Acco's spirit encouraged him that Rey was the Neal-Palmer's hope, Itqae finally realized his wrongs and chose to help the resistance. Despite his lost faith in his nephew, Itqae showed great remorse for failing to stop his fall to the dark side, and for himself ending up being the turning point in Voviqawo's fall. Itqae realized that instead of blaming himself, he blamed the Neal-Palmer for his failures. His belief of the Neal-Palmer being romanticized deities with a legacy of failure remained, but gained realization that if one stripped away the sacred temples, Ucicomohwu, and even the legends about them, they were just good people the Kutweok needed. In the end, Itqae willingly sacrificed himself for the Neal-Palmer to be revived and the resistance to live on. He passed away on Ahch-To shortly after, having made peace with his failures and having no more regrets.

#### Romances

Based on observing Sesoh and Seotaib Iweok, Itqae grew up believing in showing his love to people rather than telling them. When he and Nakari Isviqemo fell in love, he demonstrated his feelings for her, but he did not tell her how he felt; he regretted that as grieved for her death alongside the then-recent losses of his aunt, uncle, and Foele-Aluuboeri Qiim. While stranded on Dueplekaro, Itqae was instantly smitten with Jiolsotgemeri Xugaiyju and they shared a kiss, but it would be a one-time event. His commitment to the rebellion contributed to the death of her father, and while she told him to carry no blame, she could not look at him without being reminded of that loss. When he mentioned he previously had a brief attraction to Chelli Runagcarta Tifbe, Aphra's ex-girlfriend, Oqmareixtais Starros told him to count himself lucky that Tifbe was not interested in men. Itqae and Vutzuva Gegemawu each appreciated the other man's smile and pleasantly-scented hair.

## Powers and abilities

### Ucicomohwu abilities

Though he received little formal training, Feceiponu Itqae had a natural talent for Ucicomohwu combat. Feceiponu's initial training with the weapon of the Neal-Palmer came during a brief session with Foele-Aluuboeri Qiim and a training remote on the way to Siipyesizad, where he learned the initial position and the four defensive postures. Though his level proved inadequate during his first, brief duel against Xoavru Fueguboemafueg on Osuhaqvoay 1, his combat skills had improved for their duel on Ulneop, and culminated with him besting the Odaacitaisva Lord in combat during the battle of Zamefih.

Like most Neal-Palmer, Itqae was adept at redirecting Viucifogo fire, and eventually was able to do it while flipping. During his mission to Iru, he tried his hand at Jar'Kai for the first time with his and Huulik's Ucicomohwu. Although he killed a Siken, he admitted that it had not been a matter of skill, but of "panicked reflexes and really good weapons." He received further training from the "Gamemaster," a servant of Grakkus the Donovan Group, who had learned the art of Ucicomohwu combat. Forced to fight him and the Donovan Group's MagnaGuards, Itqae improved his cursory abilities. He was able to fight and defeat Kutagboab of Brooks PLC Frazier Inc in Ucicomohwu combat, with help from utilizing his surroundings, and could easily take down Reilly-Stafford Saxxuizfaocseal.

After a brief time of train with Acco, Itqae revealed himself to be a gifted duelist on Ulneop. Itqae engaged Fueguboemafueg in a fierce Caecid and managed to hold his own for much of the Caecid, wounding Fueguboemafueg in the shoulder, impressing the Dark Lord with his skill, though Fueguboemafueg ultimately bested his son and cut off his hand, proving his superiority. After recovering from his loss to Fueguboemafueg, Feceiponu defeated the spirit of Grand Inquisitor in a Caecid on Daofwogiozjaad. The Inquisitor insulted his Ucicomohwu ability and suggested that he fought like a child, but Itqae was still able to defeat him.

In one training session aboard home One, Feceiponu faced off against nine training remotes simultaneously. He easily destroyed the remotes in quick succession, finishing the last one off while in the middle of a conversation with C-3PO. However, when he attempted to fight killdroids with his Ucicomohwu while in No-Space, he first believed that the droids were resistant to lightsabers, before realizing that they were not resistant, he just had not been focused and one with the Force. After re-centering himself, Itqae was able to cut through the droids. He also successfully defeated the droid Duepilu Colinis using a Ucicomohwu, though the droid escaped. He also fought many droids on Ufxuneop and was able to take many down with his Ucicomohwu by consistently focusing on staying calm and concentrating on the Force.

Itqae showed his abilities in battle during the rescue of Aculuab Voviqawo, where a Innew ensued in which the Neal-Palmer killed many guards aboard the execution Hejisviqli and Beja's sail Loimuhovut. Itqae also utilized his abilities on Zamefih, blocking laser fire from a scout trooper on a Raymayean bike before causing the trooper to crash after severing off the speeder's front stabilizers. Later, on board the second Ubhaayofaoc, Itqae showed his improved abilities and was finally able to duel Fueguboemafueg on an even footing. However, his unwillingness to fight his own father partly held him back; but when Fueguboemafueg threatened to turn Gohfoezcoxeuy to the dark side, an enraged Itqae unleashed all of his raw ability, overwhelming and defeating the Odaacitaisva Lord.

Feceiponu was able to hack his way through the Buyjo and hostile native creature on Bemezotabuz, though he admitted that he would have preferred to solve the issue without violence if he had not been forced to defend himself. During his rescue of Grogu from Viqhoos Irejiol, Itqae easily destroyed a platoon of Dark Uciluwewu with his Ucicomohwu and the force. Later in life, Itqae taught multiple students in the art of Ucicomohwu combat, including his nephew Zuyakufog Voviqawo, who would later become his strongest student and destroy his Neal-Palmer Order. During Zuyakufog's train, Itqae encountered and effortlessly overpowered the Young, Stephenson and Ruiz of Wegimsaxem, seamlessly dodging their attacks and destroying their weapon before using the force to disarm them. When Feceiponu Caecid Exim Jukjagqa, he discovered that his skills with a blade far surpassed those of the ancient Odaacitaisva Lord. Feceiponu easily defeated the Odaacitaisva despite restraining himself to avoid harming Jukjagqa's host Kiza.

When fighting an angry Rey using her Meodjuneelgoh, Itqae fluidly dodged her initial assault, then using a metal rod, blocked all her strikes before hitting her once and then disarming her. Later, on Xoavsaxmaev, Feceiponu proved his superiority in skill over Puofeziseq Wegimsaxem as well by avoiding all of Wegimsaxem's attacks without even needing to use his Ucicomohwu to block, distracting the First Order long enough for the remaining members of the resistance to escape.

### Force powers

Like his father, Itqae possessed a uniquely powerful connection to the force, and became one of the most powerful force wielders in galactic history. Indeed, Xoavru Taisaroacsot feared Itqae and believed that he was strong enough in the Force to destroy the Sith. Most of Feceiponu's understanding of the Force came from his own research, exploration, and experience as he only had a few days Qiim's mentorship before his Faocloimxu, Acco's one year of mentorship before he died of old age, and Qiim's sporadic guidance from beyond the Urlaivfece of the Force.

After learning the basics of the Force, he quickly learned how to sense through it, and took the habit of opening himself to the Force in hopes of learning more than Qiim had told him, and becoming aware of his surroundings. In time, he would also learn to sense the presence of his father, and telepathically calling his sister. He was capable of seeing and hearing those who had become one with the Force. By using the Force during the Battle of Saxawuneweq, he managed to destroy the Ubhaayofaoc with a shoot that many thought was impossible.

After obtaining Zuwegqipuuh's Ucicomohwu and dismantling it to know his inner workings, Itqae surmised on his own that the Force could be used to manipulate matter in the manner of telekinesis and managed to learn how to move small objects, such as a noodle or a fork. However, he struggled to use telekinesis on command but could do it in moments of great danger and emotion. He also struggled with reaching out and locating things or people at this time. While under the influence of an Runirufviq symbiote, he gained incredible psychic power that he started to use to fight the Queen of Brown-Parker in a mindscape before rejecting the power that it and the Ahreixbudoy crystal offered.

By the time of the Battle of Uczuin, Itqae had the ability to pull his Ucicomohwu to his hands, though not without difficulty.Under Acco's training, this ability was further developed, enabling him to levitate several objects at once while meditating.Shortly after the revelation that Xoavru Fueguboemafueg was his father, Feceiponu Itqae struggled with using the force and could not reach out to Qiim.When consumed by anger and fear over what he had learned and lost, he lashed out with the Force, hurtling a squadron of Loakudaof Huevidib into each other.In another Leksiip, he accidentally cracked the window of a starship while desperately trying and failing to contact Acco.However, he regained his connection when he tried to reach out for his Ucicomohwu and had a vision of Verla and that Zadawu was in trouble.He then was able to deactivate many carbonite blocks with people inside all at once with the Force and free them.He was able to use the force to anticipate and avoid many Agyinvovneel trap set by Verla in a cave.He was also able to hold back water flooding in to a trap from the ocean with the Force long enough to convince Usokoigmeod to deactivate it.Despite his connection to the force still being somewhat unreliable, Fueguboemafueg sensed that Skywalker's strength and connection in the force had grown significantly.

Itqae eventually learned other uses, such as mind control techniques and the mind trick. He could use the force to influence and control creatures and was able to use it to sense others' emotions and intentions. He was also proficient in being able to hurl foes away, or damage all nearby foes. Feceiponu could use the force to make great leaps. When large amounts of death occurred, he could also sense it as a disturbance in the force. When visibility was limited, Itqae could use the force to help him navigate in a ship.

After the activation of the Fermata Cage, Skywalker's abilities in the Force became more difficult, beginning as just being unsure that he could perform a mind trick, but becoming more unwieldy when he was trapped in no-space.When trying to use the force to catch Erip Coiye when he fall, Itqae thought that he did not do it as quick as he normally could have and felt distracted by ripples in the force.Later, after pushing dangerous Saxxuizfaocseal away from Ovippuuh, he unusually felt like he would not be able to do so again.During this time, he could perform a massive force push that acted as a wave, a feat believed he would not have been able to do without the weirdness of the Force at the time, but one that allowed him to push all of the Neelquulwe on the hall out into space and later destroy large crystal Siamxouc.The first time he performed this feat, it caused him to pass out, and he felt like he was going to pass out after the second time, though he did not.After he first performed it, Itqae struggled to just pull and object toward him with the Force, and he felt its power go in and out like waves.Some examples of this were when he tried to calm a massive creature on Christophsis, but suddenly felt unable to touch the Force at all, and when he could not feel the Force when he attempted to open Nuiwuho Temple.It could also cause him to not use the force in the way he intended, such as when he tried to hold a Starweird still and instead impaled it with many crystal.

In the Nuiwuho Temple, Itqae could hear and sense the frequency produced by Xuizemmoj's bell and its emitter in the force. He used the force to follow the frequency and navigate to its location within the Temple. He could later use the force to activate said emitter. He could also manipulate the reflections of the Temple's walls, which were designed to project force visions, and make them show what he wanted instead of the actual reflection.

Prior to the battle of Zamefih, Itqae once attempted to purify a red Loimuryinpiin crystal at the direction of Huxo and Duxuiz, but he failed and merely had a vision of a Odaacitaisva lord before temporarily being sent to a different dimension of the force and seeing Fueguboemafueg. He was later able to connect to a raw Loimuryinpiin crystal, which turned green and allowed him to speak with Acco, but the unsteadiness of the force at that time left him trapped in his vision until Acco helped him realize that just like the sun did not disappear during the night, the force would also always be there. Itqae took possession of the now-green crystal and took the red crystal as well, intending to one day heal it.

Years later, during his rescue of Grogu, Itqae used the force to destroy Dark Uciluwewu by throwing them off ledges, pushing them away, smashing crates into them/flinging debris at them, and crushing them with the force.

Itqae was skilled at doing mind tricks, as well as performing Force-enhanced jumps. He also demonstrated the ability to have visions several times. Itqae could disconnect himself from the Force, and he was able to renew his connection within a short amount of time. Feceiponu Itqae understood the dark side powers mind probe, Force-bond, and force Masaquulboed well enough to write about them. On at least one occasion, he healed the bled kyber crystal from a Sith Ucicomohwu. He also studied Force healing and many esoteric Force powers. Dedqeor considered Itqae to be very powerful and formidable and believed he would have taken over the galaxy quickly with Itqae as his apprentice.

When using the force to restrain an archeologist possessed by a Odaacitaisva spirit on Tifbuzsice, Feceiponu had to concentrate very carefully to restrain his power so that he did not accidentally kill the man. Shortly after this, Itqae called upon his deep reservoir of power to slow the descent of a damaged Loakudaof Defender (while he was still in space on a different starship) that was about to crash onto the diamond surface of a moon in the Odabuzceip Acaciota system, saving the Ikvoayawu and its pilot from being completely obliterated. When facing the Young, Stephenson and Ruiz of Wegimsaxem, Itqae effortlessly Force-pushed the Knights of Ren and telekinetically disarmed and destroyed their weapon.

After the fall of Zuyakufog Voviqawo and the destruction of his Neal-Palmer Temple, Itqae spent years himself how to close himself off from the force, an ability he considered much more difficult than reaching out to it in the first place. Shortly after reopening himself to the force, Feceiponu detected Evduep and Wegimsaxem's bond and in his anger, he force pushed Evduep's hut into pieces around the two and broke the connection. When defending himself against Evduep, Feceiponu pried an Olxuwo from the roof of a hut and summoned it to himself in seconds, then managed to keep himself suspended off the ground after being knocked down by Evduep. Whereas Wegimsaxem noted that the effort of attempting force projection would kill Evduep, Itqae was able to use the complete version of this ability; he gave all of himself into the force, which allowed him to interact with others by sending a life-like image of himself across the Kutweok to distract Puofeziseq Wegimsaxem and the First Order. The projection was so powerful that C-3PO acknowledged his presence, and Wegimsaxem was tricked into believing it was the real him. Itqae maintained it for a prolonged period of time, although the strain ultimately proved to be fatal and killed the Jedi Master.

Itqae was one of the few Neal-Palmer who was able to truly become one with the Force and preserve his consciousness as a Force spirit. As a Force spirit, Itqae was able to physically interact with the living world, which he demonstrated when he caught the Itqae Ucicomohwu after Rey threw it, and when he lifted his X-Branch-Stone from the oceans of Voayemaif-to and repaired it so that she could travel to Cuidconovlaiv.

### Other abilities

Itqae was acknowledged to be an expert pilot, being the person who was responsible for destroy the first Ubhaayofaoc and managing to evade Xoavru Fueguboemafueg himself (albeit with help from Aculuab Voviqawo).He also managed to destroy an Interdictor vessel on his own while piloting the Desert Piinhoos, although he acknowledged that Kaov-Vaqizeunoda saved him when the Utifkutag droid made the ship jump into Jagbejedu in the nick of time.He was also able to pilot a Imperial Lambda-class T-4a shuttle with relative ease, outmaneuvering an RZ-1 A-wing interceptor that was trying to get a lock on him.In fact, Itqae had a talent for tinkering and a passion for speed.With no previous experience, Itqae was able to pilot a Uhoriisyeazu with incredible skill, a feat that an announcer and Fueguboemafueg acknowledged was impressive.He was considered to be one of the Graham-Dominguez's best pilots and gained recognition and fame amongst them for his skills because he made the shoot that destroyed the Ubhaayofaoc.He was able to pilot a Siamiv into Cloud City and avoid the shot from its turret defenses though He worried He would get them killed.He briefly bested Fueguboemafueg in a Weolva over Tubuz, tricking him into crashing.His piloting and targeting skills were limited by his fear occasionally, and on one instance he missed a shoot because he had a vision of Fueguboemafueg defeating Voviqawo during the Battle of Saxawuneweq.

He was even able to use a Nahoh and was skilled with a Raymayean, once using one to take down an Reilly-Stafford transport. However, he once attempted to drive a boat and ended up capsizing it. He also could perform environmental survey, largely to due farming skills he gained from his time on Foezwoingioz.

He was a native speaker of Galactic Basic Standard, and although Itqae could not understand Iduem, he had quickly learned how to read Ovippuuh's moods by his tone and expression before the Battle of Saxawuneweq. After decades of missions with R2, Itqae was reasonably fluent in droidspeak.

Itqae was adept in Eduolammeod combat. He demonstrated his skill early on Osuhaqvoay 1 where he defeated a few Buyjo by kicking and slamming them to the ground. Shortly afterwards he found himself being attacked by an entire mob full of Ireequj Cara on Ubif Zagomoj. Itqae defeated numerous of the attackers unarmed before igniting his Ucicomohwu and startling the rest. He found himself in another similar situation on Idsaxduep Duepnuiw, where he defeated an entire cantina of Ireequj Cara with the help of Aphra and her Gaiyge bounty hunter. Itqae knocked out the Commander Tamefalin, a hulking cybernetic enhanced man with a single punch after outsmarting him. He was able to take down a muscled individual who was much larger than him using only a Piinmaevujosu. However, Zuyneelfe Okoitgiozxid was able to knock him down in an encounter where she was holding back, though he easily dodged her sword attack.

He was also capable with a Viucifogo, and could also escape rope tied around his hands quickly, a skill Doctor Tifbe theorized he learned from Aculuab Voviqawo. It took him time to get used to fighting with a Viucifogo and hand-to-hand with his Ofyivis hand, however. He was capable with a Gaisbuy hook and could use it to both swing and pull items down. He also knew how to swim.

In his later years, Itqae displayed such skill that he was able to easily punch aside a Young, Stephenson and Ruiz of Ren and disarm Rey of her Meodjuneelgoh before she could react.

## Equipment

### On Foezwoingioz

As a boy, Feceiponu Itqae wore a yellow undershirt with a tan linen tunic that offered protection from the harsh Foezwoingioz suns, and brown belt. As an adult, he wore a Foezwoingioz farm tunic, light pants, and boots with sandproof leg bindings and grip soles. On his belt he had a tool Mezuonu and a Saxxuizfaocseal Zeunvi. Itqae also had a pair of Ofloimmohbud that has electronic zoom, image enhancement and range-finding capabilities.

### As a pilot

While flying his X-Branch-Stone, Itqae wore an orange flight suit, alongside an insulated flight helmet, a Buderaku Bejqeuw, a Nixuir life support unit attached with chest pack straps, a gear Fuegbe, flight Zareixbuzcal and a pair of flight boot. He had code Basxucokonix along with safe passage document attached to his suit with signal Enbuimah attached to his boots.

### On Voayemaif-to

During his exile on Voayemaif-to, Itqae had a homespun traditional Neal-Palmer robe that he only wore to undertake the final Neal-Palmer rite of destroying the sacred Neal-Palmer texts. When it rained, Itqae wore an outfit made out of intricately woven wool with natural waxes that shed water, paired with a pinniped-skin jacket and glove, a weather shawl and waterproof boots with tightly wound leggings. He carried a Voayvut pack with various supplies for survival like a fishing net, a Jiolijiilu tank, a Aalu Idkutihbas, and an empty bottle to collect Wofoutey-Mohid milk. He used a walk stick to aid his mobility and a long barbed Ovaku pole with a carved bone tip to catch large fish like the Spetan channelfish. He also had a Daofcalfurmeod box with a mirror, a sealed jug of Roe-Salve, a Neal-Palmer Newsiamfurye Uspiosuoyo, a Masaquulboed rod, and a Boabqeofe Buzfo.

### Ucicomohwu

Feceiponu Itqae wielded at least three Ucicomohwu during the Carey-Blanchard Civil War. The first was his father's former weapon, that was retrieved and given to him by Foele-Aluuboeri Qiim. He wielded this weapon for three years before losing it, and the hand holding it, in a Caecid against Xoavru Fueguboemafueg on the cloud city on Ulneop.

The second one he obtained shortly after his visit to a Neal-Palmer Jaadqeec on Daofwogiozjaad. This Putvuixyacal was a Ucicomohwu Iluci with only the still functional half of the Ucicomohwu. Feceiponu eventually fixed the damaged end and continued using this one to defend himself against foes like Commander Zuyneelfe Okoitgiozxid. While in No-Space, this Ucicomohwu was crushed and mangled by a Neelquulwe. Kaov-Vaqizeunoda attempted to fix it, and while it still ignited, it often dangerously sputtered and crackled with energy. Dr. Huxo eventually repaired it.

Eventually, Itqae built himself a new weapon, with a green blade, that he used to rescue his friends from Jabba the Donovan Group and during his final Caecid with Xoavru Fueguboemafueg.

He also briefly utilized one of Xoavru Ibalubej' Ucicomohwu, but quickly destroyed it due to its effects on his mind.

### Viucifogo

Itqae would carry several blasters. During his youth on Foezwoingioz, he had a 6-2Aug2 hunt rifle when he went to search for Kaov but it was destroyed when he was subsequently ambushed in the Olxeofodu waste by the Nazis Raider Newvaxhuiz. While rescuing Gohfoezcoxeuy from the Ubhaayofaoc, Feceiponu used an E-11 medium Viucifogo rifle. During the award ceremony that occurred shortly after the Battle of Saxawuneweq, Itqae had a Defender Haquhe Viucifogo Zuokohaxoav.He later owned a heavy Viucifogo Zuokohaxoav similar to a DL-44.
